FILE: src\losses\delay_ipw.py

================================================================================

"""Delay-weighted BCE: observed zeros are weighted by how old they are."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import Tensor

from src.losses import PNU_P, PNU_U, prepare_inputs, zero_loss


def lam_from_median_delay(median_days: float) -> float:
    """Rate ``lambda`` (1/day) of an exponential delay with the given median: ``ln 2 / median``.

    Only an approximation for Weibull delays (KHÔNG CHẮC, CẦN VERIFY): choose ``lam`` by a sweep.
    """
    if median_days <= 0:
        raise ValueError("median_days must be > 0.")
    return math.log(2.0) / median_days


class DelayIPWLoss(nn.Module):
    """Weighted BCE with ``w = 1 - exp(-lam * age_days)`` on observed zeros; P rows weigh 1.

    ``w(age)`` is the probability that a fraud of that age has already been reported when the delay is
    exponential with rate ``lam``: a fresh zero (age ~ 0) is almost surely "not reported yet" and barely
    counts; an old zero counts fully. KHÔNG CHẮC, CẦN VERIFY: this is the heuristic of the brief, NOT a
    proven unbiased estimator.

    Ambiguity in the brief ("BCE on P/N, weight N by age"), resolved by ``negative_scope``:
      * ``"all_zeros"`` (default): every observed zero (N and U) is weighted by age. Rows in N are all
        older than the maturity window, so their weight is ~1 and the age effect lives in U.
      * ``"mature_only"``: literal reading, U is masked and only N is weighted. Then the loss is
        nearly identical to ``MaturityMaskLoss`` (N weights are close to 1). Kept as an ablation.

    Args:
        lam: rate in 1/day (see ``lam_from_median_delay``).
        normalize: ``"weights"`` divides by the sum of weights (scale comparable to BCE);
            ``"count"`` divides by the number of rows in scope.
        min_weight: floor of the zero-row weights (0 -> a row at age 0 has no gradient).
        negative_scope: ``"all_zeros"`` or ``"mature_only"``.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        lam: float,
        normalize: str = "weights",
        min_weight: float = 0.0,
        negative_scope: str = "all_zeros",
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if lam <= 0:
            raise ValueError("lam must be > 0.")
        if normalize not in ("weights", "count"):
            raise ValueError("normalize must be 'weights' or 'count'.")
        if not 0.0 <= min_weight <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("min_weight must be in [0, 1].")
        if negative_scope not in ("all_zeros", "mature_only"):
            raise ValueError("negative_scope must be 'all_zeros' or 'mature_only'.")
        self.lam = float(lam)
        self.normalize = normalize
        self.min_weight = float(min_weight)
        self.negative_scope = negative_scope

    def weights(self, pnu: Tensor, age_days: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        """Per-row weight before masking by ``in_train_view``."""
        age = age_days.clamp(min=0.0)
        w_zero = (1.0 - torch.exp(-self.lam * age)).clamp(min=self.min_weight)
        w = torch.where(pnu == PNU_P, torch.ones_like(w_zero), w_zero)
        if self.negative_scope == "mature_only":
            w = torch.where(pnu == PNU_U, torch.zeros_like(w), w)
        return w

    def forward(
        self,
        preds: Tensor,
        y_obs: Tensor,
        pnu_status: Tensor,
        *,
        age_days: Tensor | None = None,
        in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> Tensor:
        if age_days is None:
            raise ValueError("DelayIPWLoss needs age_days (see compute_age_days).")
        preds, y, pnu, view = prepare_inputs(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, in_train_view)
        age = age_days.reshape(-1).to(preds.dtype)
        if age.numel() != preds.numel():
            raise ValueError("age_days must have the same length as preds.")
        scope = view & (pnu != PNU_U) if self.negative_scope == "mature_only" else view
        if not bool(scope.any()):
            return zero_loss(preds)

        w = self.weights(pnu, age) * scope.to(preds.dtype)  # rows out of scope weigh 0
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(preds, y, reduction="none")
        denom = w.sum() if self.normalize == "weights" else scope.sum().to(preds.dtype)
        return (w * bce).sum() / denom.clamp_min(1e-8)


def delay_ipw_loss(
    preds: Tensor,
    y_obs: Tensor,
    pnu_status: Tensor,
    *,
    age_days: Tensor,
    lam: float,
    in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
    **kwargs,
) -> Tensor:
    """Functional form of :class:`DelayIPWLoss` (extra kwargs go to the constructor)."""
    return DelayIPWLoss(lam, **kwargs)(
        preds, y_obs, pnu_status, age_days=age_days, in_train_view=in_train_view
    )

================================================================================

FILE: src\losses\maturity_mask.py

================================================================================

"""Maturity masking: BCE on P and N only; U contributes 0, except amount-aware TRUSTED rows.

Window + Amount-Aware masking
-----------------------------
Rule: mature negative  <=>  age >= W   OR   (amount >= p95(TRAIN amounts)  AND  age >= amount_min_age_days).
N rows already encode ``age >= W``; the second branch promotes U rows to "trusted negative".
Business rationale: a large transaction (amount >= p95) enters MANUAL REVIEW immediately (real time). If the review
does not flag it and no complaint arrives, it is treated as mature even before W days have passed.
ASSUMPTION (KHÔNG CHẮC): there is no ``is_manual_review_cleared`` column, so it is assumed True for every large
transaction; pass ``manual_review_cleared`` to ``trusted_negative_mask`` to override it when real data exist.

IMPROVEMENT OVER THE ORIGINAL FORMULA (record it in Threats to Validity): the literal rule would also label a
brand-new, large, unreported transaction as negative. A trusted row must ALSO satisfy
``age >= amount_min_age_days`` (the trainer defaults it to W/2; 0 reproduces the literal rule). W/2 is a design
choice, not an estimate. Trusted rows can still hide frauds: the trainer logs ``diag_true_fraud_rate_in_trusted_U``
(reads y_true for DIAGNOSTICS only). The p95 is computed by the trainer on TRAIN amounts only.

Only ``y_obs``, ``pnu``, ``age_days`` and ``amount`` are read; ``y_true`` is never an input.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import Tensor

from src.losses import PNU_U, prepare_inputs, zero_loss


def trusted_negative_mask(
    pnu_status: Tensor,
    amount: Tensor,
    age_days: Tensor | None,
    amount_threshold: float,
    amount_min_age_days: float = 0.0,
    manual_review_cleared: Tensor | None = None,
) -> Tensor:
    """Bool mask of U rows that amount-aware masking promotes to "mature negative".

    ``U  &  amount >= amount_threshold  &  age >= amount_min_age_days  [&  manual_review_cleared]``.
    Rows outside the train view must be removed by the caller (``& in_train_view``).
    """
    pnu = pnu_status.reshape(-1)
    amt = amount.reshape(-1)
    if amt.numel() != pnu.numel():
        raise ValueError("amount must have the same length as pnu_status.")
    mask = (pnu == PNU_U) & (amt >= amount_threshold)
    if amount_min_age_days > 0:
        if age_days is None:
            raise ValueError("age_days is required when amount_min_age_days > 0.")
        age = age_days.reshape(-1)
        if age.numel() != pnu.numel():
            raise ValueError("age_days must have the same length as pnu_status.")
        mask = mask & (age.to(amt.dtype) >= amount_min_age_days)
    if manual_review_cleared is not None:
        mask = mask & manual_review_cleared.reshape(-1).bool()
    return mask


class MaturityMaskLoss(nn.Module):
    """Mean BCE over the rows kept by the mask.

    Default mask = rows with status P or N. With ``amount_threshold`` set (the trainer passes the p95 of the TRAIN
    amounts) trusted U rows are also kept, with target ``y_obs = 0``. ``mature_only_window_days=W`` is an ablation
    that also drops every row younger than W (needs ``age_days``).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        mature_only_window_days: float | None = None,
        amount_threshold: float | None = None,
        amount_min_age_days: float = 0.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if mature_only_window_days is not None and mature_only_window_days <= 0:
            raise ValueError("mature_only_window_days must be > 0.")
        if amount_threshold is not None and amount_threshold <= 0:
            raise ValueError("amount_threshold must be > 0.")
        if amount_min_age_days < 0:
            raise ValueError("amount_min_age_days must be >= 0.")
        self.mature_only_window_days = mature_only_window_days
        self.amount_threshold = None if amount_threshold is None else float(amount_threshold)
        self.amount_min_age_days = float(amount_min_age_days)

    @property
    def needs_amount(self) -> bool:
        """The trainer hands ``amount`` only to losses that declare they need it."""
        return self.amount_threshold is not None

    def forward(
        self,
        preds: Tensor,
        y_obs: Tensor,
        pnu_status: Tensor,
        *,
        age_days: Tensor | None = None,
        in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
        amount: Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> Tensor:
        preds, y, pnu, view = prepare_inputs(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, in_train_view)
        keep = pnu != PNU_U
        if self.amount_threshold is not None:
            if amount is None:
                raise ValueError("amount is required when amount_threshold is set.")
            keep = keep | trusted_negative_mask(
                pnu, amount.to(preds.device), age_days, self.amount_threshold, self.amount_min_age_days
            )
        mask = view & keep
        if self.mature_only_window_days is not None:
            if age_days is None:
                raise ValueError("age_days is required when mature_only_window_days is set.")
            mask = mask & (age_days.reshape(-1).to(preds.dtype) >= self.mature_only_window_days)
        if not bool(mask.any()):
            return zero_loss(preds)
        return F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(preds[mask], y[mask])


MaturityMaskedBCELoss = MaturityMaskLoss  # name used in the project brief


def maturity_mask_loss(
    preds: Tensor,
    y_obs: Tensor,
    pnu_status: Tensor,
    *,
    age_days: Tensor | None = None,
    in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
    amount: Tensor | None = None,
    mature_only_window_days: float | None = None,
    amount_threshold: float | None = None,
    amount_min_age_days: float = 0.0,
) -> Tensor:
    """Functional form of :class:`MaturityMaskLoss`."""
    return MaturityMaskLoss(mature_only_window_days, amount_threshold, amount_min_age_days)(
        preds, y_obs, pnu_status, age_days=age_days, in_train_view=in_train_view, amount=amount
    )


__all__ = ["trusted_negative_mask", "MaturityMaskLoss", "MaturityMaskedBCELoss", "maturity_mask_loss"]

================================================================================

FILE: src\losses\naive_bce.py

================================================================================

"""Baseline A0: plain BCE where every unlabeled row counts as a negative."""
from __future__ import annotations

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import Tensor

from src.losses import prepare_inputs, zero_loss


class NaiveBCELoss(nn.Module):
    """``BCEWithLogits`` over all train-view rows with ``y_obs`` as target.

    Immature rows (status U, ``y_obs = 0``) are treated as legitimate: this is the bias the other
    three losses try to remove. ``pnu_status`` and ``age_days`` are accepted only to share one
    signature. Gradient on a U row is ``sigmoid(f) / n``, never 0.

    Oracle runs reuse this class: pass ``y_true`` as ``y_obs`` (trainer switch, round 6).
    """

    def forward(
        self,
        preds: Tensor,
        y_obs: Tensor,
        pnu_status: Tensor,
        *,
        age_days: Tensor | None = None,
        in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> Tensor:
        preds, y, _, view = prepare_inputs(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, in_train_view)
        if not bool(view.any()):
            return zero_loss(preds)
        return F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(preds[view], y[view])


def naive_bce_loss(
    preds: Tensor,
    y_obs: Tensor,
    pnu_status: Tensor,
    *,
    age_days: Tensor | None = None,
    in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
) -> Tensor:
    """Functional form of :class:`NaiveBCELoss`."""
    return NaiveBCELoss()(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, age_days=age_days, in_train_view=in_train_view)

================================================================================

FILE: src\losses\tw_nndf.py

================================================================================

"""TW-nnDF = Maturity-Masked BCE (mature region) + Cost-Sensitive nnPU (immature region).

Two classes
-----------
``TimeWindowNNDFLoss`` (variant ``pu_only``): the V1 / V1.5 PU risk of P versus U only; N rows are NOT used.
``HybridTWnnDFLoss`` (variant ``hybrid``, the specification of the project brief)::

    loss = BCE(P=1, N=0, trusted-U=0)  +  lambda_pu * PU_risk(P vs U_remaining)

* BCE region ("mature"): P rows, N rows, and (amount-aware masking) U rows with ``amount >= p95_train`` and
  ``age >= amount_min_age_days`` (``maturity_mask.trusted_negative_mask``). Trusted rows leave U.
* PU region: P versus the remaining U. P appears in BOTH terms (design choice, KHÔNG CHẮC).
* Cost-sensitive: ``amount_weighted=True`` multiplies every P / U row's loss by ``min(amount / amount_ref, max_weight)``
  inside the same means, with the same prior. The identity behind it needs the hidden positives in U to share P's
  amount distribution (SCAR); the amount-dependent delay of the benchmark violates it (threat to validity).
* Prior: a fixed ``prior`` (scalar from the trainer), or, if ``sliding_prior`` is given, the batch prior
  ``mean(sliding_prior.query(age_days[remaining U rows]))``: a per-batch scalar computed from TRAIN-only tables.
* Calibration: the PU term sees ``logits / T`` with ``T`` from ``MatureTemperatureTracker`` (fitted on the
  maturity-masked TRAIN rows with ``y_obs``, never on validation). The BCE term sees the raw logits.
* Only ``y_obs``, ``pnu``, ``age_days`` and ``amount`` are read; ``y_true`` is never an input.

PU modes: ``clamp`` = ``pos + max(0, neg_raw)``; ``nnpu`` = Kiryo et al. 2017 style: ``neg_raw >= -beta`` ->
``pos + neg_raw``, else ``-gamma * neg_raw`` (Algorithm 1 as recalled, KHÔNG CHẮC, CẦN VERIFY).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol, Sequence

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import Tensor

from src.graph.builder import PNU_CODES
from src.losses import PNU_N, PNU_P, PNU_U, prepare_inputs, zero_loss
from src.losses.maturity_mask import trusted_negative_mask

NNDFMode = Literal["clamp", "nnpu"]
_PRIOR_EPS = 1e-4


class AgePrior(Protocol):
    """Anything with ``query(age_days) -> pi`` (e.g. ``calibration.SlidingWindowPrior``)."""

    def query(self, age_days: Tensor) -> Tensor: ...


def estimate_class_prior(
    y_obs: Tensor,
    age_days: Tensor,
    window_days: float,
    in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
    eps: float = 1e-4,
) -> float:
    """Positive prior = fraction of observed positives among MATURE train-view rows (observed labels only)."""
    y = y_obs.reshape(-1).float()
    age = age_days.reshape(-1)
    view = torch.ones_like(y, dtype=torch.bool) if in_train_view is None else in_train_view.reshape(-1).bool()
    mature = view & (age >= window_days)
    if int(mature.sum()) == 0:
        raise ValueError("No mature rows: cannot estimate the class prior.")
    return float(min(max(float(y[mature].mean()), eps), 1.0 - eps))


class TimeWindowNNDFLoss(nn.Module):
    """PU risk (logistic loss) of P versus U; see the module docstring.

    Args:
        prior: positive prior ``pi`` in (0, 1).
        unlabeled_statuses: statuses forming U, a non-empty subset of ``("U", "N")``.
        mode: ``"clamp"`` or ``"nnpu"``.
        beta / gamma: ``nnpu`` only; tolerance >= 0 and ascent discount in (0, 1].
        amount_weighted / amount_ref / max_weight: cost-sensitive weights ``min(amount / amount_ref, max_weight)``.
    After every ``forward`` the detached components are in ``self.last_risks`` (``pos_risk``, ``neg_risk_raw``,
    ``neg_risk``, ``clamped``, ``ascent``). ``clamped`` = (neg_raw < 0) in both modes.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        prior: float,
        unlabeled_statuses: Sequence[str] = ("U",),
        mode: NNDFMode = "clamp",
        beta: float = 0.0,
        gamma: float = 1.0,
        amount_weighted: bool = False,
        amount_ref: float | None = None,
        max_weight: float = 10.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if not 0.0 < prior < 1.0:
            raise ValueError("prior must be in (0, 1).")
        statuses = tuple(unlabeled_statuses)
        if not statuses or any(s not in ("U", "N") for s in statuses):
            raise ValueError("unlabeled_statuses must be a non-empty subset of ('U', 'N').")
        if mode not in ("clamp", "nnpu"):
            raise ValueError("mode must be 'clamp' or 'nnpu'.")
        if beta < 0.0:
            raise ValueError("beta must be >= 0.")
        if not 0.0 < gamma <= 1.0:
            raise ValueError("gamma must be in (0, 1].")
        if amount_weighted and (amount_ref is None or amount_ref <= 0):
            raise ValueError("amount_weighted=True needs amount_ref > 0.")
        if max_weight <= 0:
            raise ValueError("max_weight must be > 0.")
        self.prior = float(prior)
        self.unlabeled_statuses = statuses
        self.mode = mode
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self.gamma = float(gamma)
        self.amount_weighted = bool(amount_weighted)
        self.amount_ref = None if amount_ref is None else float(amount_ref)
        self.max_weight = float(max_weight)
        self.register_buffer(
            "_unlabeled_codes", torch.tensor([PNU_CODES[s] for s in statuses], dtype=torch.long), persistent=False
        )
        self.last_risks: dict[str, Tensor] | None = None

    @property
    def needs_amount(self) -> bool:
        """The trainer hands ``amount`` only to losses that declare they need it."""
        return self.amount_weighted

    def _row_weights(self, amount: Tensor | None, n: int, like: Tensor) -> Tensor:
        if amount is None:
            raise ValueError("amount_weighted=True needs the `amount` argument.")
        a = amount.reshape(-1).to(device=like.device, dtype=like.dtype)
        if a.numel() != n:
            raise ValueError("amount must have the same length as preds.")
        return (a / self.amount_ref).clamp(min=0.0, max=self.max_weight)

    def risks(
        self,
        preds: Tensor,
        y_obs: Tensor,
        pnu_status: Tensor,
        in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
        amount: Tensor | None = None,
        prior: Tensor | float | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Tensor] | None:
        """Risk components, or ``None`` if P or U is empty. ``prior`` overrides ``self.prior`` for this call."""
        preds, _, pnu, view = prepare_inputs(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, in_train_view)
        pos = view & (pnu == PNU_P)
        unl = view & torch.isin(pnu, self._unlabeled_codes.to(pnu.device))
        if not bool(pos.any()) or not bool(unl.any()):
            return None
        if self.amount_weighted:
            w = self._row_weights(amount, preds.numel(), preds)
            wp, wu = w[pos], w[unl]
        else:
            wp = wu = torch.ones((), dtype=preds.dtype, device=preds.device)
        pi = self.prior if prior is None else prior
        pos_as_pos = (wp * F.softplus(-preds[pos])).mean()  # l(f, +1) on P
        pos_as_neg = (wp * F.softplus(preds[pos])).mean()  # l(f, -1) on P
        unl_as_neg = (wu * F.softplus(preds[unl])).mean()  # l(f, -1) on U
        neg_raw = unl_as_neg - pi * pos_as_neg  # estimates (1 - pi) * E_N[l(f, -1)], should be >= 0
        return {
            "pos_risk": pi * pos_as_pos,
            "neg_risk_raw": neg_raw,
            "neg_risk": torch.clamp(neg_raw, min=0.0),  # logging / clamp mode only
            "clamped": (neg_raw < 0).to(preds.dtype),
        }

    def _pu_loss(self, parts: dict[str, Tensor]) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:
        """``(PU loss, ascent flag)`` from the risk components."""
        raw = parts["neg_risk_raw"]
        if self.mode == "clamp":
            return parts["pos_risk"] + parts["neg_risk"], torch.zeros_like(raw)
        use_ascent = raw < -self.beta
        loss = torch.where(use_ascent, -self.gamma * raw, parts["pos_risk"] + raw)
        return loss, use_ascent.to(raw.dtype)

    def forward(
        self,
        preds: Tensor,
        y_obs: Tensor,
        pnu_status: Tensor,
        *,
        age_days: Tensor | None = None,
        in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
        amount: Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> Tensor:
        parts = self.risks(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, in_train_view, amount)
        if parts is None:
            self.last_risks = None
            return zero_loss(preds.reshape(-1))
        loss, ascent = self._pu_loss(parts)
        self.last_risks = {k: v.detach() for k, v in parts.items()}
        self.last_risks["ascent"] = ascent.detach()
        return loss


class HybridTWnnDFLoss(TimeWindowNNDFLoss):
    """``bce + lambda_pu * pu`` as described in the module docstring.

    Extra args: ``lambda_pu`` (>= 0), ``amount_threshold`` (p95 of the TRAIN amounts from the trainer; ``None``
    disables amount-aware masking), ``amount_min_age_days``, ``calibrator`` (``MatureTemperatureTracker`` or
    ``None``), ``sliding_prior`` (object with ``query(age_days)``, e.g. ``SlidingWindowPrior``; ``None`` -> the
    fixed scalar ``prior``).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        prior: float,
        unlabeled_statuses: Sequence[str] = ("U",),
        mode: NNDFMode = "nnpu",
        beta: float = 0.0,
        gamma: float = 1.0,
        amount_weighted: bool = False,
        amount_ref: float | None = None,
        max_weight: float = 10.0,
        *,
        lambda_pu: float = 1.0,
        amount_threshold: float | None = None,
        amount_min_age_days: float = 0.0,
        calibrator: Any | None = None,
        sliding_prior: AgePrior | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if tuple(unlabeled_statuses) != ("U",):
            raise ValueError("hybrid variant: unlabeled_statuses must be ('U',) (N feeds the BCE term).")
        super().__init__(prior, unlabeled_statuses, mode, beta, gamma, amount_weighted, amount_ref, max_weight)
        if lambda_pu < 0:
            raise ValueError("lambda_pu must be >= 0.")
        if amount_threshold is not None and amount_threshold <= 0:
            raise ValueError("amount_threshold must be > 0.")
        if amount_min_age_days < 0:
            raise ValueError("amount_min_age_days must be >= 0.")
        self.lambda_pu = float(lambda_pu)
        self.amount_threshold = None if amount_threshold is None else float(amount_threshold)
        self.amount_min_age_days = float(amount_min_age_days)
        self.calibrator = calibrator
        self.sliding_prior = sliding_prior

    @property
    def needs_amount(self) -> bool:
        return self.amount_weighted or self.amount_threshold is not None

    def end_epoch(self) -> float | None:
        """Called by the trainer after each training epoch: refit ``T`` on the TRAIN rows collected so far."""
        return None if self.calibrator is None else self.calibrator.refit()

    def forward(
        self,
        preds: Tensor,
        y_obs: Tensor,
        pnu_status: Tensor,
        *,
        age_days: Tensor | None = None,
        in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
        amount: Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> Tensor:
        preds, _, pnu, view = prepare_inputs(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, in_train_view)
        amt = None if amount is None else amount.reshape(-1).to(preds.device)
        trusted = torch.zeros_like(view)
        if self.amount_threshold is not None:
            if amt is None:
                raise ValueError("amount_threshold is set: `amount` is required.")
            trusted = view & trusted_negative_mask(
                pnu, amt, age_days, self.amount_threshold, self.amount_min_age_days
            )

        # ---- BCE on the mature region: P=1, N=0, trusted U=0 ------------------------------------------------
        is_pos = view & (pnu == PNU_P)
        keep = is_pos | (view & (pnu == PNU_N)) | trusted
        has_keep = bool(keep.any())
        bce = (
            F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(preds[keep], is_pos[keep].to(preds.dtype))
            if has_keep else zero_loss(preds)
        )
        if self.calibrator is not None and has_keep and self.training and torch.is_grad_enabled():
            self.calibrator.collect(preds[keep].detach(), is_pos[keep])  # TRAIN mature rows only

        # ---- nnPU on P versus the remaining U, on calibrated logits -----------------------------------------
        z = preds if self.calibrator is None else preds / self.calibrator.temperature
        pnu_pu = torch.where(trusted, torch.full_like(pnu, PNU_N), pnu)  # trusted rows leave U
        pi: Tensor | None = None
        if self.sliding_prior is not None:
            if age_days is None:
                raise ValueError("sliding_prior is set: `age_days` is required.")
            u_rows = view & (pnu_pu == PNU_U)
            if bool(u_rows.any()):
                age_u = age_days.reshape(-1).to(preds.device)[u_rows]
                pi = self.sliding_prior.query(age_u).to(preds.dtype).mean().clamp(_PRIOR_EPS, 1.0 - _PRIOR_EPS)
        parts = self.risks(z, y_obs, pnu_pu, in_train_view, amt, prior=pi)
        zero = preds.detach().sum() * 0.0
        if parts is None:
            pu = zero
            risks = {k: zero for k in ("pos_risk", "neg_risk_raw", "neg_risk", "clamped", "ascent", "prior_used")}
        else:
            pu, ascent = self._pu_loss(parts)
            risks = {k: v.detach() for k, v in parts.items()}
            risks["ascent"] = ascent.detach()
            risks["prior_used"] = (preds.new_tensor(self.prior) if pi is None else pi).detach()
        risks["bce"] = bce.detach()
        self.last_risks = risks
        return bce + self.lambda_pu * pu


def tw_nndf_loss(
    preds: Tensor,
    y_obs: Tensor,
    pnu_status: Tensor,
    *,
    prior: float,
    age_days: Tensor | None = None,
    in_train_view: Tensor | None = None,
    amount: Tensor | None = None,
    unlabeled_statuses: Sequence[str] = ("U",),
    mode: NNDFMode = "clamp",
    beta: float = 0.0,
    gamma: float = 1.0,
    amount_weighted: bool = False,
    amount_ref: float | None = None,
    max_weight: float = 10.0,
) -> Tensor:
    """Functional form of :class:`TimeWindowNNDFLoss` (pu_only)."""
    return TimeWindowNNDFLoss(
        prior, unlabeled_statuses, mode, beta, gamma, amount_weighted, amount_ref, max_weight
    )(preds, y_obs, pnu_status, age_days=age_days, in_train_view=in_train_view, amount=amount)

================================================================================

FILE: src\models\classifier.py

================================================================================

"""FraudClassifier: backbone embeddings -> MLP head -> one LOGIT per seed transaction."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch import Tensor
from torch_geometric.data import HeteroData

from src.models.temporal_gnn import ModularTemporalGNN


def entity_vocab_sizes_from_meta(meta: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Embedding-table sizes from ``meta.json`` (``vocab_size_all_phases`` covers val/test ids)."""
    return {
        name: int(st["vocab_size_all_phases"])
        for name, st in meta["entity_stats"].items()
        if int(st["vocab_size_all_phases"]) > 0
    }


class FraudClassifier(nn.Module):
    """``backbone -> Linear -> ReLU -> Dropout -> Linear`` returning logits ``[batch_size]``.

    No sigmoid inside: every loss in ``src/losses`` expects logits. Use ``predict_proba`` for
    probabilities (call ``model.eval()`` yourself).
    """

    def __init__(self, backbone: ModularTemporalGNN, head_hidden_dim: int | None = None, dropout: float = 0.2) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.backbone = backbone
        hidden = head_hidden_dim or backbone.out_dim
        self.head = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(backbone.out_dim, hidden),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(hidden, 1),
        )

    def forward(self, batch: HeteroData) -> Tensor:
        return self.head(self.backbone(batch)).squeeze(-1)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def predict_proba(self, batch: HeteroData) -> Tensor:
        return torch.sigmoid(self.forward(batch))

    def num_parameters(self) -> int:
        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters() if p.requires_grad)

    @classmethod
    def from_config(
        cls,
        model_cfg: Mapping[str, Any],
        in_dim: int,
        entity_vocab_sizes: Mapping[str, int],
        conv_type: str | None = None,
    ) -> "FraudClassifier":
        """Build from the ``model:`` section of configs/base.yaml.

        ``conv_type`` (or ``model.backbone``): "sage" (main, default) or "gat" (secondary).
        Optional keys: max_history (20), gru_layers (1), use_temporal (True), gat_heads (4),
        head_hidden_dim (= hidden_dim).
        """
        backbone = ModularTemporalGNN(
            in_dim=in_dim,
            entity_vocab_sizes=entity_vocab_sizes,
            hidden_dim=int(model_cfg.get("hidden_dim", 128)),
            num_layers=int(model_cfg.get("num_layers", 2)),
            dropout=float(model_cfg.get("dropout", 0.2)),
            entity_embedding_dim=int(model_cfg.get("entity_embedding_dim", 32)),
            conv_type=conv_type or str(model_cfg.get("backbone", "sage")),
            gat_heads=int(model_cfg.get("gat_heads", 4)),
            max_history=int(model_cfg.get("max_history", 20)),
            gru_layers=int(model_cfg.get("gru_layers", 1)),
            use_temporal=bool(model_cfg.get("use_temporal", True)),
        )
        head_hidden = model_cfg.get("head_hidden_dim")
        return cls(
            backbone,
            head_hidden_dim=int(head_hidden) if head_hidden is not None else None,
            dropout=float(model_cfg.get("dropout", 0.2)),
        )


def build_classifier(
    model_cfg: Mapping[str, Any], meta: Mapping[str, Any], conv_type: str | None = None, extra_in_dim: int = 0
) -> FraudClassifier:
    """Convenience: ``in_dim`` and entity tables come from ``meta.json`` (written by prepare_data)."""
    return FraudClassifier.from_config(
        model_cfg,
        in_dim=int(meta["feature_dim"]) + int(extra_in_dim),  # +4 with LP features (ds.model_in_dim - ds.feature_dim)
        entity_vocab_sizes=entity_vocab_sizes_from_meta(meta),
        conv_type=conv_type,
    )

================================================================================

FILE: src\models\temporal_gnn.py

================================================================================

"""ModularTemporalGNN: spatial encoder (HeteroConv of SAGEConv or GATConv) + GRU temporal encoder.

Input: a mini-batch from ``src.graph.sampler.build_neighbor_loader`` (disjoint + temporal):
  * ``batch['transaction']``: ``x`` [N, F], ``time`` [N] int64 s, ``batch`` [N] subgraph id,
    ``batch_size``. The FIRST ``batch_size`` transaction nodes are the seeds.
  * entity nodes (``card`` / ``device``): ``node_id`` [K] (global id, identical in train/val/test).
  * edges ``(transaction, uses_<e>, <e>)`` and ``(<e>, rev_uses_<e>, transaction)``.
No label field (``y_obs``, ``y_true``, ``pnu``) is ever read here.

Output: seed embeddings ``[batch_size, hidden_dim]`` (the logit comes from ``FraudClassifier``).

Information flow with num_layers=2 and 2-hop sampling
  layer 1: entity <- history transactions (uses_*)   |  seed <- entity (rev_uses_*)
  layer 2: seed <- entity (now carrying history information)
A history transaction is reached only through an entity; its incoming edges would need a 3rd hop,
so its state stays equal to its input projection.

Backbones (``conv_type``)
  "sage": one ``SAGEConv`` per edge type (main backbone, the 72 benchmark runs).
  "gat" : one ``GATConv`` per edge type (secondary check). ``add_self_loops=False`` is required for
          bipartite edge types; ``hidden_dim`` must be divisible by ``gat_heads``.

Temporal encoder (DESIGN CHOICE of this project, not a reproduction of a paper; whether it helps is
KHÔNG CHẮC, CẦN VERIFY by the ablation ``use_temporal=False``)
  Each seed owns its own subgraph (disjoint batch). For every seed we take the other TRANSACTION nodes
  of its subgraph (its sampled history), sort them by time, keep the ``max_history`` most recent, and
  feed ``[projected features, log1p(days before the seed)]`` to a GRU: oldest ... most recent, then the
  seed itself. The last GRU state is fused with the seed's spatial embedding. The "sequence" is the set
  of transactions linked to the seed through shared entities, ordered by time; it is NOT a per-entity
  sequence.

Entity ids: ``nn.Embedding`` per entity type, sized by ``vocab_size_all_phases`` in meta.json. Ids that
never occur in the train period are never updated and keep a near-zero vector (std=0.01), which acts as
"unknown entity" at validation / test time.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Literal, Mapping

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import Tensor
from torch.nn.utils.rnn import pack_padded_sequence
from torch_geometric.data import HeteroData
from torch_geometric.nn import GATConv, HeteroConv, SAGEConv

from src.data.loader import SECONDS_PER_DAY
from src.graph.builder import TXN

ConvType = Literal["sage", "gat"]


class ModularTemporalGNN(nn.Module):
    """Spatial HeteroConv encoder + optional GRU temporal encoder.

    Args:
        in_dim: transaction feature dimension (meta['feature_dim'], 457 here).
        entity_vocab_sizes: ``{entity_type: number_of_ids}`` over ALL phases.
        hidden_dim: width of every node state and of the output embedding.
        num_layers: HeteroConv layers (2 reaches the history transactions).
        dropout: dropout in the encoder (and GAT attention dropout).
        entity_embedding_dim: width of the ID embeddings (projected to ``hidden_dim``).
        conv_type: "sage" or "gat".
        gat_heads: attention heads for "gat".
        max_history: most recent history transactions fed to the GRU per seed.
        gru_layers: stacked GRU layers.
        use_temporal: False -> spatial encoder only (SAGEConv/GATConv + MLP ablation).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        in_dim: int,
        entity_vocab_sizes: Mapping[str, int],
        hidden_dim: int = 128,
        num_layers: int = 2,
        dropout: float = 0.2,
        entity_embedding_dim: int = 32,
        conv_type: ConvType = "sage",
        gat_heads: int = 4,
        max_history: int = 20,
        gru_layers: int = 1,
        use_temporal: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if conv_type not in ("sage", "gat"):
            raise ValueError("conv_type must be 'sage' or 'gat'.")
        if conv_type == "gat" and hidden_dim % gat_heads != 0:
            raise ValueError("hidden_dim must be divisible by gat_heads for GAT.")
        if num_layers < 1 or max_history < 1:
            raise ValueError("num_layers and max_history must be >= 1.")
        if not entity_vocab_sizes:
            raise ValueError("At least one entity type is required.")
        self.conv_type = conv_type
        self.hidden_dim = hidden_dim
        self.num_layers = num_layers
        self.max_history = max_history
        self.use_temporal = use_temporal
        self.entity_names: list[str] = list(entity_vocab_sizes)
        self.node_types: list[str] = [TXN, *self.entity_names]
        # edge-type names must match src/graph/builder.py
        self.edge_types: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = [
            et for e in self.entity_names for et in ((TXN, f"uses_{e}", e), (e, f"rev_uses_{e}", TXN))
        ]

        # --- input projections -------------------------------------------------------------
        self.txn_in = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(in_dim, hidden_dim), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(dropout))
        self.ent_emb = nn.ModuleDict(
            {e: nn.Embedding(max(int(n), 1), entity_embedding_dim) for e, n in entity_vocab_sizes.items()}
        )
        for emb in self.ent_emb.values():
            nn.init.normal_(emb.weight, std=0.01)  # never-trained ids stay ~0 ("unknown entity")
        self.ent_proj = nn.ModuleDict({e: nn.Linear(entity_embedding_dim, hidden_dim) for e in self.entity_names})

        # --- spatial encoder ---------------------------------------------------------------
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList(
            [
                HeteroConv(
                    {et: self._make_conv(conv_type, hidden_dim, gat_heads, dropout) for et in self.edge_types},
                    aggr="sum",
                )
                for _ in range(num_layers)
            ]
        )
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList(
            [nn.ModuleDict({nt: nn.LayerNorm(hidden_dim) for nt in self.node_types}) for _ in range(num_layers)]
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

        # --- temporal encoder --------------------------------------------------------------
        if use_temporal:
            self.gru = nn.GRU(
                input_size=hidden_dim + 1,  # +1: log1p(days between the history transaction and its seed)
                hidden_size=hidden_dim,
                num_layers=gru_layers,
                batch_first=True,
                dropout=dropout if gru_layers > 1 else 0.0,
            )
            self.fuse = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2 * hidden_dim, hidden_dim), nn.ReLU())
        self.out_dim = hidden_dim

    @staticmethod
    def _make_conv(conv_type: str, hidden: int, heads: int, dropout: float) -> nn.Module:
        if conv_type == "sage":
            return SAGEConv((hidden, hidden), hidden)
        # bipartite GAT: self-loops would mix source and destination index spaces -> disabled
        return GATConv((hidden, hidden), hidden // heads, heads=heads, concat=True,
                       dropout=dropout, add_self_loops=False)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(self, batch: HeteroData) -> Tensor:
        """Return seed embeddings ``[batch_size, out_dim]``."""
        store = batch[TXN]
        bs = int(store.batch_size)

        h: dict[str, Tensor] = {TXN: self.txn_in(store.x)}
        for e in self.entity_names:
            if e in batch.node_types:  # a type can be absent if it had no edges in this phase
                h[e] = self.ent_proj[e](self.ent_emb[e](batch[e].node_id))
        h_txn0 = h[TXN]  # input projection of every sampled transaction (history + seeds)

        present = set(batch.edge_types)
        edge_index_dict = {et: batch[et].edge_index for et in self.edge_types if et in present}
        for conv, norms in zip(self.convs, self.norms):
            out = conv(h, edge_index_dict)  # only destination types that received a message
            new_h: dict[str, Tensor] = {}
            for nt, x in h.items():
                if nt in out:
                    x = norms[nt](x + self.dropout(F.relu(out[nt])))
                new_h[nt] = x
            h = new_h

        spatial = h[TXN][:bs]  # seeds are the first ``batch_size`` transaction nodes
        if not self.use_temporal:
            return spatial
        temporal = self._temporal_encode(store, h_txn0, bs)
        return self.fuse(torch.cat([spatial, temporal], dim=-1))

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _temporal_encode(self, store, h0: Tensor, bs: int) -> Tensor:
        """GRU over [oldest history ... most recent history, seed] for every seed."""
        if "batch" not in store:
            raise ValueError("Temporal encoder needs disjoint batches (build_neighbor_loader provides them).")
        device = h0.device
        n = h0.size(0)
        sub = store.batch  # [n] subgraph id of each transaction node
        t = store.time  # [n] int64 seconds

        # row index of the seed that owns each node (does not assume subgraph id == seed position)
        row_of_sub = torch.zeros(bs, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
        row_of_sub[sub[:bs]] = torch.arange(bs, device=device)
        row = row_of_sub[sub]  # [n]; row[:bs] == arange(bs)

        seed_time = torch.zeros(bs, dtype=t.dtype, device=device)
        seed_time[row[:bs]] = t[:bs]
        dt_days = (seed_time[row] - t).clamp(min=0).to(h0.dtype) / float(SECONDS_PER_DAY)  # 0 for seeds
        feat = torch.cat([h0, torch.log1p(dt_days).unsqueeze(-1)], dim=-1)  # [n, hidden + 1]

        k = self.max_history
        seq = feat.new_zeros(bs, k + 1, feat.size(-1))
        kept = torch.zeros(bs, dtype=torch.long, device=device)  # history length kept per seed

        if n > bs:
            hist = torch.arange(bs, n, device=device)  # non-seed transaction nodes = history
            order = hist[torch.argsort(t[hist], stable=True)]  # by time ...
            order = order[torch.argsort(row[order], stable=True)]  # ... then grouped by seed row
            r = row[order]
            counts = torch.bincount(r, minlength=bs)  # history size per seed
            start = torch.cumsum(counts, dim=0) - counts  # first index of each group
            pos = torch.arange(order.numel(), device=device) - start[r]  # 0 = oldest of the group
            from_end = counts[r] - 1 - pos  # 0 = most recent of the group
            keep = from_end < k  # keep the k most recent
            order, r, from_end = order[keep], r[keep], from_end[keep]
            kept = counts.clamp(max=k)
            col = kept[r] - 1 - from_end  # oldest kept -> column 0, most recent -> column kept-1
            seq[r, col] = feat[order]

        seq[torch.arange(bs, device=device), kept] = feat[:bs]  # the seed closes its own sequence
        lengths = (kept + 1).cpu()  # >= 1
        packed = pack_padded_sequence(seq, lengths, batch_first=True, enforce_sorted=False)
        _, h_n = self.gru(packed)  # [gru_layers, bs, hidden], returned in the original seed order
        return h_n[-1]

================================================================================

FILE: src\train\trainer.py

================================================================================

"""Trainer: warm-up, early stopping, validation metrics, oracle switch, tw_nndf diagnostics, run_one().

Protocol (frozen by construction, see docstrings):
  * train graph: labels are ``y_obs`` / ``pnu`` at the label cut; ``y_true`` is dropped by the loader.
    ORACLE run: the train loader keeps ``y_true`` and ``NaiveBCELoss`` receives ``y_true`` as the target.
  * validation (``y_true``, full labels) selects the best epoch and the FPR=1% threshold; the test set is only
    scored once, after the best epoch is restored. Test labels never select anything.
  * scores are LOGITS.
Nothing here is a reproduction of a paper; see the open points in the round-6 notes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import math
import time
import gc
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

from src.losses.calibration import (
    MatureTemperatureTracker, SlidingWindowPrior, sliding_window_prior, unlabeled_prior, young_counts,
)
from src.losses.maturity_mask import MaturityMaskLoss
from src.losses.tw_nndf import HybridTWnnDFLoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss, estimate_class_prior
from src.losses.tw_nndf import HybridTWnnDFLoss
from src.data.dataset import load_phase_datasets, read_meta, seed_labels
from src.eval import metrics as M
from src.graph.builder import PNU_CODES, TXN
from src.losses.tw_nndf import HybridTWnnDFLoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss
from src.losses import (
    NaiveBCELoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss, build_loss, compute_age_days, estimate_class_prior,
    lam_from_median_delay,
)
from src.losses import DelayIPWLoss
from src.models.classifier import FraudClassifier, entity_vocab_sizes_from_meta
from src.train.utils import (
    PREREG_STATUS, EarlyStopper, Stopwatch, get_device, make_run_id, peak_memory_mb, reset_peak_memory,
    set_seed, warmup_factor,
)

LOSS_NAMES = ("naive_bce", "maturity_mask", "delay_ipw", "tw_nndf")
MONITORS = ("pr_auc", "roc_auc", "recall_at_fpr")
NNDF_KEYS = ("pos_risk", "neg_risk_raw", "clamped", "ascent", "bce")
PRIOR_MODES = ("mature_mean", "sliding_window", "sliding_unlabeled", "sliding_dynamic")
AMOUNT_KEYS = ("amount_aware", "amount_quantile", "amount_min_age_days")

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Configuration
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass
class TrainConfig:
    loss: str = "naive_bce"
    oracle: bool = False                 # True: NaiveBCE on y_true (needs expose_train_true_labels)
    epochs: int = 30
    lr: float = 1e-3
    weight_decay: float = 1e-5
    warmup_epochs: int = 1
    patience: int = 5
    min_delta: float = 0.0
    monitor: str = "pr_auc"
    grad_clip: float | None = 1.0
    seed: int = 42
    device: str = "auto"
    fpr: float = 0.01
    review_cost: float = 0.0
    prior: float | None = None           # tw_nndf: None -> estimated on the mature region (observed labels only)
    loss_params: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    max_train_batches: int | None = None  # debug / profiling only
    save_dir: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.loss not in LOSS_NAMES:
            raise ValueError(f"loss must be one of {LOSS_NAMES}.")
        if self.monitor not in MONITORS:
            raise ValueError(f"monitor must be one of {MONITORS}.")
        if self.epochs < 1 or self.warmup_epochs < 0 or self.warmup_epochs >= self.epochs:
            raise ValueError("need epochs >= 1 and 0 <= warmup_epochs < epochs.")

    @classmethod
    def from_cfg(cls, cfg: Mapping[str, Any], **overrides: Any) -> "TrainConfig":
        """Read ``train:``, ``eval:`` and ``loss:`` of base.yaml; keyword overrides win."""
        t, e, lc = dict(cfg.get("train", {})), dict(cfg.get("eval", {})), dict(cfg.get("loss", {}))
        name = overrides.pop("loss", None) or lc.get("name", "naive_bce")
        clip = t.get("grad_clip", 1.0)
        kw: dict[str, Any] = dict(
            loss=name,
            loss_params=dict(lc.get(name, {}) or {}),
            epochs=int(t.get("epochs", 30)),
            lr=float(t.get("lr", 1e-3)),
            weight_decay=float(t.get("weight_decay", 1e-5)),
            warmup_epochs=int(t.get("warmup_epochs", 1)),
            patience=int(t.get("patience", 5)),
            min_delta=float(t.get("min_delta", 0.0)),
            monitor=str(t.get("monitor", "pr_auc")),
            grad_clip=None if clip is None else float(clip),
            seed=int(cfg.get("seed", 42)),
            fpr=float(e.get("fpr", 0.01)),
            review_cost=float(e.get("review_cost", 0.0)),
        )
        kw.update(overrides)
        return cls(**kw)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Loss construction (+ prior / delay diagnostics for the log)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #

def build_training_loss(cfg: TrainConfig, train_ds: Any, meta: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[nn.Module, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return ``(loss module, info dict)``. ``info`` is stored with every run for the prior analysis.

    Only ``train_ds`` is given: no validation / test statistic can reach a loss through this function. The p95
    amount, mean amount and every prior come from the TRAIN graph (train-view rows) only.
    """
    if cfg.oracle:
        return NaiveBCELoss(), {"loss": "naive_bce", "oracle": True}
    full = train_ds.data[TXN]
    label_cut = float(train_ds.data.label_cut)
    window = float(meta["config"]["delay"]["maturity_window_days"])
    in_u = (full.pnu == PNU_CODES["U"]) & full.in_train_view
    age_all = compute_age_days(full.time, label_cut)
    amt_view = full.amount[full.in_train_view].float()  # TRAIN graph, train-view rows only
    info: dict[str, Any] = {
        "loss": cfg.loss, "oracle": False, "maturity_window_days": window,
        "n_P": int((full.pnu == PNU_CODES["P"]).sum()), "n_N": int((full.pnu == PNU_CODES["N"]).sum()),
        "n_U": int(in_u.sum()),
        # DIAGNOSTIC ONLY (reads y_true to document the prior mismatch; never passed to any loss)
        "diag_true_fraud_rate_in_U": float(full.y_true[in_u].float().mean()) if bool(in_u.any()) else None,
    }
    params = dict(cfg.loss_params)

    def amount_rule(aw: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[float | None, float]:
        """Window + Amount-Aware rule shared by maturity_mask and tw_nndf: ``(p95_train or None, min_age)``."""
        if not bool(aw.get("amount_aware", False)):
            return None, 0.0
        q = float(aw.get("amount_quantile", 0.95))
        if not 0.0 < q < 1.0:
            raise ValueError("amount_quantile must be in (0, 1).")
        if amt_view.numel() == 0:
            raise ValueError("No train-view rows: cannot compute the amount quantile.")
        thr = float(torch.quantile(amt_view, q))  # TRAIN amounts only
        mn = aw.get("amount_min_age_days")
        min_age = window / 2.0 if mn is None else float(mn)  # default W/2 = design choice (Threats to Validity)
        trusted = in_u & (full.amount >= thr) & (age_all >= min_age)
        info.update(
            amount_aware=True, amount_quantile=q, amount_threshold=thr, amount_min_age_days=min_age,
            n_U_trusted=int(trusted.sum()),
            # DIAGNOSTIC ONLY (reads y_true: share of hidden fraud among the U rows treated as negatives)
            diag_true_fraud_rate_in_trusted_U=(
                float(full.y_true[trusted].float().mean()) if bool(trusted.any()) else None),
        )
        return thr, min_age

    if cfg.loss == "maturity_mask":
        thr, min_age = amount_rule({k: params.pop(k) for k in AMOUNT_KEYS if k in params})
        if thr is not None:
            params.update(amount_threshold=thr, amount_min_age_days=min_age)
        return MaturityMaskLoss(**params), info

    if cfg.loss == "tw_nndf":
        prior_mode = str(params.pop("prior_mode", "mature_mean"))
        span_days = float(params.pop("prior_span_days", 14.0))
        n_windows = int(params.pop("prior_n_windows", 3))
        shrink = float(params.pop("prior_shrink", 200.0))
        variant = str(params.pop("variant", "pu_only"))
        lambda_pu = float(params.pop("lambda_pu", 1.0))
        calib_cfg = dict(params.pop("calibration", None) or {})
        aw = {k: params.pop(k) for k in AMOUNT_KEYS if k in params}
        if prior_mode not in PRIOR_MODES:
            raise ValueError(f"prior_mode must be one of {PRIOR_MODES}.")
        if variant not in ("pu_only", "hybrid"):
            raise ValueError("loss.tw_nndf.variant must be 'pu_only' or 'hybrid'.")
        if "unlabeled_statuses" in params:
            params["unlabeled_statuses"] = tuple(params["unlabeled_statuses"])

        est = estimate_class_prior(full.y_obs, age_all, window, full.in_train_view)  # observed labels only
        prior_auto = est
        info.update(prior_estimated=est)
        if prior_mode != "mature_mean":
            sw = sliding_window_prior(full.y_obs, age_all, window, in_train_view=full.in_train_view,
                                      span_days=span_days, n_windows=n_windows)
            prior_auto = sw.prior  # sliding_dynamic uses it as the fallback scalar
            info.update(prior_sliding=sw.prior, prior_window_rates=list(sw.window_rates),
                        prior_window_counts=list(sw.window_counts))
            if prior_mode == "sliding_unlabeled":
                if tuple(params.get("unlabeled_statuses", ("U",))) != ("U",):
                    raise ValueError("prior_mode='sliding_unlabeled' needs unlabeled_statuses == ['U'].")
                n_p, n_u = young_counts(full.pnu, age_all, window, full.in_train_view)
                prior_auto = unlabeled_prior(sw.prior, n_p, n_u)
                info.update(n_P_young=n_p, n_U_young=n_u)
        prior = cfg.prior if cfg.prior is not None else prior_auto
        source = "config" if cfg.prior is not None else ("estimated" if prior_mode == "mature_mean" else prior_mode)
        info.update(prior=prior, prior_mode=prior_mode, prior_source=source, variant=variant)
        if bool(params.get("amount_weighted", False)):
            params["amount_ref"] = float(amt_view.mean())  # mean TRAIN amount
            info.update(amount_weighted=True, amount_ref=params["amount_ref"])

        if variant == "pu_only":  # V1 / V1.5 behaviour: hybrid-only keys were popped above and are ignored
            return TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=prior, **params), info

        thr, min_age = amount_rule(aw)
        sp = None
        if prior_mode == "sliding_dynamic" and cfg.prior is None:  # --prior override pins a fixed pi
            sp = SlidingWindowPrior.fit(full.y_obs, age_all, window, full.in_train_view,
                                        span_days=span_days, shrink=shrink)
            info.update(prior_dynamic_min=float(sp.table.min()), prior_dynamic_max=float(sp.table.max()))
        tracker = MatureTemperatureTracker(**calib_cfg) if bool(calib_cfg.pop("enabled", True)) else None
        info.update(lambda_pu=lambda_pu, calibration=tracker is not None)
        return HybridTWnnDFLoss(prior=prior, lambda_pu=lambda_pu, amount_threshold=thr,
                                amount_min_age_days=min_age, calibrator=tracker, sliding_prior=sp, **params), info

    if cfg.loss == "delay_ipw":
        lam = params.pop("lam", None)
        info["lam_defaulted"] = lam is None
        lam = lam_from_median_delay(window / 2.0) if lam is None else float(lam)  # arbitrary default: sweep it
        info["lam"] = lam
        return DelayIPWLoss(lam=lam, **params), info

    return build_loss(cfg.loss, **params), info


def compute_batch_loss(
    model: nn.Module, loss_fn: nn.Module, batch: Any, label_cut: float, oracle: bool
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    """Forward + loss on the seed rows of one batch (shared with scripts/profile_epoch.py)."""
    labels = seed_labels(batch)
    if oracle:
        if "y_true" not in labels:
            raise KeyError("Oracle run needs y_true in the train batches (expose_train_true_labels=True).")
        y = labels["y_true"]
    else:
        y = labels["y_obs"]
    age = compute_age_days(labels["time"], label_cut)
    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {"age_days": age, "in_train_view": labels["in_train_view"]}
    if getattr(loss_fn, "needs_amount", False):  # amount-aware masking / cost-sensitive nnDF only
        if "amount" not in labels:
            raise KeyError("This loss needs `amount` in the batches.")
        kwargs["amount"] = labels["amount"]
    logits = model(batch)
    loss = loss_fn(logits, y, labels["pnu"], **kwargs)
    return loss, logits


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# One step / prediction
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def compute_batch_loss(
    model: nn.Module, loss_fn: nn.Module, batch: Any, label_cut: float, oracle: bool
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    """Forward + loss on the seed rows of one batch (shared with scripts/profile_epoch.py)."""
    labels = seed_labels(batch)
    if oracle:
        if "y_true" not in labels:
            raise KeyError("Oracle run needs y_true in the train batches (expose_train_true_labels=True).")
        y = labels["y_true"]
    else:
        y = labels["y_obs"]
    age = compute_age_days(labels["time"], label_cut)
    logits = model(batch)
    extra = {"amount": labels["amount"]} if getattr(loss_fn, "needs_amount", False) else {}
    loss = loss_fn(logits, y, labels["pnu"], age_days=age, in_train_view=labels["in_train_view"], **extra)
    return loss, logits


@torch.no_grad()
def predict(model: nn.Module, ds: Any, device: torch.device, max_batches: int | None = None) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    """Logits and evaluation fields for every seed of ``ds`` (needs ``y_true`` and ``amount`` in the batches)."""
    was_training = model.training
    model.eval()
    scores, ys, amounts = [], [], []
    try:
        for i, batch in enumerate(ds):
            if max_batches is not None and i >= max_batches:
                break
            batch = batch.to(device)
            labels = seed_labels(batch)
            if "y_true" not in labels or "amount" not in labels:
                raise KeyError("Evaluation needs y_true and amount in the batches (expose_true_labels=True).")
            scores.append(model(batch).detach().float().cpu().numpy())
            ys.append(labels["y_true"].cpu().numpy())
            amounts.append(labels["amount"].cpu().numpy().astype(np.float64))
    finally:
        model.train(was_training)
    return {"scores": np.concatenate(scores), "y_true": np.concatenate(ys), "amount": np.concatenate(amounts)}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Trainer
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
class Trainer:
    def __init__(
        self, model: nn.Module, loss_fn: nn.Module, train_ds: Any, val_ds: Any, cfg: TrainConfig,
        *, log: Callable[[str], None] = print,
    ) -> None:
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.device = get_device(cfg.device)
        self.model = model.to(self.device)
        self.loss_fn = loss_fn.to(self.device)
        self.train_ds, self.val_ds = train_ds, val_ds
        self.label_cut = float(train_ds.data.label_cut)
        self.log = log
        self.opt = torch.optim.Adam(self.model.parameters(), lr=cfg.lr, weight_decay=cfg.weight_decay)
        n = len(train_ds)
        self.steps_per_epoch = n if cfg.max_train_batches is None else min(n, cfg.max_train_batches)
        self.warmup_steps = cfg.warmup_epochs * self.steps_per_epoch
        self.global_step = 0
        self._is_nndf = isinstance(loss_fn, TimeWindowNNDFLoss)

    # ---- one epoch ---------------------------------------------------------- #
    def train_epoch(self) -> dict[str, float]:
        cfg, dev = self.cfg, self.device
        self.model.train()
        sw = Stopwatch(dev)
        sw.start()
        loss_sum = torch.zeros((), device=dev)
        risk_sum = {k: torch.zeros((), device=dev) for k in NNDF_KEYS}
        n_used = n_empty = 0
        for step, batch in enumerate(self.train_ds):
            if cfg.max_train_batches is not None and step >= cfg.max_train_batches:
                break
            batch = batch.to(dev)
            lr = cfg.lr * warmup_factor(self.global_step, self.warmup_steps)
            for g in self.opt.param_groups:
                g["lr"] = lr
            self.global_step += 1

            loss, _ = compute_batch_loss(self.model, self.loss_fn, batch, self.label_cut, cfg.oracle)
            self.opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            if self._is_nndf and self.loss_fn.last_risks is None:  # no P or no U in this batch
                n_empty += 1
                continue
            loss.backward()
            if cfg.grad_clip is not None:
                nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.model.parameters(), cfg.grad_clip)
            self.opt.step()
            loss_sum += loss.detach()
            n_used += 1
            if self._is_nndf:  # tensors accumulated on device: no GPU sync per step
                for k in NNDF_KEYS:
                    risk_sum[k] += self.loss_fn.last_risks.get(k, loss_sum.new_zeros(())).to(dev)  # pu_only has no "bce"
        elapsed = sw.stop()

        denom = max(n_used, 1)
        mean_loss = float(loss_sum) / denom
        if not math.isfinite(mean_loss):
            raise FloatingPointError("Training loss is not finite: training diverged.")
        out = {"train_loss": mean_loss, "n_steps": float(n_used), "n_empty_batches": float(n_empty),
               "lr_end": float(self.opt.param_groups[0]["lr"]), "train_sec": elapsed}
        if self._is_nndf:
            out.update({"nndf_pos_risk": float(risk_sum["pos_risk"]) / denom,
                        "nndf_neg_risk_raw": float(risk_sum["neg_risk_raw"]) / denom,
                        "nndf_clamped_rate": float(risk_sum["clamped"]) / denom,
                        "nndf_bce": float(risk_sum["bce"]) / denom,
                        "nndf_ascent_rate": float(risk_sum["ascent"]) / denom})
        return out

    # ---- evaluation ---------------------------------------------------------- #
    def evaluate(self, ds: Any) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, np.ndarray]]:
        out = predict(self.model, ds, self.device)
        return M.summarize_scores(out["y_true"], out["scores"], fpr=self.cfg.fpr), out

    def evaluate_test(self, test_ds: Any, threshold: float) -> dict[str, float]:
        """Score the test set ONCE with the threshold fitted on validation."""
        m, out = self.evaluate(test_ds)
        op = M.operating_point(out["y_true"], out["scores"], out["amount"], threshold,
                               review_cost=self.cfg.review_cost)
        return {**m, **{f"op_{k}": v for k, v in op.items()}}

    # ---- full training run ---------------------------------------------------- #
    def fit(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        cfg = self.cfg
        stopper = EarlyStopper(cfg.patience, cfg.min_delta, start_epoch=cfg.warmup_epochs + 1)
        best_state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None = None
        history: list[dict[str, float]] = []
        stopped_early = False
        reset_peak_memory(self.device)
        t0 = time.perf_counter()

        for epoch in range(1, cfg.epochs + 1):
            tr = self.train_epoch()
            va, _ = self.evaluate(self.val_ds)
            improved = stopper.update(va[cfg.monitor], epoch)
            if improved:
                best_state = {k: v.detach().cpu().clone() for k, v in self.model.state_dict().items()}
                if cfg.save_dir:
                    Path(cfg.save_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    torch.save({"epoch": epoch, "state_dict": best_state, "cfg": asdict(cfg)},
                               Path(cfg.save_dir) / "best.pt")
            history.append({"epoch": float(epoch), **tr, "val_pr_auc": va["pr_auc"],
                            "val_roc_auc": va["roc_auc"], "val_recall_at_fpr": va["recall_at_fpr"]})
            extra = (f" | nndf neg_raw={tr['nndf_neg_risk_raw']:+.4f} clamped={tr['nndf_clamped_rate']:.2f}"
                     f" ascent={tr['nndf_ascent_rate']:.2f} bce={tr['nndf_bce']:.4f} T={tr.get('calib_T', float('nan')):.3f}"
                     f" empty={int(tr['n_empty_batches'])}") if self._is_nndf else ""
            self.log(f"epoch {epoch:>2} loss={tr['train_loss']:.4f} val_prauc={va['pr_auc']:.4f} "
                     f"val_auc={va['roc_auc']:.4f} val_rec@fpr={va['recall_at_fpr']:.4f} "
                     f"{'*' if improved else ' '} ({tr['train_sec']:.0f}s){extra}")
            if stopper.should_stop:
                stopped_early = True
                break

        if best_state is None:
            raise RuntimeError("No finite validation score: training diverged.")
        self.model.load_state_dict(best_state)
        val, val_out = self.evaluate(self.val_ds)
        thr = val["threshold_at_fpr"]
        val_op = M.operating_point(val_out["y_true"], val_out["scores"], val_out["amount"], thr,
                                   review_cost=cfg.review_cost)
        return {
            "best_epoch": stopper.best_epoch, "epochs_run": len(history), "stopped_early": stopped_early,
            "history": history, "val": val, "val_op": val_op, "val_threshold": thr,
            "train_seconds": time.perf_counter() - t0, "peak_vram_mb": peak_memory_mb(self.device),
        }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# One benchmark run
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def run_one(
    cfg: Mapping[str, Any],
    processed_dir: str | Path,
    *,
    loss: str | None = None,
    oracle: bool = False,
    backbone: str | None = None,
    use_temporal: bool | None = None,
    lp: bool | None = None,
    seed: int | None = None,
    prior: float | None = None,          # tw_nndf prior override (prior-sensitivity group only)
    device: str = "auto",
    save_dir: str | None = None,
    max_train_batches: int | None = None,
    log: Callable[[str], None] = print,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Train one configuration end to end; return ``{"row": flat dict for the results CSV, "details": ...}``."""
    pdir = Path(processed_dir)
    meta = read_meta(pdir)
    tcfg = TrainConfig.from_cfg(cfg, loss=loss, oracle=oracle, device=device, save_dir=save_dir,
                                 max_train_batches=max_train_batches, prior=prior,
                                **({} if seed is None else {"seed": seed}))
    set_seed(tcfg.seed)

    features_cfg = copy.deepcopy(dict(cfg.get("features", {})))
    if lp is not None:
        features_cfg["lp_features"] = {**(features_cfg.get("lp_features") or {}), "enabled": bool(lp)}
    sampler_cfg = dict(cfg["sampler"])
    dsets = load_phase_datasets(pdir, sampler_cfg, phases=("train", "val"), features_cfg=features_cfg,
                                expose_train_true_labels=oracle)
    train_ds, val_ds = dsets["train"], dsets["val"]

    model_cfg = dict(cfg["model"])
    if use_temporal is not None:
        model_cfg["use_temporal"] = use_temporal
    backbone = backbone or str(model_cfg.get("backbone", "sage"))
    model = FraudClassifier.from_config(model_cfg, in_dim=train_ds.model_in_dim,  # 457, or 461 with LP features
                                        entity_vocab_sizes=entity_vocab_sizes_from_meta(meta), conv_type=backbone)
    loss_fn, info = build_training_loss(tcfg, train_ds, meta)

    lp_on = train_ds.lp_features is not None
    gru_on = bool(model_cfg.get("use_temporal", True))
    label = "oracle" if oracle else tcfg.loss
    run_id = make_run_id(pdir.name, label, backbone, gru_on, lp_on, tcfg.seed)
    log(f"[{run_id}] in_dim={train_ds.model_in_dim} params={model.num_parameters():,} info={info}")

    trainer = Trainer(model, loss_fn, train_ds, val_ds, tcfg, log=log)
    fit = trainer.fit()
    trainer.train_ds = trainer.val_ds = None   # Trainer holds references: drop them or `del` frees nothing
    del dsets, train_ds, val_ds
    gc.collect()
    test_ds = load_phase_datasets(pdir, sampler_cfg, phases=("test",), features_cfg=features_cfg)["test"]
    test = trainer.evaluate_test(test_ds, fit["val_threshold"])

    row = {
        "run_id": run_id, "delay": pdir.name, "loss": label, "oracle": oracle, "backbone": backbone,
        "use_temporal": gru_on, "lp_features": lp_on, "seed": tcfg.seed, "in_dim": model.backbone.txn_in[0].in_features,
        "n_params": model.num_parameters(), "prereg_status": PREREG_STATUS,
        "best_epoch": fit["best_epoch"], "epochs_run": fit["epochs_run"],
        "val_pr_auc": fit["val"]["pr_auc"], "val_roc_auc": fit["val"]["roc_auc"],
        "test_pr_auc": test["pr_auc"], "test_roc_auc": test["roc_auc"],
        "test_recall_at_fpr": test["recall_at_fpr"],            # own-curve recall at FPR <= fpr
        "test_recall_at_valthr": test["op_recall"], "test_fpr_at_valthr": test["op_fpr"],
        "test_efl": test["op_efl"], "test_efl_ratio": test["op_efl_ratio"],
        "train_seconds": fit["train_seconds"],
        "peak_vram_alloc_mb": None if fit["peak_vram_mb"] is None else fit["peak_vram_mb"]["allocated"],
    }
    return {"row": row, "details": {"info": info, "fit": fit, "test": test, "cfg": asdict(tcfg)}}


__all__ = ["TrainConfig", "build_training_loss", "compute_batch_loss", "predict", "Trainer", "run_one"]

FILE: src\train\utils.py

"""Training helpers: config merge, seeding, device/timing/VRAM, JSON, LR warm-up, early stopping."""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
import math
import random
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping

import numpy as np
import torch
import yaml

# The benchmark matrix (72 vs 90 runs) is NOT frozen yet: every result row carries this tag.
# Change it to "frozen" only after the matrix, thresholds and sweeps are fixed and committed.
PREREG_STATUS: str = "unfrozen"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Config
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def deep_update(base: dict[str, Any], overlay: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Recursively merge ``overlay`` into a copy of ``base``."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(base)
    for k, v in overlay.items():
        if isinstance(v, Mapping) and isinstance(out.get(k), dict):
            out[k] = deep_update(out[k], v)
        else:
            out[k] = copy.deepcopy(v)
    return out


def load_config(base_path: str | Path, delay_path: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """``base.yaml`` (+ an optional ``delay_*.yaml`` overlay that must contain a ``delay:`` section)."""
    cfg = yaml.safe_load(Path(base_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    if delay_path is not None:
        overlay = yaml.safe_load(Path(delay_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
        if "delay" not in overlay:
            raise KeyError(f"{delay_path} must contain a top-level 'delay:' section.")
        cfg = deep_update(cfg, overlay)
    return cfg


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Reproducibility / device / timing
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def set_seed(seed: int) -> None:
    """Seed python, numpy and torch. GPU scatter ops are NOT bit-reproducible, and whether the pyg-lib
    sampler honours ``torch.manual_seed`` was not verified (KHÔNG CHẮC): expect seed-to-seed variance."""
    random.seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)


def get_device(name: str = "auto") -> torch.device:
    if name == "auto":
        return torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    dev = torch.device(name)
    if dev.type == "cuda" and not torch.cuda.is_available():
        raise RuntimeError("CUDA requested but not available.")
    return dev


def sync(device: torch.device) -> None:
    if device.type == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.synchronize(device)


def reset_peak_memory(device: torch.device) -> None:
    if device.type == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats(device)


def peak_memory_mb(device: torch.device) -> dict[str, float] | None:
    """Peak allocated / reserved CUDA memory in MB since the last reset (``None`` on CPU)."""
    if device.type != "cuda":
        return None
    return {
        "allocated": torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated(device) / 2**20,
        "reserved": torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved(device) / 2**20,
    }


class Stopwatch:
    """Accumulating wall-clock timer that synchronises CUDA before reading the clock."""

    def __init__(self, device: torch.device | None = None) -> None:
        self.device = device
        self.total = 0.0
        self._t0: float | None = None

    def start(self) -> None:
        if self.device is not None:
            sync(self.device)
        self._t0 = time.perf_counter()

    def stop(self) -> float:
        if self._t0 is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Stopwatch.stop() before start().")
        if self.device is not None:
            sync(self.device)
        dt = time.perf_counter() - self._t0
        self.total += dt
        self._t0 = None
        return dt


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Schedules
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def warmup_factor(step: int, warmup_steps: int) -> float:
    """Linear LR multiplier in (0, 1]: ``(step + 1) / warmup_steps`` until warm-up ends, then 1."""
    if warmup_steps <= 0:
        return 1.0
    return min(1.0, (step + 1) / float(warmup_steps))


class EarlyStopper:
    """Early stopping on a metric to MAXIMISE (PR-AUC, ROC-AUC, recall).

    Epochs before ``start_epoch`` (the warm-up) can still become the best epoch, but a non-improving
    epoch only counts towards ``patience`` from ``start_epoch`` on. NaN/inf never counts as improvement.
    """

    def __init__(self, patience: int, min_delta: float = 0.0, start_epoch: int = 1) -> None:
        if patience < 1:
            raise ValueError("patience must be >= 1.")
        self.patience = patience
        self.min_delta = float(min_delta)
        self.start_epoch = start_epoch
        self.best: float | None = None
        self.best_epoch = 0
        self.bad_epochs = 0

    def update(self, value: float | None, epoch: int) -> bool:
        """Record the epoch's metric; return True if it is a new best."""
        finite = value is not None and math.isfinite(value)
        improved = finite and (self.best is None or value > self.best + self.min_delta)
        if improved:
            self.best, self.best_epoch, self.bad_epochs = float(value), epoch, 0
        elif epoch >= self.start_epoch:
            self.bad_epochs += 1
        return bool(improved)

    @property
    def should_stop(self) -> bool:
        return self.bad_epochs >= self.patience


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Output helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def jsonable(obj: Any) -> Any:
    """Make numpy / torch / Path values JSON-safe; non-finite floats become ``null``."""
    if isinstance(obj, Mapping):
        return {str(k): jsonable(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple, set)):
        return [jsonable(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):
        return jsonable(obj.tolist())
    if isinstance(obj, torch.Tensor):
        return jsonable(obj.detach().cpu().tolist())
    if isinstance(obj, (np.bool_, bool)):
        return bool(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer, int)):
        return int(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, (np.floating, float)):
        v = float(obj)
        return v if math.isfinite(v) else None
    if isinstance(obj, Path):
        return str(obj)
    return obj if (obj is None or isinstance(obj, str)) else str(obj)


def write_json(path: str | Path, obj: Any) -> Path:
    p = Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text(json.dumps(jsonable(obj), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return p


def make_run_id(delay: str, loss: str, backbone: str, use_temporal: bool, lp: bool, seed: int) -> str:
    """Stable id, e.g. ``delay_high_tw_nndf_sage_gru_nolp_s42`` (also the auto-resume key in round 7)."""
    return "_".join([delay, loss, backbone, "gru" if use_temporal else "nogru", "lp" if lp else "nolp", f"s{seed}"])

FILE: scripts\check_sampler_causality.py

"""Throwaway diagnostic: causal sampler + value-based feature-leak scan."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

import numpy as np
import torch

from src.data.dataset import load_phase_graph
from src.graph.builder import TXN
from src.graph.sampler import build_neighbor_loader, has_pyg_lib, verify_batch_causality

ROOT = Path("data/processed/delay_high")
assert has_pyg_lib(), "pyg-lib missing: temporal sampling impossible (do NOT fall back)"
tr, va = load_phase_graph(ROOT / "train.pt"), load_phase_graph(ROOT / "val.pt")

# 1) feature leakage by VALUE: correlation of every feature with y_true on train
x, y = tr[TXN].x.numpy(), tr[TXN].y_true.numpy().astype(np.float32)
corr = np.array([np.corrcoef(x[:, j], y)[0, 1] if x[:, j].std() > 0 else 0.0
                 for j in range(x.shape[1])])
top = np.argsort(-np.abs(corr))[:8]
print("top |corr|:", [(tr.feature_names[i], round(float(corr[i]), 3)) for i in top])
assert np.abs(corr).max() < 0.9, "a feature is almost a copy of the label"
bad = [n for n in tr.feature_names
       if n.lower().startswith(("detection", "y_", "pnu", "in_train", "delay"))]
assert not bad, bad

# 2) "delete the future": same train seeds, same history in train.pt and val.pt
rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
cand = torch.nonzero(tr[TXN].seed_mask).view(-1)[-5000:]        # late-train seeds: longest histories
seeds = cand[torch.from_numpy(rng.choice(len(cand), 50, replace=False))].sort().values


def history(data):
    m = torch.zeros(data[TXN].num_nodes, dtype=torch.bool)
    m[seeds] = True
    loader = build_neighbor_loader(data, [15, 10], batch_size=len(seeds), seed_mask=m,
                                   temporal_strategy="last")
    b = next(iter(loader))
    verify_batch_causality(b)
    st, bs = b[TXN], int(b[TXN].batch_size)
    return st.tid[:bs], [sorted(st.tid[st.batch == i].tolist()) for i in range(bs)]


s_tr, h_tr = history(tr)
s_va, h_va = history(va)
assert torch.equal(s_tr, s_va)
assert h_tr == h_va, "history of a train seed changed when LATER rows were added -> future leaks"
print("OK: causal sampler, delete-the-future and value-based leak scan passed")
