# =====================================================================
# FILE: scripts/make_report.py
# TRẠNG THÁI: ĐÃ SỬA (đường dẫn mặc định: hết lặp "results/results" khi chạy local;
#                     trên Kaggle tìm cả <repo>/results và /kaggle/working/results, loại trùng)
# =====================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Final report: merge benchmark CSVs, 300-dpi PNG figures (+PDF), LaTeX tables printed to stdout.

    python scripts/make_report.py                       # defaults: <repo>/results (+ /kaggle/working/results on Kaggle)
    python scripts/make_report.py --results results/benchmark_results_nnpu.csv --fig_dir figures

Pass --results explicitly when V1 (clamp) and V1.5 (nnpu) CSVs both exist: run_id of tw_nndf is the same in both,
and the analyzer keeps the LAST row per run_id, so a blind glob would let V1.5 silently replace V1.

All statistics come from src.eval.analyzer (same CI / verdict / Recovery-Ratio rules as the pre-registration).
Colour follows the METHOD (Okabe-Ito, colour-blind safe); no-GRU variants are hatched; individual seeds are shown.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import glob
import math
import sys
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

try:
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
    import numpy as np
    import pandas as pd
except ImportError as exc:  # pragma: no cover
    raise SystemExit(f"Missing package '{exc.name}'. Install it with: pip install {exc.name}")

from src.eval import analyzer as A

plt.rcParams.update({"font.size": 9, "axes.titlesize": 10, "axes.labelsize": 9, "legend.fontsize": 8,
                     "axes.spines.top": False, "axes.spines.right": False, "pdf.fonttype": 42,
                     "savefig.dpi": 300})
COLORS = {"naive_bce": "#555555", "maturity_mask": "#0072B2", "delay_ipw": "#E69F00",
          "tw_nndf": "#D55E00", "oracle": "#009E73", "lp": "#CC79A7"}          # Okabe-Ito
MARKERS = {"naive_bce": "s", "maturity_mask": "o", "delay_ipw": "^", "tw_nndf": "D", "oracle": "*", "lp": "v"}
SHORT = {"naive_bce": "A0", "maturity_mask": "Maturity\nMask", "delay_ipw": "Delay-\nIPW", "tw_nndf": "TW-\nnnDF",
         "oracle": "Oracle"}
SHADES = ["#BBBBBB", "#666666", "#222222"]
KAGGLE_ROOT = Path("/kaggle/working")


def style(c: A.Cell) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
    """(label, colour, hatch, marker): colour follows the method, never its rank."""
    key = "lp" if (c.lp and c.loss == "naive_bce") else c.loss
    label = "A0\n+LP" if key == "lp" else SHORT.get(c.loss, c.loss)
    if not c.use_temporal:
        label += "\n(no GRU)"
    return label, COLORS.get(key, "#999999"), "" if c.use_temporal else "//", MARKERS.get(key, "o")


def _delays(names: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(names, key=lambda d: (0 if "low" in d else 1 if "high" in d else 2, d))


def _half(s: dict[str, float]) -> float:
    return s["half"] if math.isfinite(s["half"]) else 0.0


def _save(fig: Any, path: Path, prereg: str) -> None:
    fig.text(0.995, 0.002, f"pre-registration: {prereg}", ha="right", va="bottom", fontsize=6.5, color="#666666")
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=300, bbox_inches="tight")
    fig.savefig(path.with_suffix(".pdf"), bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)


def figure_bars(values: dict, summ: dict, comp: dict, metric: str, path: Path, prereg: str) -> None:
    header, higher = A.METRICS[metric]
    delays = _delays({c.delay for c in values})
    n_seeds = sorted({len(v[metric]) for v in values.values() if v.get(metric)})
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, len(delays), figsize=(4.6 * len(delays), 4.2), squeeze=False, sharey=True)
    for ax, delay in zip(axes[0], delays):
        cells = sorted([c for c, d in values.items() if c.delay == delay and d.get(metric)], key=A._sort_key)
        for x, c in enumerate(cells):
            label, color, hatch, _ = style(c)
            s = summ[(c, metric)]
            ax.bar(x, s["mean"], yerr=_half(s), color=color, alpha=0.85, hatch=hatch, edgecolor="black",
                   linewidth=0.6, capsize=3, zorder=2)
            ax.scatter([x] * len(values[c][metric]), values[c][metric], s=9, color="black", zorder=4)
            if comp.get((c, metric), {}).get("verdict") == "improves":
                ax.annotate("†", (x, s["mean"] + _half(s)), ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=12)
        a0 = [c for c in cells if A._kind(c) == "a0" and c.use_temporal]
        if a0 and math.isfinite(summ[(a0[0], metric)]["ci_low"]):
            s0 = summ[(a0[0], metric)]
            ax.axhspan(s0["ci_low"], s0["ci_high"], color="#999999", alpha=0.2, zorder=0)
            ax.axhline(s0["mean"], color="#555555", ls="--", lw=0.8, zorder=1)
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(cells)))
        ax.set_xticklabels([style(c)[0] for c in cells], fontsize=7.5)
        ax.set_title(delay.replace("_", " "))
        ax.margins(y=0.15)
        ax.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.3, zorder=0)
    axes[0][0].set_ylabel(f"Test {header} ({'higher' if higher else 'lower'} is better)")
    fig.suptitle(f"Test {header} by method (mean ± 95% CI, Student-t, n = {'/'.join(map(str, n_seeds))} seeds)", y=1.0)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0.08, 1, 0.97))
    fig.legend(handles=[Patch(facecolor="#999999", alpha=0.3, label="A0 95% CI"),
                        Line2D([], [], marker="o", color="black", ls="", ms=3, label="individual seeds"),
                        Line2D([], [], marker="$†$", color="black", ls="", ms=7, label="outside A0 CI, favourable side")],
               loc="lower center", ncol=3, frameon=False)
    _save(fig, path, prereg)


def figure_lines(values: dict, summ: dict, path: Path, prereg: str) -> None:
    delays = _delays({c.delay for c in values})
    xs = {d: i for i, d in enumerate(delays)}
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(9.2, 4.0))
    for ax, metric in zip(axes, A.PRIMARY):
        header, higher = A.METRICS[metric]
        fams: dict[tuple, list[A.Cell]] = {}
        for c in values:
            fams.setdefault((c.loss, c.lp, c.use_temporal, c.backbone), []).append(c)
        for cells in fams.values():
            pts = sorted((xs[c.delay], summ[(c, metric)]) for c in cells if (c, metric) in summ)
            if not pts:
                continue
            label, color, _, marker = style(cells[0])
            ax.errorbar([p[0] for p in pts], [p[1]["mean"] for p in pts], yerr=[_half(p[1]) for p in pts],
                        color=color, marker=marker, ms=6, lw=1.4, capsize=3,
                        ls="-" if cells[0].use_temporal else "--", label=label.replace("\n", " "))
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(delays)))
        ax.set_xticklabels([d.replace("_", " ") for d in delays])
        ax.set_xlim(-0.3, len(delays) - 0.7)
        ax.set_xlabel("label-delay regime (lines only connect the regimes; no interpolation is implied)", fontsize=7.5)
        ax.set_ylabel(f"Test {header} ({'higher' if higher else 'lower'} is better)")
        ax.set_title(header)
        ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
        ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=7)
    fig.suptitle("Effect of the delay regime (mean ± 95% CI)", y=1.0)
    fig.tight_layout()
    _save(fig, path, prereg)


def figure_recovery(rec: Sequence[dict], path: Path, prereg: str) -> bool:
    if not rec:
        return False
    delays = _delays({r["delay"] for r in rec})
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(9.2, 4.0))
    for ax, metric in zip(axes, A.PRIMARY):
        rows = [r for r in rec if r["metric"] == metric]
        fams = sorted({(r["loss"], r["lp"], r["use_temporal"], r["backbone"]) for r in rows},
                      key=lambda f: A._sort_key(A.Cell("", f[0], f[3], f[2], f[1])))
        width = 0.8 / max(len(delays), 1)
        for j, d in enumerate(delays):
            for i, f in enumerate(fams):
                r = next((q for q in rows if q["delay"] == d and (q["loss"], q["lp"], q["use_temporal"], q["backbone"]) == f), None)
                if r is None:
                    continue
                x = i + (j - (len(delays) - 1) / 2) * width
                if r["reported"]:
                    ax.bar(x, r["value"], width * 0.92, color=SHADES[j % 3], edgecolor="black", linewidth=0.6)
                else:
                    ax.text(x, 0.02, "n/r", ha="center", va="bottom", rotation=90, fontsize=7, color="#444444")
        ax.axhline(0, color="black", lw=0.8)
        ax.axhline(1, color="#009E73", ls="--", lw=0.9)
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(fams)))
        ax.set_xticklabels([style(A.Cell("", f[0], f[3], f[2], f[1]))[0] for f in fams], fontsize=7.5)
        ax.set_ylabel("Recovery Ratio (0 = A0, 1 = oracle)")
        ax.set_title(A.METRICS[metric][0])
        ax.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.3)
        ax.legend(handles=[Patch(facecolor=SHADES[j % 3], edgecolor="black", label=d.replace("_", " ")) for j, d in enumerate(delays)],
                  frameon=False, fontsize=7)
    fig.suptitle("Recovery Ratio (n/r: oracle-A0 gap inside A0's 95% CI; not clipped)", y=1.0)
    fig.tight_layout()
    _save(fig, path, prereg)
    return True


def bundle(dirs: Sequence[Path], out_zip: Path) -> None:
    with zipfile.ZipFile(out_zip, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
        for root in dirs:
            for f in root.rglob("*"):
                if f.is_file():
                    z.write(f, f.relative_to(root.parent))


def default_result_patterns() -> list[str]:
    """Where benchmark CSVs normally live: <repo>/results, plus /kaggle/working/results when it exists."""
    pats = [str(REPO_ROOT / "results" / "benchmark_results*.csv")]
    if KAGGLE_ROOT.exists():
        pats.append(str(KAGGLE_ROOT / "results" / "benchmark_results*.csv"))
    return pats


def expand_files(patterns: Sequence[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Glob every pattern, drop duplicates that resolve to the same file (repo may BE /kaggle/working)."""
    seen: dict[Path, str] = {}
    for pat in patterns:
        for p in sorted(glob.glob(pat)):
            seen.setdefault(Path(p).resolve(), p)
    return sorted(seen.values())


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    out_base = KAGGLE_ROOT if KAGGLE_ROOT.exists() else REPO_ROOT / "results"
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Figures and LaTeX tables from benchmark CSVs.")
    ap.add_argument("--results", nargs="+", default=default_result_patterns())
    ap.add_argument("--fig_dir", default=str(out_base / "figures"))
    ap.add_argument("--out_dir", default=str(out_base / "analysis"))
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    files = expand_files(args.results)
    if not files:
        print(f"[report] ERROR: no CSV matches {args.results}")
        return 1
    df = pd.concat([pd.read_csv(f, dtype=str, keep_default_na=False) for f in files], ignore_index=True)
    rows = df.to_dict("records")
    fig_dir, out_dir = Path(args.fig_dir), Path(args.out_dir)
    try:
        res = A.run_analysis(rows, out_dir, plots=False)       # CSVs + .tex tables (same rules as the pre-registration)
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(f"[report] ERROR: {exc}")
        return 1
    values = A.collect_values(A.clean_rows(rows)[0])
    summ, comp, rec, prereg = res["summary"], res["comparison"], res["recovery"], res["prereg"]

    print(f"[report] {len(files)} file(s), {res['n_runs']} ok runs | pre-registration: {prereg.upper()}")
    for f in files:
        print(f"[report] using {f}")
    for w in res["warnings"]:
        print(f"[report] WARNING: {w}")
    figure_bars(values, summ, comp, "test_pr_auc", fig_dir / "fig1_pr_auc_bars.png", prereg)
    figure_bars(values, summ, comp, "test_efl_ratio", fig_dir / "fig2_efl_bars.png", prereg)
    figure_lines(values, summ, fig_dir / "fig3_delay_lines.png", prereg)
    figure_recovery(rec, fig_dir / "fig4_recovery.png", prereg)

    tab = pd.DataFrame([{"delay": c.delay, "method": A.method_label(c, latex=False).replace("\n", " "), "metric": m,
                         "n": int(s["n"]), "mean": round(s["mean"], 4), "ci_half": round(s["half"], 4),
                         "verdict": comp.get((c, m), {}).get("verdict", "")}
                        for (c, m), s in summ.items() if m in A.PRIMARY])
    print("\n" + tab.sort_values(["delay", "metric", "method"]).to_string(index=False))
    print("\n" + "=" * 30 + " LaTeX " + "=" * 30)
    for f in sorted(out_dir.glob("table_*.tex")):
        print(f"% ---- {f.name} ----")
        print(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    bundle([fig_dir, out_dir], out_base / "report_bundle.zip")
    print(f"[report] figures -> {fig_dir} | tables/CSV -> {out_dir} | zip -> {out_base / 'report_bundle.zip'}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================

FILE: scripts\prepare_data.py

================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""One-time data preparation: CSV -> split -> label delay -> phase graphs (.pt).

Pipeline
--------
1. load train_transaction.csv (+ train_identity.csv)       src/data/loader.py
2. temporal split 65/16/19                                  src/data/splitter.py
3. simulate label delay + P/N/U masks at t_train            src/data/delay_simulator.py
4. fit features / super-nodes on TRAIN, build HeteroData    src/graph/builder.py
5. validate every graph, save ``train.pt`` ``val.pt`` ``test.pt`` + metadata

Usage (from the repo root)::

    python scripts/prepare_data.py --data_dir data --out_dir data/processed \\
        --config configs/base.yaml --delay_config configs/delay_low.yaml

Output folder: ``<out_dir>/<delay_config stem>/`` (e.g. ``data/processed/delay_low``)
holding ``train.pt``, ``val.pt``, ``test.pt``, ``builder_state.pt``, ``meta.json``.
Graph structure and features do not depend on the delay regime, only the labels do,
so run it once per regime (two regimes ~ 2x the disk, roughly rows x features x 4 bytes
x 2.5 per regime because the cumulative val/test graphs repeat earlier rows).

Refuses to overwrite an existing output folder unless ``--overwrite`` is given.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import copy
import json
import platform
import shutil
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch_geometric
import yaml

from src.data.delay_simulator import DelayParams, assign_pnu_masks, pnu_diagnostics, simulate_delay
from src.data.loader import AMOUNT_COL, TARGET_COL, DataLoader, get_time_stats
from src.data.splitter import compute_split_boundaries, summarize_splits, temporal_split
from src.graph.builder import TXN, GraphBuilder, GraphConfig, validate_phase_graph


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Config helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def deep_update(base: dict[str, Any], overlay: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Recursively merge ``overlay`` into a copy of ``base``."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(base)
    for k, v in overlay.items():
        if isinstance(v, dict) and isinstance(out.get(k), dict):
            out[k] = deep_update(out[k], v)
        else:
            out[k] = copy.deepcopy(v)
    return out


def load_config(base_path: Path, delay_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    cfg = yaml.safe_load(base_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    delay = yaml.safe_load(delay_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    if "delay" not in delay:
        raise KeyError(f"{delay_path} must contain a top-level 'delay:' section.")
    return deep_update(cfg, delay)


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="One-time data preparation (CSV -> phase graphs).")
    p.add_argument("--data_dir", type=str, default=None, help="Folder with the IEEE-CIS CSVs (default: paths.data_dir).")
    p.add_argument("--out_dir", type=str, default=None, help="Root output folder (default: paths.processed_dir).")
    p.add_argument("--config", type=str, default=str(REPO_ROOT / "configs" / "base.yaml"))
    p.add_argument("--delay_config", type=str, default=str(REPO_ROOT / "configs" / "delay_low.yaml"))
    p.add_argument("--nrows", type=int, default=None, help="Read only the first N transaction rows (debug).")
    p.add_argument("--no_identity", action="store_true", help="Skip train_identity.csv (the device entity disappears).")
    p.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true", help="Replace an existing output folder.")
    return p.parse_args()


def _resolve(path: str | None, fallback: str) -> Path:
    p = Path(path if path is not None else fallback)
    return p if p.is_absolute() else Path.cwd() / p


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def apply_delay(frame: pd.DataFrame, t_train: float, delay_cfg: dict[str, Any], amount_ref: float | None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Delay + P/N/U masks for one split frame (draws depend on TransactionID only)."""
    params = dict(delay_cfg.get("params", {}))
    if float(params.get("amount_exponent", 0.0)) > 0 and params.get("amount_ref") is None:
        params["amount_ref"] = amount_ref  # median TRAIN amount, shared by all frames
    with_delay = simulate_delay(frame, t_train, mode=delay_cfg["mode"], params=params)
    return assign_pnu_masks(with_delay, t_train, maturity_window_days=delay_cfg.get("maturity_window_days"))


def _jsonable(obj: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {str(k): _jsonable(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        return [_jsonable(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):
        return int(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, (np.floating,)):
        return float(obj)
    return obj


def main() -> None:
    args = parse_args()
    cfg = load_config(Path(args.config), Path(args.delay_config))
    paths = cfg.get("paths", {})
    data_dir = _resolve(args.data_dir, paths.get("data_dir", "data"))
    out_root = _resolve(args.out_dir, paths.get("processed_dir", "data/processed"))
    out_dir = out_root / Path(args.delay_config).stem
    if out_dir.exists() and any(out_dir.iterdir()):
        if not args.overwrite:
            sys.exit(f"Output folder {out_dir} is not empty. Use --overwrite to replace it.")
        shutil.rmtree(out_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    t0 = time.time()

    # 1) load --------------------------------------------------------------
    loader = DataLoader(data_dir)
    print(f"[1/5] Loading {loader.transaction_path} ...")
    df = loader.load_transactions(nrows=args.nrows) if args.no_identity else loader.load_merged(nrows=args.nrows)
    print(f"      rows={len(df):,} cols={df.shape[1]} frauds={int(df[TARGET_COL].sum()):,} "
          f"({100 * df[TARGET_COL].mean():.3f}%)")
    print(f"      {get_time_stats(df).summary()}")

    # 2) split -------------------------------------------------------------
    s = cfg["split"]
    split_kw = dict(train_ratio=s["train_ratio"], val_ratio=s["val_ratio"], test_ratio=s["test_ratio"],
                    by=s.get("by", "time"), snap_to_day=s.get("snap_to_day", True))
    bounds = compute_split_boundaries(df, **split_kw)
    train_df, val_df, test_df = temporal_split(df, **split_kw)
    del df
    print(f"[2/5] Split: {bounds.summary()}")
    print(summarize_splits(train_df, val_df, test_df).round(3).to_string())

    # 3) delay + P/N/U -----------------------------------------------------
    delay_cfg = cfg["delay"]
    DelayParams.from_dict(delay_cfg.get("params")).validate(delay_cfg["mode"])  # fail early
    amount_ref = float(train_df[AMOUNT_COL].median())
    frames = [apply_delay(f, bounds.t_train, delay_cfg, amount_ref) for f in (train_df, val_df, test_df)]
    del train_df, val_df, test_df
    diag = pnu_diagnostics(frames[0])
    print(f"[3/5] Delay mode={delay_cfg['mode']} W={delay_cfg.get('maturity_window_days')} d | "
          f"train view: P={diag['n_P']:,} N={diag['n_N']:,} U={diag['n_U']:,} "
          f"({diag['share_U_%']:.1f}% U) | frauds hidden: {diag['n_fraud_hidden']:,} "
          f"({diag['hidden_fraud_%']:.1f}%) | frauds in N (label noise): {diag['n_fraud_in_N']:,}")

    # 4) fit + build ---------------------------------------------------------
    print("[4/5] Fitting encoder / super-nodes on TRAIN and building graphs ...")
    builder = GraphBuilder(GraphConfig.from_dict(cfg.get("graph"))).fit(*frames, bounds)
    for name, st in builder.entity_stats.items():
        print(f"      entity {name:<7} train entities={st['n_entities_train']:>8,.0f} "
              f"deg p50/p90/p99/max={st['deg_p50']:.0f}/{st['deg_p90']:.0f}/{st['deg_p99']:.0f}/{st['deg_max']:.0f} "
              f"cap={st['max_degree_cap']:.0f} -> removed {st['n_super_removed']:.0f} super-nodes "
              f"({st['train_edges_dropped_%']:.1f}% of train edges)")

    graph_summary: dict[str, Any] = {}
    cumulative: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    for phase, frame in zip(("train", "val", "test"), frames):
        cumulative.append(frame)
        data = builder.build(pd.concat(cumulative, ignore_index=True), phase)
        validate_phase_graph(data)  # fail fast: nothing leaky is ever written to disk
        path = out_dir / f"{phase}.pt"
        torch.save(data, path)
        size_mb = path.stat().st_size / 1e6
        graph_summary[phase] = {
            "n_transactions": int(data[TXN].num_nodes),
            "n_seeds": int(data[TXN].seed_mask.sum()),
            "entity_nodes": {nt: int(data[nt].num_nodes) for nt in data.node_types if nt != TXN},
            "edges": {"/".join(et): int(data[et].edge_index.size(1)) for et in data.edge_types},
            "file_mb": round(size_mb, 1),
        }
        print(f"      saved {path.name}: txn={graph_summary[phase]['n_transactions']:,} "
              f"seeds={graph_summary[phase]['n_seeds']:,} entities={graph_summary[phase]['entity_nodes']} "
              f"({size_mb:,.0f} MB)")
        del data

    # 5) metadata ------------------------------------------------------------
    torch.save(builder.state_dict(), out_dir / "builder_state.pt")
    meta = {
        "created_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "config": cfg,
        "boundaries": {"t_start": bounds.t_start, "t_train": bounds.t_train, "t_val": bounds.t_val, "t_end": bounds.t_end,
                       "t_train_day": bounds.t_train_day, "t_val_day": bounds.t_val_day},
        "amount_ref_train_median": amount_ref,
        "pnu_train_diagnostics": diag,
        "feature_dim": len(builder.encoder.feature_names),
        "dropped_missing_cols": getattr(builder.encoder, "dropped_missing_cols", {}),
        "entity_stats": builder.entity_stats,
        "graphs": graph_summary,
        "versions": {"python": platform.python_version(), "torch": torch.__version__,
                     "torch_geometric": torch_geometric.__version__, "pandas": pd.__version__, "numpy": np.__version__},
    }
    (out_dir / "meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(_jsonable(meta), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[5/5] Wrote builder_state.pt and meta.json to {out_dir}  (total {time.time() - t0:,.0f}s)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================

FILE: scripts\profile_data.py

================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""E0 pre-flight: profile the IEEE-CIS time axis, weekly fraud rate and amounts.

Usage (from the repo root)::

    python scripts/profile_data.py --data_dir data/ieee-cis
    python scripts/profile_data.py --data_dir data/ieee-cis --nrows 50000   # quick debug

Outputs
-------
* stdout: min/max day, weekly table, proposed 65/16/19 split, amount stats.
* ``results/eda/weekly_fraud_dist.png`` (folder is created if missing).
* ``results/eda/weekly_stats.csv``.

Only the four columns needed for profiling are read. The weekly view covers
the whole labelled timeline to expose drift; it is for *looking*, never for
tuning on Val/Test labels.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:  # make `src` importable when run as a script
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

import matplotlib

matplotlib.use("Agg")  # headless (Kaggle / servers)
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from src.data.loader import (
    AMOUNT_COL,
    ID_COL,
    SECONDS_PER_DAY,
    SECONDS_PER_WEEK,
    TARGET_COL,
    TIME_COL,
    DataLoader,
    TimeStats,
    get_time_stats,
)
from src.data.splitter import SplitBoundaries, compute_split_boundaries, summarize_splits, temporal_split

PROFILE_COLS = [ID_COL, TARGET_COL, TIME_COL, AMOUNT_COL]
DRIFT_Z = 3.0          # |z| above this flags a week (exploratory, not a test)
MIN_WEEK_TXN = 500     # ignore tiny weeks for the drift flag


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="E0 data profiling for IEEE-CIS.")
    p.add_argument("--data_dir", type=str, default="data", help="Folder with train_transaction.csv.")
    p.add_argument("--out_dir", type=str, default=str(REPO_ROOT / "results" / "eda"),
                   help="Where to save plots/tables (default: <repo>/results/eda).")
    p.add_argument("--nrows", type=int, default=None, help="Read only the first N rows (debug).")
    return p.parse_args()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def weekly_stats(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Transactions, fraud count and fraud rate per (relative) 7-day bucket."""
    tmp = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "week": (df[TIME_COL] // SECONDS_PER_WEEK).astype(int),
            "day": (df[TIME_COL] // SECONDS_PER_DAY).astype(int),
            "fraud": df[TARGET_COL].astype(int),
        }
    )
    agg = tmp.groupby("week").agg(
        n_txn=("fraud", "size"), n_fraud=("fraud", "sum"), days_observed=("day", "nunique")
    )
    agg["fraud_rate"] = agg["n_fraud"] / agg["n_txn"]
    agg["is_partial"] = agg["days_observed"] < 7
    p0 = float(df[TARGET_COL].mean())
    se = np.sqrt(p0 * (1.0 - p0) / agg["n_txn"])
    agg["z_vs_pooled"] = (agg["fraud_rate"] - p0) / se
    agg["drift_flag"] = (agg["z_vs_pooled"].abs() > DRIFT_Z) & (agg["n_txn"] >= MIN_WEEK_TXN) & ~agg["is_partial"]
    return agg


def print_time_stats(ts: TimeStats) -> None:
    print("=" * 78)
    print("1) TIME AXIS (TransactionDT is a relative offset in seconds, not a calendar date)")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"  min : {ts.min_seconds:>12,.0f} s = {ts.min_day:8.3f} days = {ts.min_day / 7:6.2f} weeks")
    print(f"  max : {ts.max_seconds:>12,.0f} s = {ts.max_day:8.3f} days = {ts.max_day / 7:6.2f} weeks")
    print(f"  span: {ts.span_days:.3f} days | day buckets touched: {ts.n_calendar_days} "
          f"| week buckets touched: {ts.n_weeks}")


def print_weekly(weekly: pd.DataFrame, pooled_rate: float) -> None:
    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print(f"2) WEEKLY DISTRIBUTION (pooled fraud rate = {100 * pooled_rate:.3f}%)")
    print("=" * 78)
    view = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "days": [f"{w * 7}-{w * 7 + 6}" for w in weekly.index],
            "n_txn": weekly["n_txn"],
            "n_fraud": weekly["n_fraud"],
            "fraud_%": (100 * weekly["fraud_rate"]).round(3),
            "z": weekly["z_vs_pooled"].round(1),
            "note": np.where(weekly["is_partial"], "partial", "")
            + np.where(weekly["drift_flag"], " DRIFT?", ""),
        },
        index=weekly.index.rename("week"),
    )
    print(view.to_string())
    flagged = weekly.index[weekly["drift_flag"]].tolist()
    print(f"\n  Weeks with |z|>{DRIFT_Z:g} (n>={MIN_WEEK_TXN}, full weeks): {flagged or 'none'} "
          "(exploratory flag, not a drift test)")


def plot_weekly(weekly: pd.DataFrame, pooled_rate: float, b: SplitBoundaries, path: Path) -> None:
    """Two panels: weekly volume and weekly fraud rate, with split boundaries."""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    x = weekly.index.to_numpy()
    fig, (ax1, ax2) = plt.subplots(2, 1, figsize=(11, 7), sharex=True, gridspec_kw={"height_ratios": [1, 1.2]})

    colors = np.where(weekly["is_partial"], "#b0b7c3", "#4c78a8")
    ax1.bar(x, weekly["n_txn"], color=colors, edgecolor="none")
    ax1.set_ylabel("Transactions / week")

    ax2.plot(x, 100 * weekly["fraud_rate"], marker="o", ms=4, lw=1.4, color="#e45756", label="weekly fraud rate")
    ax2.axhline(100 * pooled_rate, ls="--", lw=1, color="black", label=f"pooled {100 * pooled_rate:.2f}%")
    flagged = weekly[weekly["drift_flag"]]
    if len(flagged):
        ax2.scatter(flagged.index, 100 * flagged["fraud_rate"], s=70, facecolors="none",
                    edgecolors="black", label=f"|z|>{DRIFT_Z:g}")
    ax2.set_ylabel("Fraud rate (%)")
    ax2.set_xlabel("Relative week index (7-day buckets of TransactionDT)")

    # bar for week w covers days [7w, 7w+7) and is centred at x=w -> day d sits at d/7 - 0.5
    for label, day in (("T_train", b.t_train_day), ("T_val", b.t_val_day)):
        xpos = day / 7.0 - 0.5
        for ax in (ax1, ax2):
            ax.axvline(xpos, color="#54a24b", ls=":", lw=1.8)
        ax1.text(xpos, 1.02, f"{label} (day {day:.0f})", color="#2f7d32", fontsize=8, ha="center",
                 va="bottom", transform=ax1.get_xaxis_transform())
    ax1.set_title("IEEE-CIS: weekly volume and fraud rate (grey = partial week)", pad=22)
    handles, labels = ax2.get_legend_handles_labels()
    fig.legend(handles, labels, loc="lower center", ncol=len(labels), fontsize=8, frameon=False)
    fig.tight_layout(rect=(0, 0.04, 1, 1))
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close(fig)


def print_split(df: pd.DataFrame, b: SplitBoundaries) -> None:
    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print("3) PROPOSED TEMPORAL SPLIT 65/16/19 (ratios over the time span, day-aligned)")
    print("=" * 78)
    print(f"  {b.summary()}")
    train_df, val_df, test_df = temporal_split(df)
    summary = summarize_splits(train_df, val_df, test_df)
    print(summary.round(3).to_string())
    total_fraud_amt = summary["fraud_amount"].sum()
    print(f"\n  Total fraud amount = {total_fraud_amt:,.2f} (EFL of a model that flags nothing).")
    low = summary[summary["n_fraud"] < 1000]
    if len(low):
        print(f"  WARNING: split(s) with < 1000 frauds -> wide CIs: {low.index.tolist()}")


def print_amount_stats(df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    print("\n" + "=" * 78)
    print(f"4) {AMOUNT_COL} STATISTICS (basis for the amount-dependent delay and EFL)")
    print("=" * 78)
    qs = [0.9, 0.95, 0.99]

    def describe(s: pd.Series) -> dict[str, float]:
        out = {"count": float(s.count()), "min": s.min(), "mean": s.mean(), "median": s.median(), "std": s.std()}
        out.update({f"p{int(q * 100)}": s.quantile(q) for q in qs})
        out["max"] = s.max()
        return out

    table = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "all": describe(df[AMOUNT_COL]),
            "legit": describe(df.loc[df[TARGET_COL] == 0, AMOUNT_COL]),
            "fraud": describe(df.loc[df[TARGET_COL] == 1, AMOUNT_COL]),
        }
    )
    print(table.round(2).to_string())
    p99 = df[AMOUNT_COL].quantile(0.99)
    top = df[AMOUNT_COL] >= p99
    fraud_amt = df.loc[df[TARGET_COL] == 1, AMOUNT_COL]
    print(f"\n  Skewness: {df[AMOUNT_COL].skew():.2f} (heavy right tail -> consider log scale / amount_ref=median).")
    print(f"  Top 1% of transactions (>= {p99:,.2f}) hold {100 * df.loc[top, AMOUNT_COL].sum() / df[AMOUNT_COL].sum():.1f}% "
          f"of total amount and {100 * df.loc[top & (df[TARGET_COL] == 1), AMOUNT_COL].sum() / fraud_amt.sum():.1f}% of fraud amount.")
    print(f"  Suggested amount_ref (median) = {df[AMOUNT_COL].median():,.2f}")


def main() -> None:
    args = parse_args()
    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    loader = DataLoader(args.data_dir)
    print(f"Loading {loader.transaction_path} (cols: {PROFILE_COLS}) ...")
    df = loader.load_transactions(usecols=PROFILE_COLS, nrows=args.nrows)
    print(f"  rows={len(df):,} | frauds={int(df[TARGET_COL].sum()):,} "
          f"({100 * df[TARGET_COL].mean():.3f}%)")

    ts = get_time_stats(df)
    print_time_stats(ts)

    weekly = weekly_stats(df)
    pooled = float(df[TARGET_COL].mean())
    print_weekly(weekly, pooled)
    weekly.to_csv(out_dir / "weekly_stats.csv")

    b = compute_split_boundaries(df)
    print_split(df, b)
    print_amount_stats(df)

    png = out_dir / "weekly_fraud_dist.png"
    plot_weekly(weekly, pooled, b, png)
    print(f"\nSaved: {png}\nSaved: {out_dir / 'weekly_stats.csv'}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


================================================================================

FILE: scripts\profile_epoch.py

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""E0 profiling on the real graph: seconds per training/validation epoch and peak VRAM.

    python scripts/profile_epoch.py --processed_dir data/processed/delay_high --backbone sage --max_batches 60

It times a SAMPLE of ``--max_batches`` training batches after ``--warmup_batches`` untimed ones and
EXTRAPOLATES to a full epoch (mean time per batch x number of batches). Fetch time (temporal disjoint
sampling, includes pyg-lib and collation) is separated from step time (transfer + forward + backward + Adam).
Peak VRAM is the CUDA peak of the sampled training steps. On CPU there is no VRAM figure and the timings
are NOT Kaggle timings: run it on the Kaggle GPU for the real E0 numbers.

Planning table: runs x epochs x (train + val epoch). Early stopping makes real runs shorter, so it is an
upper bound; no weekly GPU quota is assumed (pass --weekly_gpu_hours if you know yours).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import copy
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

import numpy as np
import torch
import yaml

from src.data.dataset import load_phase_datasets, read_meta
from src.graph.builder import TXN
from src.models.classifier import FraudClassifier, entity_vocab_sizes_from_meta
from src.train.trainer import TrainConfig, build_training_loss, compute_batch_loss
from src.train.utils import get_device, peak_memory_mb, reset_peak_memory, set_seed, sync, write_json


def _bool(v: str) -> bool:
    if v.lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "y"):
        return True
    if v.lower() in ("0", "false", "no", "n"):
        return False
    raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("expected true/false")


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Profile one epoch (time + VRAM) on the real graph.")
    p.add_argument("--processed_dir", default="data/processed/delay_high")
    p.add_argument("--config", default=str(REPO_ROOT / "configs" / "base.yaml"))
    p.add_argument("--backbone", choices=["sage", "gat"], default="sage")
    p.add_argument("--use_temporal", type=_bool, default=True, help="false = GRU ablation (SAGE/GAT + MLP)")
    p.add_argument("--lp", type=_bool, default=False, help="enable LP features (in_dim 457 -> 461)")
    p.add_argument("--loss", choices=["naive_bce", "maturity_mask", "delay_ipw", "tw_nndf"], default="naive_bce")
    p.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=None)
    p.add_argument("--max_batches", type=int, default=60, help="timed training batches (<= 0: the whole epoch)")
    p.add_argument("--warmup_batches", type=int, default=5)
    p.add_argument("--eval_batches", type=int, default=10)
    p.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=None, help="epochs per run for the planning table (default: train.epochs)")
    p.add_argument("--runs", type=int, nargs="*", default=[72, 90], help="run counts for the planning table")
    p.add_argument("--weekly_gpu_hours", type=float, default=None)
    p.add_argument("--device", default="auto")
    p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    p.add_argument("--out", default=None)
    return p.parse_args()


def _stats(x: list[float]) -> dict[str, float]:
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    return {"mean": float(a.mean()), "median": float(np.median(a)), "p90": float(np.percentile(a, 90))}


def main() -> int:
    args = parse_args()
    cfg = yaml.safe_load(Path(args.config).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    pdir = Path(args.processed_dir)
    meta = read_meta(pdir)
    device = get_device(args.device)
    set_seed(args.seed)

    sampler_cfg = dict(cfg["sampler"])
    if args.batch_size:
        sampler_cfg["batch_size"] = args.batch_size
    features_cfg = copy.deepcopy(dict(cfg.get("features", {})))
    features_cfg["lp_features"] = {**(features_cfg.get("lp_features") or {}), "enabled": args.lp}
    dsets = load_phase_datasets(pdir, sampler_cfg, phases=("train", "val"), features_cfg=features_cfg)
    train_ds, val_ds = dsets["train"], dsets["val"]

    model_cfg = dict(cfg["model"])
    model_cfg["use_temporal"] = args.use_temporal
    model = FraudClassifier.from_config(model_cfg, in_dim=train_ds.model_in_dim,
                                        entity_vocab_sizes=entity_vocab_sizes_from_meta(meta),
                                        conv_type=args.backbone).to(device)
    tcfg = TrainConfig.from_cfg(cfg, loss=args.loss)
    loss_fn, info = build_training_loss(tcfg, train_ds, meta)
    loss_fn = loss_fn.to(device)
    opt = torch.optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=tcfg.lr, weight_decay=tcfg.weight_decay)
    label_cut = float(train_ds.data.label_cut)

    gpu = torch.cuda.get_device_name(device) if device.type == "cuda" else "CPU"
    print(f"device={gpu} | backbone={args.backbone} temporal={args.use_temporal} lp={args.lp} loss={args.loss} | "
          f"in_dim={train_ds.model_in_dim} params={model.num_parameters():,} | "
          f"train batches/epoch={len(train_ds)} (batch_size={sampler_cfg['batch_size']}) val batches={len(val_ds)}")

    # ---- training steps -------------------------------------------------------------- #
    model.train()
    limit = len(train_ds) if args.max_batches <= 0 else args.warmup_batches + args.max_batches
    t_fetch, t_step, nodes = [], [], []
    reset_peak_memory(device)
    it = iter(train_ds)
    for i in range(limit):
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            batch = next(it)
        except StopIteration:
            break
        t1 = time.perf_counter()
        batch = batch.to(device)
        loss, _ = compute_batch_loss(model, loss_fn, batch, label_cut, False)
        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        loss.backward()
        opt.step()
        sync(device)
        t2 = time.perf_counter()
        if i >= args.warmup_batches:
            t_fetch.append(t1 - t0)
            t_step.append(t2 - t1)
            nodes.append(int(batch[TXN].x.size(0)))
    if not t_step:
        print("FAIL: no timed batches (raise --max_batches or lower --warmup_batches).")
        return 1
    peak_train = peak_memory_mb(device)
    total = [a + b for a, b in zip(t_fetch, t_step)]
    train_epoch_s = float(np.mean(total)) * len(train_ds)

    # ---- validation steps -------------------------------------------------------------- #
    model.eval()
    t_eval = []
    reset_peak_memory(device)
    with torch.no_grad():
        it = iter(val_ds)
        for i in range(args.eval_batches):
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            try:
                batch = next(it)
            except StopIteration:
                break
            batch = batch.to(device)
            model(batch)
            sync(device)
            t_eval.append(time.perf_counter() - t0)
    peak_eval = peak_memory_mb(device)
    val_epoch_s = float(np.mean(t_eval)) * len(val_ds) if t_eval else float("nan")

    epochs = args.epochs or int(cfg["train"]["epochs"])
    per_epoch = train_epoch_s + val_epoch_s
    plan = {str(r): {"hours_upper_bound": r * epochs * per_epoch / 3600.0} for r in args.runs}
    if args.weekly_gpu_hours:
        for r in args.runs:
            plan[str(r)]["weekly_quota_fraction"] = plan[str(r)]["hours_upper_bound"] / args.weekly_gpu_hours

    result = {
        "device": gpu, "backbone": args.backbone, "use_temporal": args.use_temporal, "lp": args.lp,
        "loss": args.loss, "in_dim": train_ds.model_in_dim, "n_params": model.num_parameters(),
        "batch_size": sampler_cfg["batch_size"], "n_train_batches": len(train_ds), "n_val_batches": len(val_ds),
        "timed_batches": len(t_step), "fetch_sec_per_batch": _stats(t_fetch), "step_sec_per_batch": _stats(t_step),
        "fetch_share": float(np.sum(t_fetch) / np.sum(total)),
        "nodes_per_batch": _stats([float(n) for n in nodes]),
        "train_epoch_sec_est": train_epoch_s, "val_epoch_sec_est": val_epoch_s,
        "peak_vram_train_mb": peak_train, "peak_vram_eval_mb": peak_eval,
        "epochs_assumed": epochs, "plan": plan, "loss_info": info,
        "note": "sampled batches extrapolated to a full epoch; upper bound (no early stopping); not Kaggle numbers on CPU",
    }
    out = Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "results" / "profile" / (
        f"epoch_{pdir.name}_{args.backbone}_{'gru' if args.use_temporal else 'nogru'}_{'lp' if args.lp else 'nolp'}.json")
    write_json(out, result)

    print(f"\ntimed batches={len(t_step)} | fetch {np.mean(t_fetch) * 1e3:.0f} ms + step {np.mean(t_step) * 1e3:.0f} ms "
          f"per batch (fetch share {100 * result['fetch_share']:.0f}%) | nodes/batch ~{np.mean(nodes):,.0f}")
    print(f"train epoch ~ {train_epoch_s / 60:.1f} min | val epoch ~ {val_epoch_s / 60:.1f} min")
    print("peak VRAM (train): " + ("n/a (CPU)" if peak_train is None else
          f"{peak_train['allocated']:,.0f} MB allocated / {peak_train['reserved']:,.0f} MB reserved"))
    for r, v in plan.items():
        q = f" = {100 * v['weekly_quota_fraction']:.0f}% of the weekly quota" if "weekly_quota_fraction" in v else ""
        print(f"plan: {r} runs x {epochs} epochs ~ {v['hours_upper_bound']:.1f} GPU-hours (upper bound){q}")
    print(f"saved {out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

FILE: scripts\run_benchmark.py

# =====================================================================
# FILE: scripts/run_benchmark.py
# TRẠNG THÁI: ĐÃ SỬA (xóa định nghĩa lặp apply_prior_override; logic giữ nguyên)
# =====================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Benchmark runner with auto-resume.

    python scripts/run_benchmark.py --dry_run                       # plan + counts, nothing is trained
    python scripts/run_benchmark.py --max_hours 10 --device auto    # real run; re-run the same command to resume

Design
------
* The matrix comes from configs/benchmark.yaml (groups with ``enabled`` flags). It is NOT frozen: every result row
  carries ``prereg_status`` (src.train.utils.PREREG_STATUS).
* One CSV row is appended (flush + fsync) after each finished run. Resume = skip every run_id whose row has
  status == "ok". A run killed mid-way leaves no row and is simply re-run.
* Failed runs are written with status == "failed" and are NOT retried unless --retry_failed (a deterministic bug
  could otherwise burn hours on every restart).
* ``cfg_hash`` (sampler/model/train/eval/loss/features + lam_by_delay) is stored in every row; resuming with a
  different hash is refused unless --allow_config_change, so two protocols never mix in one CSV.
* --max_hours stops BEFORE a run that would not fit (estimate = slowest finished run of this invocation, or
  --assume_run_minutes for the first one). Exit code 0, re-run to continue.
* Seeds [42, 43, 44]: GPU runs are not bit-reproducible and the pyg-lib sampler seeding is unverified (KHÔNG CHẮC).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import copy
import csv
import gc
import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import sys
import time
import traceback
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Mapping, Sequence

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

import yaml

from src.train.utils import PREREG_STATUS, deep_update, jsonable, load_config, make_run_id, write_json

LOSSES = ("naive_bce", "maturity_mask", "delay_ipw", "tw_nndf")
HASHED_SECTIONS = ("sampler", "model", "train", "eval", "loss", "features")

RESULT_COLUMNS: list[str] = [
    "run_id", "status", "group", "delay", "loss", "oracle", "backbone", "use_temporal", "lp_features", "seed",
    "prior_override", "in_dim", "n_params", "best_epoch", "epochs_run",
    "val_pr_auc", "val_roc_auc", "test_pr_auc", "test_roc_auc", "test_recall_at_fpr",
    "test_recall_at_valthr", "test_fpr_at_valthr", "test_efl", "test_efl_ratio",
    "lam", "nndf_prior", "nndf_prior_estimated", "diag_true_fraud_rate_in_U",
    "nndf_clamped_best", "nndf_clamped_mean", "nndf_neg_risk_raw_best", "nndf_pos_risk_best", "n_empty_batches_total",
    "train_seconds", "wall_seconds", "peak_vram_alloc_mb", "prereg_status", "cfg_hash", "meta_sha",
    "finished_utc", "error",
]

DEFAULT_RESULTS = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "benchmark_results.csv"
DEFAULT_DETAILS = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "runs"

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Plan
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunSpec:
    group: str
    delay: str          # directory name under data/processed/
    loss: str
    oracle: bool
    backbone: str
    use_temporal: bool
    lp: bool
    seed: int
    prior: float | None = None

    @property
    def label(self) -> str:
        return "oracle" if self.oracle else self.loss

    @property
    def run_id(self) -> str:
        rid = make_run_id(self.delay, self.label, self.backbone, self.use_temporal, self.lp, self.seed)
        return rid if self.prior is None else f"{rid}_pi{self.prior:g}"


def build_plan(bench: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[RunSpec]:
    """Expand the benchmark spec into an ordered list of runs; duplicate run_ids are an error."""
    seeds = [int(s) for s in bench["seeds"]]
    default_delays = list(bench["delays"])
    default_backbone = str(bench.get("backbone", "sage"))
    plan: list[RunSpec] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for gname, g in bench["groups"].items():
        if not g.get("enabled", True):
            continue
        oracle = bool(g.get("oracle", False))
        losses = ["naive_bce"] if oracle else list(g["losses"])
        bad = [x for x in losses if x not in LOSSES]
        if bad:
            raise ValueError(f"group '{gname}': unknown loss {bad}; allowed {LOSSES}")
        prior_map = g.get("priors_by_delay")
        for delay in g.get("delays", default_delays):
            priors: list[float | None] = [None] if prior_map is None else list(prior_map.get(delay, []))
            for loss in losses:
                for prior in priors:
                    if prior is not None and (loss != "tw_nndf" or oracle):
                        raise ValueError(f"group '{gname}': priors_by_delay only makes sense for tw_nndf")
                    for seed in seeds:
                        spec = RunSpec(group=gname, delay=delay, loss=loss, oracle=oracle,
                                       backbone=str(g.get("backbone", default_backbone)),
                                       use_temporal=bool(g.get("use_temporal", True)), lp=bool(g.get("lp", False)),
                                       seed=seed, prior=None if prior is None else float(prior))
                        if spec.run_id in seen:
                            raise ValueError(f"duplicate run_id {spec.run_id} (group '{gname}')")
                        seen.add(spec.run_id)
                        plan.append(spec)
    return plan


def resolve_cfg(base_cfg: Mapping[str, Any], bench: Mapping[str, Any], spec: RunSpec, allow_default_lam: bool) -> dict:
    """Per-run config. delay_ipw gets its lambda from ``lam_by_delay`` ONLY (base.yaml's lam is ignored)."""
    cfg = copy.deepcopy(dict(base_cfg))
    if spec.loss == "delay_ipw" and not spec.oracle:
        lam = (bench.get("lam_by_delay") or {}).get(spec.delay)
        if lam is None and not allow_default_lam:
            raise KeyError(f"lam_by_delay['{spec.delay}'] is not set in benchmark.yaml (decision C). "
                           f"Set lambda = ln2 / median_delay_days of that delay, or pass --allow_default_lam (smoke only).")
        loss_cfg = cfg.setdefault("loss", {})
        d = dict(loss_cfg.get("delay_ipw") or {})
        d.pop("lam", None)
        if lam is not None:
            d["lam"] = float(lam)
        loss_cfg["delay_ipw"] = d
    return cfg


def apply_prior_override(plan: Sequence[RunSpec], prior: float) -> list[RunSpec]:
    """--prior: keep ONLY tw_nndf runs and pin their prior (run_id gets the ``_pi<value>`` suffix)."""
    if not 0.0 < prior < 1.0:
        raise ValueError("--prior must be in (0, 1).")
    out: list[RunSpec] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for s in plan:
        if s.loss != "tw_nndf" or s.oracle:
            continue
        t = replace(s, group="prior_cli", prior=float(prior))
        if t.run_id not in seen:                      # e.g. main + prior_sensitivity would collide
            seen.add(t.run_id)
            out.append(t)
    return out


def hash_config(base_cfg: Mapping[str, Any], bench: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    payload = {k: base_cfg.get(k) for k in HASHED_SECTIONS}
    payload["lam_by_delay"] = bench.get("lam_by_delay")
    payload["backbone"] = bench.get("backbone")
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(jsonable(payload), sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Results file
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def read_rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    if not path.exists() or path.stat().st_size == 0:
        return []
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        if reader.fieldnames != RESULT_COLUMNS:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{path}: header differs from RESULT_COLUMNS (older version?). Move it aside or migrate it.")
        return list(reader)


def append_row(path: Path, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Append one row and fsync. A truncated last line (killed session) is terminated first."""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    new = (not path.exists()) or path.stat().st_size == 0
    needs_newline = False
    if not new:
        with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            header = next(csv.reader(f), None)
        if header != RESULT_COLUMNS:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{path}: header differs from RESULT_COLUMNS; refusing to append.")
        with path.open("rb") as fb:
            fb.seek(-1, os.SEEK_END)
            needs_newline = fb.read(1) != b"\n"
    with path.open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        if needs_newline:
            f.write("\n")
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=RESULT_COLUMNS, extrasaction="ignore", restval="")
        if new:
            w.writeheader()
        w.writerow(jsonable(dict(row)))
        f.flush()
        os.fsync(f.fileno())


def _best_history_row(history: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]], best_epoch: int) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    for h in history:
        if int(h.get("epoch", -1)) == int(best_epoch):
            return h
    return {}


def _meta_sha(pdir: Path) -> str:
    p = pdir / "meta.json"
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()[:12] if p.exists() else ""


def _now_utc() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")


def finalize_row(spec: RunSpec, res: Mapping[str, Any], *, wall: float, cfg_hash: str, meta_sha: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """run_one() row + benchmark fields + tw_nndf diagnostics at the best epoch (the model that is evaluated)."""
    row = dict(res["row"])
    det = res["details"]
    info, hist = det.get("info", {}), det.get("fit", {}).get("history", [])
    row.update(run_id=spec.run_id, status="ok", group=spec.group, prior_override=spec.prior, wall_seconds=wall,
               cfg_hash=cfg_hash, meta_sha=meta_sha, finished_utc=_now_utc(), error="",
               lam=info.get("lam"), nndf_prior=info.get("prior"), nndf_prior_estimated=info.get("prior_estimated"),
               diag_true_fraud_rate_in_U=info.get("diag_true_fraud_rate_in_U"))
    if hist and "nndf_clamped_rate" in hist[0]:
        h = _best_history_row(hist, int(row["best_epoch"]))
        row["nndf_clamped_best"] = h.get("nndf_clamped_rate")
        row["nndf_neg_risk_raw_best"] = h.get("nndf_neg_risk_raw")
        row["nndf_pos_risk_best"] = h.get("nndf_pos_risk")
        row["nndf_clamped_mean"] = sum(float(x["nndf_clamped_rate"]) for x in hist) / len(hist)
        row["n_empty_batches_total"] = sum(float(x.get("n_empty_batches", 0.0)) for x in hist)
    return row


def failed_row(spec: RunSpec, exc: BaseException, *, wall: float, cfg_hash: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    msg = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}".replace("\n", " ")[:300]
    return {"run_id": spec.run_id, "status": "failed", "group": spec.group, "delay": spec.delay, "loss": spec.label,
            "oracle": spec.oracle, "backbone": spec.backbone, "use_temporal": spec.use_temporal,
            "lp_features": spec.lp, "seed": spec.seed, "prior_override": spec.prior, "wall_seconds": wall,
            "prereg_status": PREREG_STATUS, "cfg_hash": cfg_hash, "finished_utc": _now_utc(), "error": msg}


def _free_memory() -> None:
    gc.collect()
    try:
        import torch
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - cleanup must never kill the benchmark loop
        pass


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Execution
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def execute(
    plan: Sequence[RunSpec], base_cfg: Mapping[str, Any], bench: Mapping[str, Any], *,
    processed_root: Path, results_path: Path, details_dir: Path, run_fn: Callable[..., dict[str, Any]],
    device: str = "auto", max_hours: float | None = None, assume_run_seconds: float | None = None,
    retry_failed: bool = False, allow_default_lam: bool = False, allow_config_change: bool = False,
    max_train_batches: int | None = None, clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
    log: Callable[[str], None] = print,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    cfg_hash = hash_config(base_cfg, bench)
    rows = read_rows(results_path)
    stale = {r["cfg_hash"] for r in rows if r.get("status") == "ok" and r.get("cfg_hash")} - {cfg_hash}
    if stale and not allow_config_change:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{results_path} holds runs with config hash {sorted(stale)} but the current one is "
                           f"{cfg_hash}. Use another --results file, or --allow_config_change if you accept mixing.")
    ok = {r["run_id"] for r in rows if r.get("status") == "ok"}
    failed = {r["run_id"] for r in rows if r.get("status") == "failed"} - ok
    todo = [s for s in plan if s.run_id not in ok and (retry_failed or s.run_id not in failed)]
    n_skipped_failed = sum(1 for s in plan if s.run_id in failed and not retry_failed)
    cfgs = {s.run_id: resolve_cfg(base_cfg, bench, s, allow_default_lam) for s in todo}  # fail NOW, not after hours

    log(f"[benchmark] plan={len(plan)} done={len([s for s in plan if s.run_id in ok])} "
        f"to_run={len(todo)} skipped_failed={n_skipped_failed} cfg_hash={cfg_hash} prereg={PREREG_STATUS}")
    t_start = clock()
    walls: list[float] = [float(assume_run_seconds)] if assume_run_seconds else []
    n_ok = n_fail = 0
    stopped = False
    for i, spec in enumerate(todo, 1):
        if max_hours is not None and walls and (clock() - t_start) + max(walls) > max_hours * 3600.0:
            stopped = True
            log(f"[benchmark] time budget: the next run (~{max(walls) / 60:.0f} min) would not fit in {max_hours} h. "
                f"Stopping; re-run the same command to resume.")
            break
        log(f"[benchmark] ({i}/{len(todo)}) {spec.run_id}")
        pdir = processed_root / spec.delay
        extra = {} if spec.prior is None else {"prior": spec.prior}
        t0 = clock()
        try:
            res = run_fn(cfgs[spec.run_id], pdir, loss=spec.loss, oracle=spec.oracle, backbone=spec.backbone,
                         use_temporal=spec.use_temporal, lp=spec.lp, seed=spec.seed, device=device,
                         save_dir=None, max_train_batches=max_train_batches, **extra)
            wall = clock() - t0
            row = finalize_row(spec, res, wall=wall, cfg_hash=cfg_hash, meta_sha=_meta_sha(pdir))
            write_json(details_dir / f"{spec.run_id}.json", res["details"])
            walls.append(wall)
            n_ok += 1
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - includes CUDA OOM; one bad run must not stop the matrix
            wall = clock() - t0
            log(traceback.format_exc())
            row = failed_row(spec, exc, wall=wall, cfg_hash=cfg_hash)
            n_fail += 1
        append_row(results_path, row)
        _free_memory()
    return {"n_todo": len(todo), "n_ok": n_ok, "n_failed": n_fail, "n_skipped_failed": n_skipped_failed,
            "n_remaining": len(todo) - n_ok - n_fail, "stopped_for_budget": stopped, "cfg_hash": cfg_hash}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# CLI
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def summarize_plan(plan: Sequence[RunSpec]) -> str:
    by_group: dict[str, int] = {}
    by_delay: dict[str, int] = {}
    for s in plan:
        by_group[s.group] = by_group.get(s.group, 0) + 1
        by_delay[s.delay] = by_delay.get(s.delay, 0) + 1
    return (f"{len(plan)} runs | by group: {by_group} | by delay: {by_delay} | "
            f"seeds: {sorted({s.seed for s in plan})}")


def parse_args(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run the DelayGraphBench matrix with auto-resume.")
    p.add_argument("--config", default=str(REPO_ROOT / "configs" / "base.yaml"))
    p.add_argument("--benchmark", default=str(REPO_ROOT / "configs" / "benchmark.yaml"))
    p.add_argument("--processed_root", default=str(REPO_ROOT / "data" / "processed"))
    p.add_argument("--results", default=str(REPO_ROOT / "results" / "benchmark_results.csv"))
    p.add_argument("--details_dir", default=str(REPO_ROOT / "results" / "runs"))
    p.add_argument("--seed_results", default=None,
                   help="CSV from a previous session (e.g. a Kaggle dataset); copied to --results if that file is absent")
    p.add_argument("--device", default="auto")
    p.add_argument("--max_hours", type=float, default=None)
    p.add_argument("--assume_run_minutes", type=float, default=None, help="duration guess for the first run of a session")
    p.add_argument("--only_group", nargs="*", default=None)
    p.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="*", default=None, help="override benchmark.seeds (smoke tests)")
    p.add_argument("--delays", nargs="*", default=None, help="override benchmark.delays (smoke tests)")
    p.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None)
    p.add_argument("--retry_failed", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--allow_default_lam", action="store_true", help="use the W/2 default lambda (smoke only)")
    p.add_argument("--allow_config_change", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--prior", type=float, default=None,
                   help="tw_nndf prior override: keeps ONLY tw_nndf runs, writes to results/prior_diag/ (diagnostic)")
    p.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="2 epochs x 20 batches, separate results file")
    p.add_argument("--dry_run", action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--est_epoch_minutes", type=float, default=None, help="(train+val) epoch minutes for an hours bound")
    return p.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None, run_fn: Callable[..., dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> int:
    args = parse_args(argv)
    base_cfg = load_config(args.config)
    bench = copy.deepcopy(yaml.safe_load(Path(args.benchmark).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["benchmark"])
    if args.seeds:
        bench["seeds"] = args.seeds
    if args.delays:
        bench["delays"] = args.delays
    plan = build_plan(bench)
    if args.only_group:
        plan = [s for s in plan if s.group in args.only_group]
    if args.prior is not None:
        plan = apply_prior_override(plan, args.prior)
        if not plan:
            print("[benchmark] ERROR: --prior left no tw_nndf run in the selected plan.")
            return 2
    if args.limit:
        plan = plan[: args.limit]

    results, details = Path(args.results), Path(args.details_dir)
    if args.prior is not None and not args.smoke:    # never mix prior-override runs into the main CSV
        if results == DEFAULT_RESULTS:
            results = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "prior_diag" / "benchmark_results_prior.csv"
        if details == DEFAULT_DETAILS:
            details = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "prior_diag" / "runs"
        print(f"[benchmark] --prior {args.prior}: results -> {results}")
    max_batches, allow_lam = None, args.allow_default_lam
    max_hours = args.max_hours if args.max_hours is not None else bench.get("max_hours")
    if args.smoke:
        base_cfg = deep_update(base_cfg, {"train": {"epochs": 2, "warmup_epochs": 1}})
        results = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "smoke" / "benchmark_results_smoke.csv"
        details = REPO_ROOT / "results" / "smoke" / "runs"
        max_batches, allow_lam = 20, True
    root = Path(args.processed_root)

    print(f"[benchmark] pre-registration status: {PREREG_STATUS.upper()} (the run matrix is not frozen)")
    print(f"[benchmark] {summarize_plan(plan)}")
    missing_dirs = sorted({s.delay for s in plan if not (root / s.delay / "meta.json").exists()})
    missing_lam = sorted({s.delay for s in plan if s.loss == "delay_ipw" and not s.oracle
                          and (bench.get("lam_by_delay") or {}).get(s.delay) is None})
    if args.dry_run:
        if args.est_epoch_minutes:
            epochs = int(base_cfg["train"]["epochs"])
            print(f"[benchmark] upper bound (no early stopping): {len(plan)} x {epochs} epochs x "
                  f"{args.est_epoch_minutes} min = {len(plan) * epochs * args.est_epoch_minutes / 60:.1f} h")
        for s in plan[:5]:
            print(f"  e.g. {s.run_id}")
        if missing_dirs:
            print(f"[benchmark] WARNING missing processed dirs / meta.json: {missing_dirs}")
        if missing_lam:
            print(f"[benchmark] WARNING lam_by_delay not set for {missing_lam}: a real run would refuse (decision C)")
        return 0
    if missing_dirs:
        print(f"[benchmark] ERROR missing processed dirs / meta.json under {root}: {missing_dirs}")
        return 2
    if args.seed_results and not results.exists() and Path(args.seed_results).exists():
        results.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile(args.seed_results, results)
        print(f"[benchmark] seeded {results} from {args.seed_results}")
    if run_fn is None:
        from src.train.trainer import run_one   # lazy: keep --dry_run and planning tests free of torch work
        run_fn = run_one

    out = execute(plan, base_cfg, bench, processed_root=root, results_path=results, details_dir=details,
                  run_fn=run_fn, device=args.device, max_hours=max_hours,
                  assume_run_seconds=None if args.assume_run_minutes is None else args.assume_run_minutes * 60.0,
                  retry_failed=args.retry_failed, allow_default_lam=allow_lam,
                  allow_config_change=args.allow_config_change, max_train_batches=max_batches)
    print(f"[benchmark] finished: ok={out['n_ok']} failed={out['n_failed']} remaining={out['n_remaining']} "
          f"skipped_failed={out['n_skipped_failed']} budget_stop={out['stopped_for_budget']} -> {results}")
    return 1 if out["n_failed"] else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================

FILE: scripts\smoke_forward.py

================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Round-5 smoke test on ONE real batch: backbone(s) -> the four losses.

    python scripts/smoke_forward.py --processed_dir data/processed/delay_low --backbone both

Needs the processed graphs from scripts/prepare_data.py and pyg-lib. Prints shapes, P/N/U counts,
the estimated prior and each loss value (expect roughly ln 2 = 0.69 at initialisation), and exits
with status 1 if a check fails.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

import torch
import yaml

from src.data.dataset import TemporalGraphDataset, read_meta, seed_labels
from src.graph.builder import PNU_CODES, TXN
from src.losses import (
    DelayIPWLoss, MaturityMaskLoss, NaiveBCELoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss,
    compute_age_days, estimate_class_prior, lam_from_median_delay,
)
from src.models.classifier import build_classifier


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Forward + 4 losses on one real batch.")
    p.add_argument("--processed_dir", default="data/processed/delay_low")
    p.add_argument("--config", default=str(REPO_ROOT / "configs" / "base.yaml"))
    p.add_argument("--backbone", choices=["sage", "gat", "both"], default="both")
    p.add_argument("--lam", type=float, default=None, help="delay_ipw rate; default ln2 / (W / 2) (arbitrary, smoke only)")
    p.add_argument("--max_tries", type=int, default=30, help="batches to scan for one with both P and U seeds")
    return p.parse_args()


def main() -> int:
    args = parse_args()
    cfg = yaml.safe_load(Path(args.config).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    pdir = Path(args.processed_dir)
    meta = read_meta(pdir)
    s = cfg["sampler"]
    torch.manual_seed(0)

    ds = TemporalGraphDataset.from_file(
        pdir / "train.pt", num_neighbors=list(s["num_neighbors"]), batch_size=int(s["batch_size"]),
        shuffle=True, strict_time=bool(s.get("strict_time", True)),
        temporal_strategy=str(s.get("temporal_strategy", "uniform")), check_causality=True,
    )
    assert ds.feature_dim == int(meta["feature_dim"]), "feature_dim mismatch between graph and meta.json"

    batch = None
    for i, b in enumerate(ds):
        pnu = b[TXN].pnu[: int(b[TXN].batch_size)]
        if bool((pnu == PNU_CODES["P"]).any()) and bool((pnu == PNU_CODES["U"]).any()):
            batch = b
            break
        if i + 1 >= args.max_tries:
            break
    if batch is None:
        print("FAIL: no batch with both P and U seeds found; raise --max_tries or check the delay config.")
        return 1

    labels = seed_labels(batch)
    bs = int(batch[TXN].batch_size)
    age = compute_age_days(labels["time"], ds.data.label_cut)
    pnu = labels["pnu"]
    print(f"batch: seeds={bs} | P={int((pnu == 0).sum())} N={int((pnu == 1).sum())} U={int((pnu == 2).sum())} "
          f"| x={tuple(batch[TXN].x.shape)} | node types={batch.node_types}")

    window = float(meta["config"]["delay"]["maturity_window_days"])
    full = ds.data[TXN]
    prior = estimate_class_prior(full.y_obs, compute_age_days(full.time, ds.data.label_cut), window, full.in_train_view)
    lam = args.lam if args.lam is not None else lam_from_median_delay(window / 2.0)
    print(f"prior (mature region, observed labels only) = {prior:.4f} | delay_ipw lam = {lam:.4f} | W = {window} d")

    losses = {
        "naive_bce": NaiveBCELoss(),
        "maturity_mask": MaturityMaskLoss(),
        "delay_ipw": DelayIPWLoss(lam=lam),
        "tw_nndf": TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=prior),
    }
    ok = True
    for bb in (["sage", "gat"] if args.backbone == "both" else [args.backbone]):
        model = build_classifier(cfg["model"], meta, conv_type=bb)
        model.train()
        logits = model(batch)
        good = logits.shape == (bs,) and bool(torch.isfinite(logits).all())
        ok &= good
        print(f"\n[{bb}] params={model.num_parameters():,} | logits shape={tuple(logits.shape)} "
              f"mean={float(logits.mean()):+.3f} std={float(logits.std()):.3f} {'OK' if good else 'FAIL'}")
        for name, fn in losses.items():
            model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss = fn(model(batch), labels["y_obs"], pnu, age_days=age, in_train_view=labels["in_train_view"])
            loss.backward()
            has_grad = any(p.grad is not None and float(p.grad.abs().sum()) > 0 for p in model.parameters())
            sane = bool(torch.isfinite(loss)) and loss.dim() == 0 and 0.1 < float(loss) < 3.0
            extra = ""
            if name == "tw_nndf" and fn.last_risks is not None:
                r = fn.last_risks
                extra = f" | pos={float(r['pos_risk']):.4f} neg_raw={float(r['neg_risk_raw']):+.4f} clamped={bool(r['clamped'])}"
            ok &= sane and has_grad
            print(f"  {name:<14} loss={float(loss):.4f} grad={'yes' if has_grad else 'NO'} "
                  f"{'OK' if sane else 'CHECK'}{extra}")
    print("\nRESULT:", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
    return 0 if ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

================================================================================

FILE: configs\base.yaml

# configs/base.yaml -- shared settings. Overlay a delay file on top of it:
#   python scripts/prepare_data.py --config configs/base.yaml --delay_config configs/delay_low.yaml
# Keys marked "round 5" are placeholders for the model/trainer and are not read yet.

seed: 42
seeds: [42, 43, 44]            # 3 seeds -> 95% CI against the naive baseline A0

paths:
  data_dir: data               # folder with train_transaction.csv / train_identity.csv
  processed_dir: data/processed
  results_dir: results
  checkpoints_dir: checkpoints

split:                         # temporal split over the time span, day-aligned
  train_ratio: 0.65
  val_ratio: 0.16
  test_ratio: 0.19
  by: time                     # time | count
  snap_to_day: true

# Top-level features (read by load_phase_datasets(features_cfg=...), NOT graph.features).
features:
  lp_features:
    enabled: false       # true -> model_in_dim = feature_dim + 4 (461)
    maturity_window_days: null
    negative_rule: maturity_window
    log1p_counts: true

graph:
  # ASSUMPTION: IEEE-CIS has no true card/device id. Each entity is a PROXY key built
  # from several columns (card1..card6; DeviceInfo+id_30+id_31).
  # A row gets an edge only if every column in required_cols is non-null.
  # max_degree = super-node cap, FITTED ON THE TRAIN PERIOD ONLY: an entity with more
  # than max_degree train transactions is removed everywhere (its edges are dropped).
  # EMAIL IS NO LONGER AN ENTITY: a purchaser-email domain is a low-cardinality attribute
  # (a few dozen values), so every domain exceeded max_degree and 98.2% of train edges were
  # removed. It is now a feature block of the transaction node (see features.email).
  entities:
    card:
      key_cols: [card1, card2, card3, card4, card5, card6]
      required_cols: [card1]
      max_degree: 2000
    device:
      key_cols: [DeviceInfo, id_30, id_31]
      required_cols: [DeviceInfo]
      max_degree: 2000
  features:
    # Transaction-node features: numeric columns (V*, C*, D*, TransactionAmt, id_01..)
    # except numeric_exclude, median-imputed and z-scored with TRAIN statistics, plus
    # one-hot of the low-cardinality columns in onehot_cols (top max_categories on train).
    onehot_cols: [ProductCD, card4, card6, M1, M2, M3, M4, M5, M6, M7, M8, M9]
    numeric_exclude: [card1, addr1, addr2]   # id-like codes: not meaningful as numbers
    max_categories: 30
    # NEW: numeric columns whose TRAIN missing ratio is > max_missing_ratio are dropped
    # before imputation / scaling. null disables the filter. 0.9 is a starting value,
    # not a tuned one: the encoder prints which columns were dropped.
    max_missing_ratio: 0.9
    clip_z: 10.0
    add_missing_ratio: true
    # NEW: email as transaction features (statistics fitted on TRAIN only).
    email:
      cols: [P_emaildomain]      # add R_emaildomain here if you want the recipient too
      top_k: 10                  # one-hot of the 10 most frequent TRAIN domains (+ other, + missing)
      add_frequency: true        # relative frequency of the domain in TRAIN (unseen -> 0.0)

sampler:
  num_neighbors: [15, 10]      # per hop. NOTE: a transaction has <= 1 neighbour per entity
                               # type in hop 1, so the effective fan-out is ~[1, 10].
  batch_size: 1024
  eval_batch_size: 2048
  temporal_strategy: uniform   # uniform | last (most recent neighbours first)
  strict_time: true            # neighbours must be STRICTLY earlier than the seed
  num_workers: 0               # >0 only after checking pyg-lib + fork on your machine

model:
  backbone: sage         # sage (main, 72 runs) | gat (secondary check only)
  hidden_dim: 128
  num_layers: 2
  dropout: 0.2
  entity_embedding_dim: 32
  gat_heads: 4           # GAT only; hidden_dim must be divisible by it
  max_history: 20        # most recent history transactions fed to the GRU per seed
  gru_layers: 1
  use_temporal: true     # false -> SAGEConv/GATConv + MLP ablation

train:
  lr: 0.001
  weight_decay: 0.00001
  epochs: 30
  patience: 5
  warmup_epochs: 1       # linear LR warm-up per step; early-stopping patience starts after it
  min_delta: 0.0
  monitor: pr_auc        # pr_auc | roc_auc | recall_at_fpr (VALIDATION)
  grad_clip: 1.0         # null disables

eval:
  fpr: 0.01              # operating point: threshold chosen on VAL so that FPR <= 1%
  review_cost: 0.0       # EFL = missed fraud amount + review_cost * #FP. 0 = no explicit FP cost (KHÔNG CHẮC)

loss:
  name: naive_bce        # naive_bce | maturity_mask | delay_ipw | tw_nndf
  delay_ipw: {lam: 0.23} # ln2 / median_delay_days, to be swept (KHÔNG CHẮC if it is the right scale)
  tw_nndf: {unlabeled_statuses: [U], mode: nnpu, beta: 0.0, gamma: 1.0}

================================================================================

FILE: configs\base_nnpu.yaml

================================================================================

# configs/base_nnpu.yaml -- V1.5 config: IDENTICAL to base.yaml except the `loss:` block (hybrid TW-nnDF).
# Keep it a COMPLETE file: run_one / profile_epoch / smoke_forward read cfg["sampler"], cfg["model"], cfg["train"]
# directly, and the benchmark config hash covers sampler/model/train/eval/loss/features.
# tests/test_config_nnpu.py checks that it differs from base.yaml only in `loss`.

seed: 42
seeds: [42, 43, 44]            # 3 seeds -> 95% CI against the naive baseline A0

paths:
  data_dir: data               # folder with train_transaction.csv / train_identity.csv
  processed_dir: data/processed
  results_dir: results
  checkpoints_dir: checkpoints

split:                         # temporal split over the time span, day-aligned
  train_ratio: 0.65
  val_ratio: 0.16
  test_ratio: 0.19
  by: time                     # time | count
  snap_to_day: true

features:
  lp_features:
    enabled: false       # true -> model_in_dim = feature_dim + 4 (461)
    maturity_window_days: null
    negative_rule: maturity_window
    log1p_counts: true

graph:
  entities:
    card:
      key_cols: [card1, card2, card3, card4, card5, card6]
      required_cols: [card1]
      max_degree: 2000
    device:
      key_cols: [DeviceInfo, id_30, id_31]
      required_cols: [DeviceInfo]
      max_degree: 2000
  features:
    onehot_cols: [ProductCD, card4, card6, M1, M2, M3, M4, M5, M6, M7, M8, M9]
    numeric_exclude: [card1, addr1, addr2]   # id-like codes: not meaningful as numbers
    max_categories: 30
    max_missing_ratio: 0.9
    clip_z: 10.0
    add_missing_ratio: true
    email:
      cols: [P_emaildomain]
      top_k: 10
      add_frequency: true

sampler:
  num_neighbors: [15, 10]
  batch_size: 1024
  eval_batch_size: 2048
  temporal_strategy: uniform   # uniform | last
  strict_time: true            # neighbours must be STRICTLY earlier than the seed
  num_workers: 0

model:
  backbone: sage         # sage (main) | gat (secondary check)
  hidden_dim: 128
  num_layers: 2
  dropout: 0.2
  entity_embedding_dim: 32
  gat_heads: 4
  max_history: 20
  gru_layers: 1
  use_temporal: true

train:
  lr: 0.001
  weight_decay: 0.00001
  epochs: 30
  patience: 5
  warmup_epochs: 1
  min_delta: 0.0
  monitor: pr_auc        # VALIDATION
  grad_clip: 1.0

eval:
  fpr: 0.01
  review_cost: 0.0       # EFL = missed fraud amount + review_cost * #FP. 0 = no explicit FP cost (KHÔNG CHẮC)

loss:
  name: naive_bce        # naive_bce | maturity_mask | delay_ipw | tw_nndf
  delay_ipw: {lam: 0.23} # overridden per delay by benchmark.lam_by_delay
  maturity_mask:
    amount_aware: true           # C12 locked: same masking as tw_nndf (p95 of TRAIN amounts, age >= W/2)
    amount_quantile: 0.95
    amount_min_age_days: null    # null -> W/2
  tw_nndf:
    variant: hybrid              # Maturity-Masked BCE + Cost-Sensitive nnPU
    unlabeled_statuses: [U]
    mode: nnpu
    beta: 0.0
    gamma: 1.0
    lambda_pu: 0.3               # locked V1.5 (design choice, KHÔNG CHẮC)
    prior_mode: sliding_window   # mature_mean | sliding_window | sliding_unlabeled
    prior_span_days: 14
    prior_n_windows: 3
    amount_weighted: true        # cost-sensitive nnPU (TransactionAmt)
    max_weight: 10.0
    amount_aware: true           # trusted U rows: amount >= p95(TRAIN) and age >= amount_min_age_days
    amount_quantile: 0.95
    amount_min_age_days: null    # null -> W/2
    calibration: {enabled: true, max_samples: 200000, t_min: 0.25, t_max: 4.0}
