benchmark:
  seeds: [42, 43, 44]
  # delay_very_high needs: python scripts/prepare_data.py --delay_config configs/delay_very_high.yaml
  delays: [delay_low, delay_high, delay_very_high]
  backbone: sage
  # Kaggle quota guard: the runner stops cleanly before this many hours (CLI --max_hours overrides it).
  # Group order below = priority: the 72 method runs first, the 18 oracle runs last.
  max_hours: 28.0
  lam_by_delay:                 # ln2 / median of the truncated Weibull at the reference amount
    delay_low: 0.324            # median ~2.14 d
    delay_high: 0.080           # median ~8.68 d
    delay_very_high: 0.0399     # median ~17.36 d (scale 24, shape 1.3, cap 60)
  groups:
    main_sage:                  # 4 methods x 3 delays x 3 seeds = 36 runs (naive_bce = A0)
      enabled: true
      backbone: sage
      losses: [naive_bce, maturity_mask, delay_ipw, tw_nndf]
      lp: false
      use_temporal: true
    main_gat:                   # secondary backbone, 36 runs  -> the 72 method runs end here
      enabled: true
      backbone: gat
      losses: [naive_bce, maturity_mask, delay_ipw, tw_nndf]
      lp: false
      use_temporal: true
    oracle_sage:                # NaiveBCE on y_true (Recovery Ratio), 9 runs, run AFTER the 72
      enabled: true
      backbone: sage
      oracle: true
    oracle_gat:                 # 9 runs
      enabled: true
      backbone: gat
      oracle: true
    lp_naive:
      enabled: false
      losses: [naive_bce]
      lp: true
      use_temporal: true

      enabled: false

# configs/delay_high.yaml -- LONG label delay (about one month). Overlay on base.yaml.
# Simulation assumptions, not estimates from IEEE-CIS. For the fixed-delay regime use:
#   mode: fixed, params: {tau_days: 30}, maturity_window_days: 30
delay:
  mode: weibull
  maturity_window_days: 30
  params:
    shape: 1.3
    scale_days: 12.0
    amount_exponent: 0.3
    amount_ref: null
    cap_days: 30
    min_delay_hours: 1.0
    seed: 42

================================================================================

FILE: configs\delay_low.yaml

================================================================================

# configs/delay_low.yaml -- SHORT label delay (about one week). Overlay on base.yaml.
# These are SIMULATION ASSUMPTIONS for the benchmark, not estimates from IEEE-CIS
# (the dataset has no detection time). Direction of the amount effect is also assumed.
delay:
  mode: weibull                # fixed | exponential | weibull
  maturity_window_days: 7      # W: an old row with no report counts as N. Keep W >= cap_days,
                               # otherwise late frauds are hidden inside N (label noise).
  params:
    shape: 1.3                 # Weibull shape (ignored for exponential/fixed)
    scale_days: 3.0            # scale at the reference amount
    amount_exponent: 0.3       # scale_i = scale * (amt_i / amount_ref) ** -exponent ; 0 = amount-independent
    amount_ref: null           # null -> median TRAIN amount (filled in by prepare_data)
    cap_days: 7                # truncate the law at 7 days (no mass piled at the cap)
    min_delay_hours: 1.0       # delays are strictly positive
    seed: 42

FILE: tests\test_analyzer.py

"""Round-7 tests: cleaning, verdicts, Recovery Ratio gate, LaTeX markers, end-to-end files (synthetic rows)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import pytest

from src.eval import analyzer as A


def mk(delay, loss, seed, pr, efl, lp=False, status="ok"):
    return {"run_id": f"{delay}_{loss}_{'lp' if lp else 'nolp'}_s{seed}", "status": status, "delay": delay, "loss": loss,
            "backbone": "sage", "use_temporal": "True", "lp_features": str(lp), "seed": str(seed),
            "test_pr_auc": str(pr), "test_efl_ratio": str(efl), "test_roc_auc": "0.9", "test_recall_at_fpr": "0.5",
            "prereg_status": "unfrozen", "cfg_hash": "abc"}


def block(delay, loss, prs, efls, **kw):
    return [mk(delay, loss, 42 + i, p, e, **kw) for i, (p, e) in enumerate(zip(prs, efls))]


@pytest.fixture()
def rows():
    r = []
    r += block("delay_low", "naive_bce", [0.30, 0.31, 0.32], [0.60, 0.61, 0.62])
    r += block("delay_low", "oracle", [0.50, 0.51, 0.52], [0.40, 0.41, 0.42])
    r += block("delay_low", "tw_nndf", [0.40, 0.41, 0.42], [0.50, 0.51, 0.52])
    r += block("delay_low", "maturity_mask", [0.31, 0.32, 0.31], [0.60, 0.61, 0.60])
    r += block("delay_high", "naive_bce", [0.30, 0.31, 0.32], [0.60, 0.61, 0.62])
    r += block("delay_high", "oracle", [0.31, 0.32, 0.33], [0.59, 0.60, 0.61])   # weak
    r += block("delay_high", "tw_nndf", [0.40, 0.41, 0.42], [0.50, 0.51, 0.52])
    r += [mk("delay_low", "tw_nndf", 99, 0.0, 0.0, status="failed")]        # must be ignored
    return r


DL_TW = A.Cell("delay_low", "tw_nndf", "sage", True, False)
DL_MM = A.Cell("delay_low", "maturity_mask", "sage", True, False)


def test_clean_rows_drops_failed_and_dedups(rows):
    dup = mk("delay_low", "naive_bce", 42, 0.99, 0.10)                        # same run_id as an existing row
    parsed, warns = A.clean_rows(rows + [dup])
    assert len(parsed) == 21                                                  # 12 (delay_low) + 9 (delay_high), no failed
    assert any("status != ok" in w for w in warns)
    p = [q for q in parsed if q["run_id"] == "delay_low_naive_bce_nolp_s42"][0]
    assert p["test_pr_auc"] == pytest.approx(0.99)                            # the LAST row wins


def test_verdicts_against_a0_ci(rows):
    values = A.collect_values(A.clean_rows(rows)[0])
    comp = A.compare_to_naive(values)
    assert comp[(DL_TW, "test_pr_auc")]["verdict"] == "improves"
    assert comp[(DL_TW, "test_efl_ratio")]["verdict"] == "improves"           # lower is better: 0.51 < CI low 0.585
    assert comp[(DL_MM, "test_pr_auc")]["verdict"] == "within_CI"
    assert comp[(DL_MM, "test_efl_ratio")]["verdict"] == "within_CI"


def test_recovery_reported_and_not_reported(rows):
    rec = A.recovery_table(A.collect_values(A.clean_rows(rows)[0]))
    get = lambda d, l, m: [r for r in rec if (r["delay"], r["loss"], r["metric"]) == (d, l, m)][0]
    ok = get("delay_low", "tw_nndf", "test_pr_auc")
    assert ok["reported"] and ok["value"] == pytest.approx((0.41 - 0.31) / (0.51 - 0.31))
    weak = get("delay_high", "tw_nndf", "test_pr_auc")                        # oracle mean 0.32 is inside A0's CI
    assert (not weak["reported"]) and math.isnan(weak["value"]) and "CI" in weak["reason"]


def test_recovery_lower_is_better_for_efl(rows):
    rec = A.recovery_table(A.collect_values(A.clean_rows(rows)[0]))
    r = [x for x in rec if (x["delay"], x["loss"], x["metric"]) == ("delay_low", "tw_nndf", "test_efl_ratio")][0]
    assert r["reported"] and r["value"] == pytest.approx((0.61 - 0.51) / (0.61 - 0.41))


def test_latex_tables_contain_markers(rows):
    values = A.collect_values(A.clean_rows(rows)[0])
    summ, comp = A.summarise(values), A.compare_to_naive(values)
    tex = A.main_table_tex("delay_low", summ, comp, "unfrozen")
    assert r"\dagger" in tex and "TW-nnDF" in tex and "unfrozen" in tex and r"\textbf{" in tex
    assert r"\begin{tabular}" in tex and tex.count(r"\toprule") == 1
    rtex = A.recovery_table_tex(A.recovery_table(values), "unfrozen")
    assert "n/r" in rtex and "0.50" in rtex


def test_end_to_end_writes_files(rows, tmp_path):
    res = A.run_analysis(rows, tmp_path, plots=False)
    assert res["prereg"].startswith("unfrozen") and res["n_runs"] == 21
    for name in ("summary.csv", "recovery.csv", "nndf_diagnostics.csv", "table_recovery.tex",
                 "table_main_delay_low.tex", "table_main_delay_high.tex"):
        assert (tmp_path / name).exists()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        A.run_analysis([mk("d", "naive_bce", 1, 0.1, 0.1, status="failed")], tmp_path, plots=False)

FILE: tests\test_benchmark.py

"""Round-7 tests: benchmark plan, resume, failure policy, config guard, time budget (fake run_fn, no training)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("run_benchmark", ROOT / "scripts" / "run_benchmark.py")
RB = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
sys.modules["run_benchmark"] = RB
_spec.loader.exec_module(RB)

LOSSES4 = ["naive_bce", "maturity_mask", "delay_ipw", "tw_nndf"]
BENCH = {
    "seeds": [1, 2, 3], "delays": ["delay_low", "delay_high"], "backbone": "sage",
    "lam_by_delay": {"delay_low": 0.2, "delay_high": 0.05},
    "groups": {
        "oracle": {"oracle": True},
        "main": {"losses": LOSSES4, "lp": False, "use_temporal": True},
        "lp_naive": {"enabled": False, "losses": ["naive_bce"], "lp": True, "use_temporal": True},
    },
}
BASE_CFG = {"sampler": {}, "model": {}, "train": {"epochs": 3}, "eval": {}, "loss": {"delay_ipw": {"lam": 0.23}}, "features": {}}


def make_run_fn(calls, fail_on=None, clock=None, cost=0.0):
    def run_fn(cfg, pdir, **kw):
        calls.append((pdir.name, kw["loss"], kw["oracle"], kw["seed"], cfg["loss"].get("delay_ipw")))
        if fail_on is not None and (pdir.name, kw["loss"], kw["seed"]) == fail_on:
            raise RuntimeError("boom")
        if clock is not None:
            clock["t"] += cost
        label = "oracle" if kw["oracle"] else kw["loss"]
        return {"row": {"run_id": "x", "delay": pdir.name, "loss": label, "oracle": kw["oracle"],
                        "backbone": kw["backbone"], "use_temporal": kw["use_temporal"], "lp_features": kw["lp"],
                        "seed": kw["seed"], "best_epoch": 1, "test_pr_auc": 0.5},
                "details": {"info": {"lam": 0.1}, "fit": {"history": [{"epoch": 1.0}]}, "test": {}, "cfg": {}}}
    return run_fn


def paths(tmp: Path) -> dict:
    return dict(processed_root=tmp / "proc", results_path=tmp / "r.csv", details_dir=tmp / "runs")


def test_plan_counts_and_order():
    plan = RB.build_plan(BENCH)
    assert len(plan) == 6 + 24 and len({s.run_id for s in plan}) == len(plan)
    assert all(s.oracle for s in plan[:6])                                   # oracle first
    assert [(s.delay, s.loss, s.seed) for s in plan[6:9]] == [("delay_low", "naive_bce", i) for i in (1, 2, 3)]
    assert plan[0].run_id == "delay_low_oracle_sage_gru_nolp_s1"
    b2 = copy.deepcopy(BENCH)
    b2["groups"]["lp_naive"]["enabled"] = True
    assert len(RB.build_plan(b2)) == 36


def test_duplicate_run_id_raises():
    b = copy.deepcopy(BENCH)
    b["groups"]["again"] = {"losses": ["naive_bce"], "lp": False, "use_temporal": True}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate"):
        RB.build_plan(b)


def test_prior_group():
    b = copy.deepcopy(BENCH)
    b["groups"] = {"p": {"losses": ["tw_nndf"], "priors_by_delay": {"delay_low": [0.0139]}}}
    plan = RB.build_plan(b)
    assert len(plan) == 3 and all(s.delay == "delay_low" and s.run_id.endswith("_pi0.0139") for s in plan)
    b["groups"] = {"p": {"losses": ["naive_bce"], "priors_by_delay": {"delay_low": [0.0139]}}}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        RB.build_plan(b)


def test_missing_lam_fails_before_any_run(tmp_path):
    b = copy.deepcopy(BENCH)
    b["lam_by_delay"]["delay_low"] = None
    calls: list = []
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="lam_by_delay"):
        RB.execute(RB.build_plan(b), BASE_CFG, b, run_fn=make_run_fn(calls), **paths(tmp_path))
    assert calls == []
    cfg = RB.resolve_cfg(BASE_CFG, BENCH, [s for s in RB.build_plan(BENCH) if s.loss == "delay_ipw"][0], False)
    assert cfg["loss"]["delay_ipw"] == {"lam": 0.2}                          # base.yaml's 0.23 is ignored


def test_resume_skips_done_and_failed_policy(tmp_path):
    plan = RB.build_plan(BENCH)
    p = paths(tmp_path)
    out = RB.execute(plan, BASE_CFG, BENCH, run_fn=make_run_fn([], fail_on=("delay_low", "maturity_mask", 1)), **p)
    assert (out["n_ok"], out["n_failed"]) == (len(plan) - 1, 1)
    rows = RB.read_rows(p["results_path"])
    assert sum(r["status"] == "ok" for r in rows) == len(plan) - 1 and rows[0]["prereg_status"] in ("unfrozen", "")

    calls2: list = []
    out2 = RB.execute(plan, BASE_CFG, BENCH, run_fn=make_run_fn(calls2), **p)       # nothing to do, failure not retried
    assert calls2 == [] and out2["n_skipped_failed"] == 1

    calls3: list = []
    RB.execute(plan, BASE_CFG, BENCH, run_fn=make_run_fn(calls3), retry_failed=True, **p)
    assert len(calls3) == 1 and calls3[0][1] == "maturity_mask"
    ok_ids = {r["run_id"] for r in RB.read_rows(p["results_path"]) if r["status"] == "ok"}
    assert ok_ids == {s.run_id for s in plan}


def test_config_hash_guard(tmp_path):
    plan = RB.build_plan(BENCH)[:2]
    p = paths(tmp_path)
    RB.execute(plan, BASE_CFG, BENCH, run_fn=make_run_fn([]), **p)
    changed = copy.deepcopy(BASE_CFG)
    changed["train"]["epochs"] = 5
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="config hash"):
        RB.execute(RB.build_plan(BENCH)[:4], changed, BENCH, run_fn=make_run_fn([]), **p)
    RB.execute(RB.build_plan(BENCH)[:4], changed, BENCH, run_fn=make_run_fn([]), allow_config_change=True, **p)


def test_time_budget_stops_before_a_run_that_does_not_fit(tmp_path):
    clock = {"t": 0.0}
    calls: list = []
    plan = RB.build_plan(BENCH)
    out = RB.execute(plan, BASE_CFG, BENCH, run_fn=make_run_fn(calls, clock=clock, cost=3600.0),
                     clock=lambda: clock["t"], max_hours=2.5, **paths(tmp_path))
    assert len(calls) == 2 and out["stopped_for_budget"] and out["n_remaining"] == len(plan) - 2


def test_csv_header_guard(tmp_path):
    bad = tmp_path / "bad.csv"
    bad.write_text("a,b\n1,2\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        RB.read_rows(bad)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        RB.append_row(bad, {"run_id": "x"})

FILE: tests\test_build_training_loss.py

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
yaml = pytest.importorskip("yaml")
pytest.importorskip("torch_geometric")

from src.graph.builder import PNU_CODES, TXN  # noqa: E402
from src.losses.maturity_mask import MaturityMaskLoss  # noqa: E402
from src.losses.tw_nndf import HybridTWnnDFLoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss  # noqa: E402
from src.train.trainer import TrainConfig, build_training_loss  # noqa: E402

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DAY, W = 86400.0, 30.0
P, N, U = PNU_CODES["P"], PNU_CODES["N"], PNU_CODES["U"]


class _Graph(dict):
    label_cut = 100 * DAY


def fake_train_ds():
    n = 4000
    idx = torch.arange(n)
    age = (idx % 100).float() + 0.5  # 0.5 .. 99.5 days before the cut
    y_obs = ((idx % 25) == 0).long()
    pnu = torch.where(y_obs == 1, torch.tensor(P), torch.where(age >= W, torch.tensor(N), torch.tensor(U)))
    store = SimpleNamespace(
        pnu=pnu.long(), in_train_view=torch.ones(n, dtype=torch.bool), y_obs=y_obs,
        time=(_Graph.label_cut - age.double() * DAY), amount=((idx % 100) + 1).float() * 10.0,
        y_true=(y_obs.bool() | ((idx % 40) == 1)).long(),
    )
    return SimpleNamespace(data=_Graph({TXN: store}))


META = {"config": {"delay": {"maturity_window_days": W}}}


def build(loss, params):
    return build_training_loss(TrainConfig(loss=loss, loss_params=params), fake_train_ds(), META)


def test_maturity_mask_amount_aware_keys_reach_the_loss():
    fn, info = build("maturity_mask", {"amount_aware": True, "amount_quantile": 0.9, "amount_min_age_days": None})
    ds = fake_train_ds().data[TXN]
    assert isinstance(fn, MaturityMaskLoss) and fn.needs_amount
    assert fn.amount_threshold == pytest.approx(float(torch.quantile(ds.amount.float(), 0.9)))
    assert fn.amount_min_age_days == pytest.approx(W / 2) and "n_U_trusted" in info


def test_maturity_mask_without_amount_keys_is_unchanged():
    fn, _ = build("maturity_mask", {})
    assert fn.amount_threshold is None and not fn.needs_amount
    fn2, _ = build("maturity_mask", {"amount_aware": False, "amount_quantile": 0.95, "amount_min_age_days": None})
    assert fn2.amount_threshold is None


def test_pu_only_ignores_hybrid_keys_without_crashing():
    fn, _ = build("tw_nndf", {"unlabeled_statuses": ["U"], "mode": "nnpu", "amount_aware": True, "lambda_pu": 0.3})
    assert type(fn) is TimeWindowNNDFLoss


@pytest.mark.parametrize("prior_mode,expect_dynamic", [("sliding_window", False), ("sliding_dynamic", True)])
def test_hybrid_builds_with_and_without_sliding_prior(prior_mode, expect_dynamic):
    params = {"variant": "hybrid", "unlabeled_statuses": ["U"], "mode": "nnpu", "lambda_pu": 0.3,
              "prior_mode": prior_mode, "amount_weighted": True, "amount_aware": True,
              "calibration": {"enabled": True, "max_samples": 1000}}
    fn, info = build("tw_nndf", params)
    assert isinstance(fn, HybridTWnnDFLoss) and fn.needs_amount and fn.lambda_pu == 0.3
    assert (fn.sliding_prior is not None) is expect_dynamic and fn.calibrator is not None
    ds = fake_train_ds().data[TXN]
    k = 256
    out = fn(torch.randn(k), ds.y_obs[:k], ds.pnu[:k],
             age_days=(_Graph.label_cut - ds.time[:k]).float() / DAY, in_train_view=ds.in_train_view[:k],
             amount=ds.amount[:k])
    assert torch.isfinite(out) and info["variant"] == "hybrid"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("loss", ["maturity_mask", "tw_nndf", "naive_bce", "delay_ipw"])
def test_every_loss_builds_from_base_nnpu_yaml(loss):
    cfg = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "configs" / "base_nnpu.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    cfg["loss"]["delay_ipw"] = {"lam": 0.1}  # as run_benchmark.resolve_cfg does
    fn, _ = build_training_loss(TrainConfig.from_cfg(cfg, loss=loss), fake_train_ds(), META)
    assert fn is not None

================================================================================

FILE: tests\test_config_nnpu.py

# =====================================================================
# FILE: tests/test_config_nnpu.py   (FILE MỚI)
# TRẠNG THÁI: MỚI. Smoke test cục bộ, không cần dữ liệu thật: chứng minh mode=nnpu trong base.yaml
#             tới được loss, ascent kích hoạt, và run_benchmark.py không còn định nghĩa lặp.
#             Chạy: pytest -q tests/test_config_nnpu.py tests/test_tw_nndf_ascent.py
# =====================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

yaml = pytest.importorskip("yaml")
torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
pytest.importorskip("torch_geometric")

from src.graph.builder import PNU_CODES  # noqa: E402
from src.losses import TimeWindowNNDFLoss  # noqa: E402
from src.train.trainer import TrainConfig  # noqa: E402

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
P, U = PNU_CODES["P"], PNU_CODES["U"]


def _loss_from_base_yaml() -> TimeWindowNNDFLoss:
    cfg = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "configs" / "base.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    t = TrainConfig.from_cfg(cfg, loss="tw_nndf")
    params = dict(t.loss_params)
    if "unlabeled_statuses" in params:
        params["unlabeled_statuses"] = tuple(params["unlabeled_statuses"])
    return TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.0338, **params)


def test_base_yaml_mode_reaches_the_loss():
    fn = _loss_from_base_yaml()
    assert fn.mode == "nnpu" and fn.beta == 0.0 and fn.gamma == 1.0


def test_ascent_branch_fires_with_config_loss():
    fn = _loss_from_base_yaml()
    n_p, n_u = 50, 500
    preds = torch.cat([torch.full((n_p,), 4.0), torch.full((n_u,), -4.0)]).requires_grad_(True)
    pnu = torch.cat([torch.full((n_p,), P), torch.full((n_u,), U)]).long()
    loss = fn(preds, (pnu == P).long(), pnu, age_days=torch.zeros_like(preds))
    loss.backward()
    assert float(fn.last_risks["ascent"]) == 1.0
    assert float(preds.grad[n_p:].abs().max()) > 0.0       # U rows are NOT dead (clamp mode would give 0)


def test_run_benchmark_has_no_duplicate_definitions():
    src = (ROOT / "scripts" / "run_benchmark.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert src.count("def apply_prior_override(") == 1

def test_base_nnpu_is_base_plus_loss_overlay():
    a = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "configs" / "base.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    b = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "configs" / "base_nnpu.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    a.pop("loss")
    b_loss = b.pop("loss")
    assert a == b, "base_nnpu.yaml drifted from base.yaml outside `loss:`"
    t = b_loss["tw_nndf"]
    assert t["variant"] == "hybrid" and t["mode"] == "nnpu" and t["lambda_pu"] == 0.3


def test_base_nnpu_reaches_the_trainer_config():
    cfg = yaml.safe_load((ROOT / "configs" / "base_nnpu.yaml").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    p = TrainConfig.from_cfg(cfg, loss="tw_nndf").loss_params
    assert p["lambda_pu"] == 0.3 and p["calibration"]["enabled"] is True

# tests/test_headroom.py
import pytest
pytest.importorskip("scipy")
from src.eval.headroom import has_headroom, headroom_table, main, welch_diff_ci


def test_welch_known_answer_equal_halves():
    # equal SEs, n=3 -> Welch df = 4, t_{.975,4} = 2.7764; half_diff = sqrt(2)*0.1*2.7764/4.3027
    r = welch_diff_ci(0.5, 0.1, 3, 0.3, 0.1, 3)
    assert r["diff"] == pytest.approx(-0.2) and r["df"] == pytest.approx(4.0, abs=1e-6)
    assert (r["hi"] - r["lo"]) / 2 == pytest.approx(0.09126, abs=5e-4)


def test_identical_groups_include_zero_and_sign_flips():
    assert not welch_diff_ci(0.4, 0.05, 3, 0.4, 0.05, 3)["excludes_zero"]
    a, b = welch_diff_ci(0.3, 0.02, 3, 0.5, 0.02, 3), welch_diff_ci(0.5, 0.02, 3, 0.3, 0.02, 3)
    assert a["excludes_zero"] and a["diff"] == pytest.approx(-b["diff"])


def test_real_v1_numbers_qualitative(tmp_path):
    rows = [  # copied from summary.csv (test_pr_auc)
        ("delay_high", "naive_bce", 0.340249667, 0.135926589), ("delay_high", "oracle", 0.411797595, 0.031264520),
        ("delay_low", "naive_bce", 0.411928949, 0.039570596), ("delay_low", "tw_nndf", 0.219110809, 0.080324570),
    ]
    p = tmp_path / "summary.csv"
    p.write_text("delay,method,metric,n,mean,half\n" +
                 "\n".join(f"{d},{m},test_pr_auc,3,{mu},{h}" for d, m, mu, h in rows) + "\n")
    from src.eval.headroom import load_summary
    t = {(r["delay"], r["method"]): r for r in headroom_table(load_summary(str(p)))}
    assert not t[("delay_high", "oracle")]["excludes_zero"] and not has_headroom(t[("delay_high", "oracle")])
    assert t[("delay_low", "tw_nndf")]["excludes_zero"] and t[("delay_low", "tw_nndf")]["hi"] < 0
    assert main(["--summary", str(p)]) == 0

FILE: tests\test_leakage.py

"""Canary tests for data leakage (round 3: masking + feature columns).

Run from the repo root:  ``pytest -q``  (or ``python -m pytest -q``).

A canary test must be able to FAIL. Besides the happy path, several tests
below inject a deliberate leak and assert that the check catches it.

(a) Masking integrity    -> implemented
(b) Feature leakage      -> implemented
(c) Causal graph checks  -> empty skeleton, completed in round 4
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from src.data.delay_simulator import assign_pnu_masks, pnu_diagnostics, simulate_delay
from src.data.loader import (
    DETECTION_COL,
    ID_COL,
    IN_TRAIN_VIEW_COL,
    PNU_COL,
    SECONDS_PER_DAY,
    TARGET_COL,
    TIME_COL,
    Y_OBS_COL,
    LeakageError,
    assert_no_label_columns,
    get_feature_columns,
    get_time_stats,
)
from src.data.splitter import compute_split_boundaries, temporal_split

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Dummy data (self-contained, mimics IEEE-CIS: relative seconds starting at day 1)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
N_DAYS = 60


def make_dummy_df(n_rows: int = 3000, n_days: int = N_DAYS, fraud_rate: float = 0.06, seed: int = 0) -> pd.DataFrame:
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = np.sort(rng.integers(SECONDS_PER_DAY, (n_days + 1) * SECONDS_PER_DAY, size=n_rows))
    df = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            ID_COL: np.arange(2_987_000, 2_987_000 + n_rows),
            TARGET_COL: (rng.random(n_rows) < fraud_rate).astype(np.int8),
            TIME_COL: t,
            "TransactionAmt": np.round(np.exp(rng.normal(4.0, 1.2, n_rows)), 2).clip(0.25),
            "ProductCD": rng.choice(list("WCRHS"), n_rows),
            "card1": rng.integers(1000, 2000, n_rows).astype(np.float32),
            "addr1": rng.integers(100, 500, n_rows).astype(np.float32),
            "V1": rng.normal(size=n_rows).astype(np.float32),
            "V2": rng.normal(size=n_rows).astype(np.float32),
            "V3": rng.normal(size=n_rows).astype(np.float32),
        }
    )
    df.loc[rng.random(n_rows) < 0.2, "V3"] = np.nan
    return df


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def dummy_df() -> pd.DataFrame:
    return make_dummy_df()


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def t_train(dummy_df: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
    return compute_split_boundaries(dummy_df).t_train


# name -> (mode, params, maturity_window_days passed to assign_pnu_masks)
DELAY_CONFIGS: dict[str, tuple[str, dict, float | None]] = {
    "fixed_7d": ("fixed", {"tau_days": 7}, None),
    "exp_7d_cap30": ("exponential", {"scale_days": 7, "cap_days": 30}, None),
    "weibull_amount": ("weibull", {"scale_days": 10, "shape": 1.3, "amount_exponent": 0.3, "cap_days": 30}, None),
    # Uncapped heavy tail with an explicit window W=30 d: some frauds are detected
    # AFTER the row is already "mature" (label noise inside N).
    "exp_uncapped_W30": ("exponential", {"scale_days": 10, "cap_days": None}, 30.0),
}


def build_masked(df: pd.DataFrame, t_train: float, name: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    mode, params, window = DELAY_CONFIGS[name]
    with_delay = simulate_delay(df, t_train, mode=mode, params=params)
    return assign_pnu_masks(with_delay, t_train, maturity_window_days=window)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# (a) Masking integrity
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", list(DELAY_CONFIGS))
def test_a_late_detection_means_y_obs_zero(dummy_df, t_train, name):
    """detection_time > t_train  =>  y_obs == 0 (fraud rows become U)."""
    m = build_masked(dummy_df, t_train, name)
    late = m[DETECTION_COL] > t_train
    late_fraud = late & (m[TARGET_COL] == 1)

    assert late_fraud.any(), "vacuous test: no late-detected fraud in dummy data"
    assert (m.loc[late, Y_OBS_COL] == 0).all()
    if DELAY_CONFIGS[name][1].get("cap_days", 1) is not None:  # capped laws: no mature hidden fraud
        assert (m.loc[late_fraud, PNU_COL] == "U").all()

    # converse: fraud detected in time, before the cut -> observed positive
    seen = (m[TARGET_COL] == 1) & m[IN_TRAIN_VIEW_COL] & (m[DETECTION_COL] <= t_train)
    assert seen.any()
    assert (m.loc[seen, Y_OBS_COL] == 1).all()
    assert (m.loc[seen, PNU_COL] == "P").all()

    # P <=> y_obs==1 ; N and U never carry a positive observed label
    assert ((m[PNU_COL] == "P") == (m[Y_OBS_COL] == 1)).all()
    assert (m.loc[m[PNU_COL] != "P", Y_OBS_COL] == 0).all()
    # rows at/after the cut are never observed
    assert (m.loc[~m[IN_TRAIN_VIEW_COL], Y_OBS_COL] == 0).all()
    assert (m.loc[~m[IN_TRAIN_VIEW_COL], PNU_COL] == "U").all()


def test_a_boundary_is_inclusive_at_t_train():
    t_cut = 10 * SECONDS_PER_DAY
    df = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            TIME_COL: [t_cut - 1000.0] * 3,
            TARGET_COL: [1, 1, 1],
            DETECTION_COL: [t_cut - 1.0, t_cut, t_cut + 1.0],
        }
    )
    m = assign_pnu_masks(df, t_cut, maturity_window_days=1.0)
    assert m[Y_OBS_COL].tolist() == [1, 1, 0]
    assert m[PNU_COL].astype(str).tolist() == ["P", "P", "U"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", [0, 1, 2])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", list(DELAY_CONFIGS))
def test_a_permuting_hidden_labels_changes_nothing(dummy_df, t_train, name, seed):
    """Shuffle isFraud among all rows with detection_time > t_train (immature
    region + Val/Test): X, detection_time, y_obs and P/N/U must be identical."""
    base = build_masked(dummy_df, t_train, name)
    region = base[DETECTION_COL] > t_train

    permuted = dummy_df.copy()
    vals = permuted.loc[region, TARGET_COL].to_numpy().copy()
    np.random.default_rng(seed).shuffle(vals)
    permuted.loc[region, TARGET_COL] = vals
    assert (permuted[TARGET_COL] != dummy_df[TARGET_COL]).any(), "vacuous: permutation changed nothing"

    other = build_masked(permuted, t_train, name)
    cols = get_feature_columns(base)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(base[cols], other[cols])
    pd.testing.assert_series_equal(base[DETECTION_COL], other[DETECTION_COL])
    pd.testing.assert_series_equal(base[Y_OBS_COL], other[Y_OBS_COL])
    pd.testing.assert_series_equal(base[PNU_COL], other[PNU_COL])


def test_a_status_is_a_function_of_time_not_of_label(dummy_df, t_train):
    """With uncapped delays some mature rows are undetected fraud. They must
    sit in N together with legit rows; if status read isFraud they would be U
    and the model could learn 'U-and-old = fraud'."""
    m = build_masked(dummy_df, t_train, "exp_uncapped_W30")
    window_s = 30.0 * SECONDS_PER_DAY
    expected_n = m[IN_TRAIN_VIEW_COL] & (m[TIME_COL] + window_s <= t_train) & (m[Y_OBS_COL] == 0)
    assert ((m[PNU_COL] == "N") == expected_n).all()
    hidden_in_n = (m[PNU_COL] == "N") & (m[DETECTION_COL] > t_train)
    assert hidden_in_n.any(), "vacuous: no long-delay rows in N"
    assert pnu_diagnostics(m)["n_fraud_in_N"] >= 0  # reported as label noise, not hidden


def test_a_checker_catches_a_naive_label_leak(dummy_df, t_train):
    """Negative control: y_obs = isFraud (no masking) must violate the invariant."""
    m = build_masked(dummy_df, t_train, "fixed_7d")
    leaky = m.copy()
    leaky[Y_OBS_COL] = leaky[TARGET_COL].astype(np.int8)
    late = leaky[DETECTION_COL] > t_train
    assert not (leaky.loc[late, Y_OBS_COL] == 0).all()


# -- delay generator sanity (supports (a)) ----------------------------------- #
def test_delay_is_label_independent_and_order_independent(dummy_df, t_train):
    params = {"scale_days": 8, "shape": 1.4, "amount_exponent": 0.2, "cap_days": 30}
    full = simulate_delay(dummy_df, t_train, mode="weibull", params=params).set_index(ID_COL)[DETECTION_COL]

    no_label = simulate_delay(dummy_df.drop(columns=[TARGET_COL]), t_train, mode="weibull", params=params)
    pd.testing.assert_series_equal(full, no_label.set_index(ID_COL)[DETECTION_COL])  # never reads isFraud

    shuffled = dummy_df.sample(frac=1.0, random_state=3)
    again = simulate_delay(shuffled, t_train, mode="weibull", params=params).set_index(ID_COL)[DETECTION_COL]
    pd.testing.assert_series_equal(full, again.loc[full.index])  # same delay per TransactionID


def test_delay_bounds_and_seed(dummy_df, t_train):
    d = simulate_delay(dummy_df, t_train, "exponential", {"scale_days": 7, "cap_days": 30, "min_delay_hours": 1})
    delay = d[DETECTION_COL] - d[TIME_COL]
    assert (delay >= 3600.0 - 1e-6).all() and (delay <= 30 * SECONDS_PER_DAY + 1e-6).all()

    fixed = simulate_delay(dummy_df, t_train, "fixed", {"tau_days": 7})
    assert np.allclose(fixed[DETECTION_COL] - fixed[TIME_COL], 7 * SECONDS_PER_DAY)

    other_seed = simulate_delay(dummy_df, t_train, "exponential", {"scale_days": 7, "cap_days": 30, "seed": 7})
    assert not np.allclose(d[DETECTION_COL], other_seed[DETECTION_COL])


def test_amount_exponent_makes_large_amounts_faster(dummy_df, t_train):
    d = simulate_delay(dummy_df, t_train, "weibull", {"scale_days": 10, "shape": 1.2, "amount_exponent": 0.5, "cap_days": 60})
    delay = d[DETECTION_COL] - d[TIME_COL]
    lo, hi = d["TransactionAmt"].quantile([1 / 3, 2 / 3])
    assert delay[d["TransactionAmt"] >= hi].median() < delay[d["TransactionAmt"] <= lo].median()


def test_delay_rejects_unknown_params(dummy_df, t_train):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        simulate_delay(dummy_df, t_train, "weibull", {"scale": 3})


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# (b) Feature leakage
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def masked_df(dummy_df, t_train) -> pd.DataFrame:
    return build_masked(dummy_df, t_train, "weibull_amount")


def test_b_feature_matrix_has_no_isfraud(masked_df):
    cols = get_feature_columns(masked_df)
    X = masked_df[cols]
    assert TARGET_COL not in X.columns
    for banned in (Y_OBS_COL, PNU_COL, DETECTION_COL, IN_TRAIN_VIEW_COL, ID_COL, TIME_COL):
        assert banned not in X.columns, banned
    assert_no_label_columns(X.columns)  # must not raise
    assert {"TransactionAmt", "card1", "V1"} <= set(X.columns)  # real features survive

    # no feature is a verbatim copy of the label (numeric columns only)
    y = masked_df[TARGET_COL].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    for c in cols:
        if pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(X[c]):
            v = X[c].to_numpy(dtype=float)
            assert not np.array_equal(v, y, equal_nan=True), f"{c} copies isFraud"
            assert not np.array_equal(v, 1 - y, equal_nan=True), f"{c} copies 1-isFraud"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [TARGET_COL, "isFraud_lag1", Y_OBS_COL, PNU_COL, DETECTION_COL, TIME_COL])
def test_b_guard_detects_injected_leaks(masked_df, bad):
    cols = get_feature_columns(masked_df)
    with pytest.raises(LeakageError):
        assert_no_label_columns([*cols, bad])


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# (c) Causal graph integrity  --  EMPTY SKELETON (to be completed in round 4)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def check_causal_graph_integrity(
    edge_index: np.ndarray,
    node_time: np.ndarray,
    node_split: np.ndarray,
) -> None:
    """Round 4. Planned checks (raise AssertionError on violation):

    1. Causality: for every edge (src -> dst), ``node_time[src] < node_time[dst]``
       (strict; PyG's temporal sampler allows equality, so ties need a rule).
    2. No Val/Test -> Train edge: ``node_split[src] <= node_split[dst]`` with
       order train < val < test, and the Train graph holds only Train nodes.
    3. (hook) sampler output: every sampled neighbour is no later than its seed.

    Args:
        edge_index: int array of shape (2, E) with rows (src, dst).
        node_time: float array (N,) with TransactionDT of each node.
        node_split: int array (N,) with 0=train, 1=val, 2=test.
    """
    raise NotImplementedError("Round 4: needs src/graph/builder.py and sampler.py")


@pytest.mark.skip(reason="Round 4: causal graph builder/sampler not written yet")
def test_c_causal_graph_integrity():
    check_causal_graph_integrity(
        edge_index=np.empty((2, 0), dtype=np.int64),
        node_time=np.empty(0),
        node_split=np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64),
    )


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Supporting modules: splitter and loader time stats
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_temporal_split_is_disjoint_ordered_and_close_to_ratios(dummy_df):
    train, val, test = temporal_split(dummy_df)
    assert len(train) + len(val) + len(test) == len(dummy_df)
    assert train[TIME_COL].max() < val[TIME_COL].min()
    assert val[TIME_COL].max() < test[TIME_COL].min()
    assert set(train[ID_COL]).isdisjoint(val[ID_COL]) and set(val[ID_COL]).isdisjoint(test[ID_COL])

    b = compute_split_boundaries(dummy_df)
    span = b.t_end - b.t_start
    tol = 1.0 / N_DAYS + 1e-9  # one day of rounding
    assert abs((b.t_train - b.t_start) / span - 0.65) <= tol
    assert abs((b.t_val - b.t_train) / span - 0.16) <= tol
    assert (b.t_train % SECONDS_PER_DAY == 0) and (b.t_val % SECONDS_PER_DAY == 0)


def test_temporal_split_rejects_bad_ratios(dummy_df):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        temporal_split(dummy_df, 0.7, 0.2, 0.2)


def test_time_stats_in_days(dummy_df):
    ts = get_time_stats(dummy_df)
    assert ts.min_day == pytest.approx(dummy_df[TIME_COL].min() / SECONDS_PER_DAY)
    assert ts.max_day == pytest.approx(dummy_df[TIME_COL].max() / SECONDS_PER_DAY)
    assert 1.0 <= ts.min_day < 1.5 and ts.max_day <= N_DAYS + 1


import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

from src.losses import (
    DelayIPWLoss, MaturityMaskLoss, NaiveBCELoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss, PNU_N, PNU_P, PNU_U,
)


def _toy():
    """6 rows: 2 P, 2 N, 2 U (U rows have y_obs = 0)."""
    pnu = torch.tensor([PNU_P, PNU_P, PNU_N, PNU_N, PNU_U, PNU_U])
    y = torch.tensor([1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0])
    age = torch.tensor([40.0, 35.0, 50.0, 31.0, 2.0, 5.0])
    logits = torch.tensor([0.3, -0.2, 0.1, -0.5, 0.7, -1.0])
    return logits, y, pnu, age


def test_mask_loss_ignores_u_rows():
    logits, y, pnu, _ = _toy()
    base = MaturityMaskLoss()(logits, y, pnu)
    l2 = logits.clone()
    l2[pnu == PNU_U] += 5.0                                  # change ONLY the U logits
    assert torch.equal(base, MaturityMaskLoss()(l2, y, pnu))
    m = pnu != PNU_U
    assert torch.allclose(base, F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(logits[m], y[m].float()))


def test_mask_loss_gradient_is_zero_on_u_rows():
    logits, y, pnu, _ = _toy()
    logits.requires_grad_(True)
    MaturityMaskLoss()(logits, y, pnu).backward()
    assert (logits.grad[pnu == PNU_U] == 0).all()
    assert (logits.grad[pnu != PNU_U] != 0).all()


def test_naive_bce_counts_u_as_negative_and_respects_view():
    logits, y, pnu, _ = _toy()
    assert torch.allclose(NaiveBCELoss()(logits, y, pnu),
                          F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(logits, y.float()))
    view = torch.tensor([True] * 5 + [False])
    assert torch.allclose(NaiveBCELoss()(logits, y, pnu, in_train_view=view),
                          F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(logits[:5], y[:5].float()))


def test_ipw_weights_and_normalisation():
    logits, y, pnu, age = _toy()
    lam = 0.1
    loss = DelayIPWLoss(lam=lam)(logits, y, pnu, age_days=age)
    w = torch.where(pnu == PNU_P, torch.ones(6), 1 - torch.exp(-lam * age))
    bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(logits, y.float(), reduction="none")
    assert torch.allclose(loss, (w * bce).sum() / w.sum())
    assert w[4] < w[2]                                       # young U row weighs less than old N row


def test_nndf_matches_formula():
    logits, y, pnu, _ = _toy()
    pi = 0.2
    loss = TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=pi)(logits, y, pnu)
    P, U = logits[pnu == PNU_P], logits[pnu == PNU_U]
    pos = pi * F.softplus(-P).mean()
    neg = F.softplus(U).mean() - pi * F.softplus(P).mean()
    assert torch.allclose(loss, pos + torch.clamp(neg, min=0))


def test_nndf_clamps_negative_risk_and_stops_u_gradient():
    z = torch.tensor([5.0, 5.0, -5.0, -5.0])
    y = torch.tensor([1, 1, 0, 0])
    pnu = torch.tensor([PNU_P, PNU_P, PNU_U, PNU_U])
    r = TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.5).risks(z, y, pnu)
    assert r["neg_risk_raw"] < 0 and r["neg_risk"] == 0
    z.requires_grad_(True)
    TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.5)(z, y, pnu).backward()
    assert (z.grad[2:] == 0).all()                           # U rows get no gradient once clamped


def test_nndf_without_positives_is_zero_but_differentiable():
    z = torch.tensor([0.1, 0.2], requires_grad=True)
    out = TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.1)(z, torch.zeros(2), torch.tensor([PNU_U, PNU_U]))
    assert out.item() == 0.0
    out.backward()                                           # must not raise

================================================================================

FILE: tests\test_losses_canary.py

================================================================================

"""Round-5 tests: loss gradient/output invariants, model label-invariance, forward -> losses smoke.

Self-contained (toy batches); needs torch + torch_geometric, no pyg-lib.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import pytest

torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
pytest.importorskip("torch_geometric")
import torch.nn.functional as F  # noqa: E402
from torch_geometric.data import HeteroData  # noqa: E402

from src.graph.builder import PNU_CODES, TXN  # noqa: E402
from src.losses import (  # noqa: E402
    DelayIPWLoss, MaturityMaskLoss, NaiveBCELoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss, compute_age_days,
)
from src.losses.canary import (  # noqa: E402
    assert_loss_invariant_to_status, assert_nonzero_grad_on_status, assert_zero_grad_on_status,
    logits_grad, model_leakage_hook,
)
from src.models.classifier import FraudClassifier  # noqa: E402
from src.models.temporal_gnn import ModularTemporalGNN  # noqa: E402

P, N, U = PNU_CODES["P"], PNU_CODES["N"], PNU_CODES["U"]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Rows with known statuses
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def make_rows(n_p: int = 6, n_n: int = 20, n_u: int = 14, seed: int = 0):
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    pnu = torch.cat([torch.full((n_p,), P), torch.full((n_n,), N), torch.full((n_u,), U)]).long()
    y_obs = (pnu == P).long()
    age = torch.cat([torch.linspace(0.5, 9.0, n_p), torch.linspace(8.0, 60.0, n_n), torch.linspace(0.2, 6.8, n_u)])
    preds = torch.randn(pnu.numel(), generator=g)
    return preds, y_obs, pnu, age


# ---- naive_bce --------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_naive_bce_matches_torch_and_gives_every_u_row_a_gradient():
    preds, y, pnu, _ = make_rows()
    loss, grad = logits_grad(NaiveBCELoss(), preds, y, pnu)
    torch.testing.assert_close(loss, F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(preds, y.float()))
    torch.testing.assert_close(grad, (torch.sigmoid(preds) - y.float()) / preds.numel())
    assert_nonzero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, U, "naive_bce")


def test_naive_bce_ignores_rows_outside_the_train_view():
    preds, y, pnu, _ = make_rows()
    view = torch.ones_like(pnu, dtype=torch.bool)
    view[-5:] = False
    _, grad = logits_grad(NaiveBCELoss(), preds, y, pnu, in_train_view=view)
    assert bool((grad[~view] == 0).all()) and bool((grad[view] != 0).all())


# ---- maturity_mask ----------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_maturity_mask_has_zero_u_gradient_and_u_invariant_output():
    preds, y, pnu, _ = make_rows()
    fn = MaturityMaskLoss()
    loss, grad = logits_grad(fn, preds, y, pnu)
    keep = pnu != U
    torch.testing.assert_close(loss, F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(preds[keep], y[keep].float()))
    assert_zero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, U, "maturity_mask")
    assert_nonzero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, P, "maturity_mask")
    assert_nonzero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, N, "maturity_mask")
    assert_loss_invariant_to_status(fn, preds, y, pnu, U)


# ---- tw_nndf ----------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _clamped_rows(prior: float = 0.035):
    preds, y, pnu, age = make_rows()
    preds = torch.where(pnu == P, torch.full_like(preds, 4.0), preds)  # l(P,-1) large -> raw < 0
    preds = torch.where(pnu == U, torch.full_like(preds, -8.0), preds)  # l(U,-1) ~ 0
    return preds, y, pnu, age


def test_tw_nndf_u_rows_get_zero_gradient_when_the_clamp_is_active():
    fn = TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.035)
    preds, y, pnu, _ = _clamped_rows()
    loss, grad = logits_grad(fn, preds, y, pnu)
    assert float(fn.last_risks["neg_risk_raw"]) < 0, "test setup is vacuous: the clamp is not active"
    assert float(fn.last_risks["clamped"]) == 1.0
    assert_zero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, U, "tw_nndf")
    assert_zero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, N, "tw_nndf")  # N is not part of U by default
    assert_nonzero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, P, "tw_nndf")
    assert_loss_invariant_to_status(fn, preds, y, pnu, U, scale=1.0)  # small noise: clamp stays active


def test_tw_nndf_u_rows_get_gradient_when_the_clamp_is_inactive():
    fn = TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.035)
    preds, y, pnu, _ = make_rows()
    preds = torch.where(pnu == P, torch.full_like(preds, -6.0), preds)
    preds = torch.where(pnu == U, torch.full_like(preds, 2.0), preds)
    _, grad = logits_grad(fn, preds, y, pnu)
    assert float(fn.last_risks["neg_risk_raw"]) > 0, "test setup is vacuous: the clamp is active"
    assert_nonzero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, U, "tw_nndf")


def test_tw_nndf_matches_the_closed_form():
    preds, y, pnu, _ = make_rows(seed=3)
    pi = 0.1
    p, u = preds[pnu == P], preds[pnu == U]
    pos = pi * F.softplus(-p).mean()
    neg = F.softplus(u).mean() - pi * F.softplus(p).mean()
    expected = pos + torch.clamp(neg, min=0.0)
    torch.testing.assert_close(TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=pi)(preds, y, pnu), expected)


def test_tw_nndf_empty_p_or_u_gives_connected_zero():
    fn = TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.05)
    preds = torch.randn(8, requires_grad=True)
    only_n = torch.full((8,), N).long()
    loss = fn(preds, torch.zeros(8).long(), only_n)
    assert float(loss) == 0.0 and fn.last_risks is None
    loss.backward()  # must not raise


# ---- delay_ipw --------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_delay_ipw_weights_follow_the_formula_and_young_rows_get_smaller_gradient():
    lam = 0.3
    fn = DelayIPWLoss(lam=lam)
    preds, y, pnu, age = make_rows()
    w = fn.weights(pnu, age)
    expected = torch.where(pnu == P, torch.ones_like(age), 1.0 - torch.exp(-lam * age))
    torch.testing.assert_close(w, expected)

    flat = torch.zeros_like(preds)  # equal logits: gradient is proportional to the weight
    _, grad = logits_grad(fn, flat, y, pnu, age_days=age)
    u_rows = pnu == U
    gu, au = grad[u_rows].abs(), age[u_rows]
    assert bool((gu > 0).all()) and bool((gu[au.argsort()].diff() >= 0).all())  # older -> larger


def test_delay_ipw_reduces_to_naive_bce_for_a_huge_rate():
    preds, y, pnu, age = make_rows()
    ipw = DelayIPWLoss(lam=1e6)(preds, y, pnu, age_days=age)  # every w == 1
    torch.testing.assert_close(ipw, NaiveBCELoss()(preds, y, pnu))


def test_delay_ipw_mature_only_scope_masks_u():
    preds, y, pnu, age = make_rows()
    fn = DelayIPWLoss(lam=0.3, negative_scope="mature_only")
    _, grad = logits_grad(fn, preds, y, pnu, age_days=age)
    assert_zero_grad_on_status(grad, pnu, U, "delay_ipw[mature_only]")


# ---- shared behaviour -------------------------------------------------------- #
@pytest.mark.parametrize("make", [NaiveBCELoss, MaturityMaskLoss])
def test_empty_selection_returns_connected_zero(make):
    preds = torch.randn(6, requires_grad=True)
    pnu = torch.full((6,), U).long()
    loss = make()(preds, torch.zeros(6).long(), pnu, in_train_view=torch.zeros(6, dtype=torch.bool))
    assert float(loss) == 0.0
    loss.backward()


def test_all_four_losses_equal_ln2_at_zero_logits():
    preds, y, pnu, age = make_rows()
    z = torch.zeros_like(preds)
    ln2 = math.log(2.0)
    vals = [
        NaiveBCELoss()(z, y, pnu),
        MaturityMaskLoss()(z, y, pnu),
        DelayIPWLoss(lam=0.3)(z, y, pnu, age_days=age),
        TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.035)(z, y, pnu),  # pi*ln2 + (1-pi)*ln2
    ]
    for v in vals:
        assert float(v) == pytest.approx(ln2, abs=1e-5)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Toy disjoint batch (same layout as NeighborLoader output)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
VOCAB = {"card": 40, "device": 25}


def make_toy_batch(bs: int = 12, hist: int = 3, in_dim: int = 16, seed: int = 0) -> HeteroData:
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    n_txn = bs * (1 + hist)
    owner = torch.cat([torch.arange(bs), torch.arange(bs).repeat_interleave(hist)])
    seed_time = 10_000_000 + torch.arange(bs, dtype=torch.long) * 7_200
    j = torch.cat([torch.zeros(bs, dtype=torch.long), torch.arange(hist).repeat(bs)])
    pos = torch.arange(n_txn)
    time = torch.where(pos < bs, seed_time[owner], seed_time[owner] - (hist - j + 1) * 86_400)

    pnu = torch.full((n_txn,), N).long()
    pnu[:2] = P
    pnu[7:bs] = U  # seeds: 2 P, 5 N, rest U (bs >= 8)
    batch = HeteroData()
    t = batch[TXN]
    t.x = torch.randn(n_txn, in_dim, generator=g)
    t.time = time
    t.batch = owner
    t.batch_size = bs
    t.pnu = pnu
    t.y_obs = (pnu == P).long()
    t.y_true = t.y_obs.clone()
    t.in_train_view = torch.ones(n_txn, dtype=torch.bool)

    hist_idx = torch.arange(bs, n_txn)
    for e, size in VOCAB.items():
        ent = batch[e]
        ent.node_id = torch.randint(0, size, (bs,), generator=g)
        ent.batch = torch.arange(bs)
        ent.time = seed_time - 5 * 86_400
        rev, fwd = (e, f"rev_uses_{e}", TXN), (TXN, f"uses_{e}", e)
        batch[rev].edge_index = torch.stack([torch.arange(bs), torch.arange(bs)])
        batch[rev].timestamp = seed_time.clone()
        batch[fwd].edge_index = torch.stack([hist_idx, owner[hist_idx]])
        batch[fwd].timestamp = time[hist_idx]
    return batch


def make_model(conv: str, use_temporal: bool = True) -> FraudClassifier:
    backbone = ModularTemporalGNN(
        in_dim=16, entity_vocab_sizes=VOCAB, hidden_dim=32, num_layers=2, dropout=0.0,
        entity_embedding_dim=8, conv_type=conv, gat_heads=4, max_history=2, use_temporal=use_temporal,
    )
    return FraudClassifier(backbone, dropout=0.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("use_temporal", [True, False])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("conv", ["sage", "gat"])
def test_model_output_shape_and_backward(conv, use_temporal):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    batch, model = make_toy_batch(), make_model(conv, use_temporal)
    logits = model(batch)
    assert logits.shape == (12,) and bool(torch.isfinite(logits).all())
    logits.sum().backward()
    assert model.backbone.ent_emb["card"].weight.grad is not None
    if use_temporal:
        assert float(model.backbone.gru.weight_ih_l0.grad.abs().sum()) > 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("conv", ["sage", "gat"])
def test_model_output_does_not_depend_on_label_fields(conv):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    model_leakage_hook(make_model(conv), make_toy_batch())  # raises on any label dependence


def test_gat_requires_divisible_hidden_dim():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ModularTemporalGNN(in_dim=4, entity_vocab_sizes=VOCAB, hidden_dim=30, conv_type="gat", gat_heads=4)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("conv", ["sage", "gat"])
def test_forward_then_four_losses_smoke(conv):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    batch, model = make_toy_batch(), make_model(conv)
    st, bs = batch[TXN], 12
    age = compute_age_days(st.time[:bs], float(st.time[:bs].max()) + 30 * 86_400)
    losses = {
        "naive_bce": NaiveBCELoss(),
        "maturity_mask": MaturityMaskLoss(),
        "delay_ipw": DelayIPWLoss(lam=0.2),
        "tw_nndf": TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.05),
    }
    for name, fn in losses.items():
        model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        loss = fn(model(batch), st.y_obs[:bs], st.pnu[:bs], age_days=age, in_train_view=st.in_train_view[:bs])
        assert loss.dim() == 0 and bool(torch.isfinite(loss)), name
        assert 0.1 < float(loss) < 3.0, f"{name}: implausible initial loss {float(loss)}"  # ~ ln 2 at init
        loss.backward()

================================================================================

FILE: tests\test_lp_features_canary.py

================================================================================

"""Canary tests for the ATLAS-style lag-safe LP features (round 5b).

Each leak test has two halves: the REAL implementation must be invariant, and a deliberately LEAKY
variant must be caught by the same check (a canary that cannot fail proves nothing).
Needs torch + torch_geometric; the real-sampler test also needs pyg-lib and skips itself without it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
pytest.importorskip("torch_geometric")
from torch_geometric.data import HeteroData  # noqa: E402

from src.data.loader import SECONDS_PER_DAY  # noqa: E402
from src.features.lp_features import (  # noqa: E402
    LP_FEATURE_NAMES, N_LP_FEATURES, LPFeatureTransform, compute_lp_features, lp_transform_from_config,
)
from src.graph.builder import TXN  # noqa: E402
from src.graph.sampler import ComposeTransforms, DropAttributes, build_neighbor_loader, has_pyg_lib  # noqa: E402

D = float(SECONDS_PER_DAY)
W = 10.0  # maturity window in days
T_CUT = int(110 * D)  # a "label cut" later than every train-like seed time (snapshot-leak variant)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Toy data with a hand-computed answer
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def toy_arrays() -> dict:
    """4 seeds (rows 0..3), then history nodes. Times in DAYS (converted to seconds below).

    seed row : t_seed  own y  |  history (t_j, det_j, y_j)
    0        : 100     1      |  a (60, 62, 1)  b (95, 97, 1)  c (98, 105, 1)  d (50, 52, 0)
                              |  e (97, 99, 0)  f (70, 120, 1) g (99, 99.5, 0)
    1        : 50      0      |  h (20, 25, 0)  i (45, 48, 1)  j (49, 51, 1)
    2        : 30      1      |  k (29, 31, 1)
    3        : 20      1      |  (no history)
    """
    seeds_t = [100, 50, 30, 20]
    seeds_y = [1, 0, 1, 1]
    hist = [
        (0, 60, 62, 1), (0, 95, 97, 1), (0, 98, 105, 1), (0, 50, 52, 0), (0, 97, 99, 0), (0, 70, 120, 1),
        (0, 99, 99.5, 0),
        (1, 20, 25, 0), (1, 45, 48, 1), (1, 49, 51, 1),
        (2, 29, 31, 1),
    ]
    time = torch.tensor([round(t * D) for t in seeds_t] + [round(t * D) for _, t, _, _ in hist], dtype=torch.long)
    det = torch.tensor([t * D + D for t in seeds_t] + [d * D for _, _, d, _ in hist], dtype=torch.float64)
    y = torch.tensor(seeds_y + [yy for *_, yy in hist], dtype=torch.long)
    sub = torch.tensor(list(range(4)) + [r for r, *_ in hist], dtype=torch.long)
    return dict(time=time, detection_time=det, y_true=y, subgraph=sub, batch_size=4,
                maturity_window_days=W, node_id=torch.arange(time.numel()))


def to_batch(arrs: dict, x_dim: int = 5) -> HeteroData:
    n = arrs["time"].numel()
    b = HeteroData()
    st = b[TXN]
    st.x = torch.arange(n * x_dim, dtype=torch.float32).view(n, x_dim) / 10.0
    st.time = arrs["time"].clone()
    st.detection_time = arrs["detection_time"].clone()
    st.y_true = arrs["y_true"].clone()
    st.batch = arrs["subgraph"].clone()
    st.batch_size = arrs["batch_size"]
    st.n_id = arrs["node_id"].clone()
    return b


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Larger random batch for the invariance tests
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def random_batch(bs: int = 60, seed: int = 0, p_fraud: float = 0.3) -> dict:
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    t_seed = ((60.0 + 40.0 * torch.rand(bs, generator=g, dtype=torch.float64)) * D).long()
    n_hist = torch.randint(0, 13, (bs,), generator=g)
    owner = torch.repeat_interleave(torch.arange(bs), n_hist)
    h = owner.numel()
    age = (torch.rand(h, generator=g, dtype=torch.float64) * 70.0 * D).long().clamp(min=1)
    time = torch.cat([t_seed, t_seed[owner] - age])
    delay = (1.0 / 24 + (30.0 - 1.0 / 24) * torch.rand(bs + h, generator=g, dtype=torch.float64)) * D
    det = time.to(torch.float64) + delay
    y = (torch.rand(bs + h, generator=g) < p_fraud).long()
    return dict(time=time, detection_time=det, y_true=y, subgraph=torch.cat([torch.arange(bs), owner]),
                batch_size=bs, maturity_window_days=W)


def hist_masks(kw: dict) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    """(unreported, reportable) masks over ALL nodes: history nodes with detection_time > / <= t_seed."""
    bs = kw["batch_size"]
    row = kw["subgraph"][bs:]
    t_seed = kw["time"][:bs][row].to(torch.float64)
    late = kw["detection_time"][bs:] > t_seed
    pad = torch.zeros(bs, dtype=torch.bool)
    return torch.cat([pad, late]), torch.cat([pad, ~late])


def output_changes(fn, kw: dict, mask: torch.Tensor, how: str = "flip", seed: int = 0, **extra) -> bool:
    """Alter y_true on ``mask`` (flip or shuffle) and report whether ``fn``'s output changed."""
    base = fn(**kw, **extra)
    y = kw["y_true"].clone()
    idx = mask.nonzero().view(-1)
    assert idx.numel() > 0, "vacuous: empty mask"
    if how == "flip":
        y[idx] = 1 - y[idx]
    else:
        g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
        y[idx] = kw["y_true"][idx[torch.randperm(idx.numel(), generator=g)]]
    assert not torch.equal(y, kw["y_true"]), "vacuous: the labels did not change"
    return not torch.equal(fn(**{**kw, "y_true": y}, **extra), base)


# --- deliberately leaky variants (negative controls) ---------------------------- #
def leaky_ignore_gate(**kw) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Reads the label of every earlier neighbour, reported or not (the classic future leak)."""
    return compute_lp_features(**{**kw, "detection_time": torch.full_like(kw["detection_time"], -1.0)})


def leaky_snapshot_y_obs(*, T_cut: int, **kw) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Uses y_obs, a snapshot at T_cut, instead of the labels known at t_seed."""
    y_obs = ((kw["y_true"] == 1) & (kw["detection_time"] <= T_cut)).long()
    return compute_lp_features(**{**kw, "y_true": y_obs, "detection_time": torch.full_like(kw["detection_time"], -1.0)})


def leaky_self_label(**kw) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Adds the seed's own label as if it were a labelled fraud neighbour."""
    out = compute_lp_features(**kw).clone()
    own = kw["y_true"][: kw["batch_size"]].to(torch.float32)
    out[:, 0] += own
    out[:, 1] += own
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Test 1: labels of neighbours reported AFTER t_seed must not be read
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_1_real_impl_ignores_labels_of_neighbours_not_yet_reported():
    kw = random_batch()
    late, reportable = hist_masks(kw)
    assert int(late.sum()) > 50 and int(reportable.sum()) > 50, "vacuous batch"
    assert not output_changes(compute_lp_features, kw, late, how="flip")
    # not trivially label-blind: flipping labels that WERE reported does change the features
    assert output_changes(compute_lp_features, kw, reportable, how="flip")


def test_1_injected_leaks_are_caught():
    kw = random_batch()
    late, _ = hist_masks(kw)
    assert output_changes(leaky_ignore_gate, kw, late, how="flip"), "gate removed: leak not detected"
    assert output_changes(leaky_snapshot_y_obs, kw, late, how="flip", T_cut=T_CUT), (
        "y_obs snapshot (t_train) leaks labels that arrive after t_seed: leak not detected"
    )


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Test 2: the seed's own label is never read
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_2_real_impl_ignores_the_seeds_own_label():
    kw = random_batch()
    seeds = torch.arange(kw["time"].numel()) < kw["batch_size"]
    assert not output_changes(compute_lp_features, kw, seeds, how="flip")


def test_2_injected_self_label_is_caught():
    kw = random_batch()
    seeds = torch.arange(kw["time"].numel()) < kw["batch_size"]
    assert output_changes(leaky_self_label, kw, seeds, how="flip")


def test_2_equal_time_and_same_id_neighbours_are_ignored():
    t = round(50 * D)
    base = dict(
        detection_time=torch.tensor([t + 3600.0, float(t - 4 * D), float(t - 4 * D)], dtype=torch.float64),
        y_true=torch.tensor([0, 1, 1]), subgraph=torch.tensor([0, 0, 0]), batch_size=1,
        maturity_window_days=W,
    )
    # same time as the seed -> not strictly earlier -> ignored even though its report "arrived"
    out = compute_lp_features(time=torch.tensor([t, t, t]), node_id=torch.tensor([7, 8, 9]), **base)
    assert out.tolist() == [[0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]]
    # earlier nodes: the one carrying the SEED'S OWN id (7) is dropped, the other (8) counts
    time = torch.tensor([t, t - round(5 * D), t - round(5 * D)])
    with_ids = compute_lp_features(time=time, node_id=torch.tensor([7, 7, 8]), **base)
    assert with_ids.tolist() == [[1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0]]
    without_ids = compute_lp_features(time=time, **base)  # no id guard: both count (shows the guard works)
    assert without_ids.tolist() == [[2.0, 2.0, 1.0, 1.0]]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Test 3: shuffling the labels of not-yet-reported neighbours changes nothing
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", [0, 1, 2])
def test_3_shuffle_of_unreported_neighbour_labels_is_invariant(seed):
    kw = random_batch(seed=seed)
    late, _ = hist_masks(kw)
    assert not output_changes(compute_lp_features, kw, late, how="shuffle", seed=seed)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("rule", ["maturity_window", "detection_time"])
def test_3_pipeline_level_invariance_and_labels_survive_until_dropped(rule):
    kw = random_batch(seed=5)
    late, _ = hist_masks(kw)
    lp = LPFeatureTransform(W, negative_rule=rule)

    def run(y):
        arrs = {**kw, "y_true": y, "node_id": torch.arange(kw["time"].numel())}
        return ComposeTransforms([lp, DropAttributes(("y_true",))])(to_batch(arrs))

    y2 = kw["y_true"].clone()
    idx = late.nonzero().view(-1)
    y2[idx] = kw["y_true"][idx[torch.randperm(idx.numel(), generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(1))]]
    assert not torch.equal(y2, kw["y_true"])
    a, b = run(kw["y_true"]), run(y2)
    assert torch.equal(a[TXN].x, b[TXN].x)
    assert "y_true" not in a[TXN]  # the transform ran first, then the labels were dropped


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Test 4: hand-computed features on a small graph
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_4_hand_computed_default_rule():
    """Seed 0 (t=100): P = {a, b}; N = {d, f} (f is a fraud reported after t_seed -> it is only
    'mature', counted as a negative: label noise, not a leak); U = {c, e, g}.
    => n_labeled=4, n_fraud=2, rate=0.5, has_fraud=1.
    Seed 1 (t=50): P={i}; N={h}; U={j (reported at 51)} -> 2, 1, 0.5, 1.
    Seed 2 (t=30): k is reported at 31 > 30 -> unknown -> 0, 0, 0, 0.  Seed 3: no neighbour -> 0, 0, 0, 0."""
    out = compute_lp_features(**toy_arrays())
    expected = torch.tensor([[4, 2, 0.5, 1], [2, 1, 0.5, 1], [0, 0, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0, 0]], dtype=torch.float32)
    torch.testing.assert_close(out, expected)


def test_4_hand_computed_detection_time_rule():
    """Oracle-like reading: a non-reported neighbour with detection_time <= t_seed also counts as negative.
    Seed 0: P={a,b}; N={d,e,g} (f and c are reported after t_seed -> unknown) -> 5, 2, 0.4, 1."""
    out = compute_lp_features(**toy_arrays(), negative_rule="detection_time")
    expected = torch.tensor([[5, 2, 0.4, 1], [2, 1, 0.5, 1], [0, 0, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0, 0]], dtype=torch.float32)
    torch.testing.assert_close(out, expected)


def test_4_transform_appends_columns_and_zero_fills_history():
    arrs = toy_arrays()
    batch = to_batch(arrs, x_dim=5)
    x0 = batch[TXN].x.clone()
    out = LPFeatureTransform(W, log1p_counts=False)(batch)
    x = out[TXN].x
    assert x.shape == (x0.size(0), 5 + N_LP_FEATURES) and int(out[TXN].lp_dim) == N_LP_FEATURES
    assert torch.equal(x[:, :5], x0)  # original columns untouched
    torch.testing.assert_close(x[:4, 5:], compute_lp_features(**arrs))
    assert bool((x[4:, 5:] == 0).all())  # history rows: zeros

    logged = LPFeatureTransform(W, log1p_counts=True)(to_batch(arrs))[TXN].x[:4, 5:]
    raw = compute_lp_features(**arrs)
    torch.testing.assert_close(logged[:, :2], torch.log1p(raw[:, :2]))
    torch.testing.assert_close(logged[:, 2:], raw[:, 2:])  # rate and flag are not transformed


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Test 5: no labelled neighbour -> zeros, never NaN
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_5_no_labelled_neighbour_gives_zeros_not_nan():
    out = compute_lp_features(**toy_arrays())
    for r in (2, 3):  # seed 2: only an unreported neighbour; seed 3: no neighbour
        assert out[r].tolist() == [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
    assert bool(torch.isfinite(out).all())


def test_5_batch_without_any_history_and_all_unknown_batch():
    t = torch.tensor([round(40 * D), round(41 * D)])
    only_seeds = compute_lp_features(time=t, detection_time=t.double() + D, y_true=torch.tensor([1, 1]),
                                     subgraph=torch.tensor([0, 1]), batch_size=2, maturity_window_days=W)
    assert only_seeds.shape == (2, 4) and bool((only_seeds == 0).all())

    kw = random_batch()
    kw["maturity_window_days"] = 1e6  # nothing is ever mature
    kw["detection_time"] = kw["time"].double() + 1e9  # nothing is ever reported
    out = compute_lp_features(**kw)
    assert bool((out == 0).all()) and bool(torch.isfinite(out).all())


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Transform guards, config, sampler wiring
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def test_transform_must_run_before_y_true_is_dropped_and_only_once():
    lp = LPFeatureTransform(W)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        ComposeTransforms([DropAttributes(("y_true",)), lp])(to_batch(toy_arrays()))
    out = lp(to_batch(toy_arrays()))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        lp(out)


def test_config_default_is_off_and_window_comes_from_meta():
    assert lp_transform_from_config(None) is None
    assert lp_transform_from_config({"lp_features": {"enabled": False}}) is None
    meta = {"config": {"delay": {"maturity_window_days": 30}}}
    lp = lp_transform_from_config({"lp_features": {"enabled": True}}, meta)
    assert lp is not None and lp.maturity_window_days == 30.0 and lp.n_features == 4
    assert lp.names == LP_FEATURE_NAMES
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        lp_transform_from_config({"lp_features": {"enabled": True}})  # no window anywhere
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        lp_transform_from_config({"lp_features": {"enabled": True, "negative_rule": "bogus"}}, meta)


def tiny_graph(n: int = 40, n_ent: int = 8, n_seeds: int = 15, seed: int = 0) -> HeteroData:
    """Phase-graph-like toy (node `time`, edge `timestamp`, one entity type) for the sampler tests."""
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    t = (torch.arange(n) * 6 * 3600 + 86_400).long()
    delay = (3600.0 + 29.0 * D * torch.rand(n, generator=g, dtype=torch.float64))
    ent = torch.randint(0, n_ent, (n,), generator=g)
    ent[:n_ent] = torch.arange(n_ent)  # every entity id occurs
    graph = HeteroData()
    st = graph[TXN]
    st.x = torch.randn(n, 3, generator=g)
    st.time = t
    st.detection_time = t.double() + delay
    st.y_true = (torch.rand(n, generator=g) < 0.3).long()
    st.seed_mask = torch.zeros(n, dtype=torch.bool)
    st.seed_mask[-n_seeds:] = True
    first = torch.stack([t[ent == e].min() for e in range(n_ent)])
    graph["card"].node_id = torch.arange(n_ent)
    graph["card"].time = first
    graph["card"].num_nodes = n_ent
    src = torch.arange(n)
    graph[(TXN, "uses_card", "card")].edge_index = torch.stack([src, ent])
    graph[(TXN, "uses_card", "card")].timestamp = t
    graph[("card", "rev_uses_card", TXN)].edge_index = torch.stack([ent, src])
    graph[("card", "rev_uses_card", TXN)].timestamp = t
    return graph


def test_sampler_composes_lp_before_drop_and_keeps_the_old_behaviour():
    g = tiny_graph()
    lp = LPFeatureTransform(W)
    train = build_neighbor_loader(g, [5, 3], 8, batch_transforms=[lp], check_backend=False)
    assert isinstance(train.transform, ComposeTransforms)
    assert train.transform.steps[0] is lp and isinstance(train.transform.steps[1], DropAttributes)
    ev = build_neighbor_loader(g, [5, 3], 8, batch_transforms=[lp], expose_true_labels=True, check_backend=False)
    assert ev.transform is lp
    plain = build_neighbor_loader(g, [5, 3], 8, check_backend=False)
    assert isinstance(plain.transform, DropAttributes)  # unchanged default
    assert build_neighbor_loader(g, [5, 3], 8, expose_true_labels=True, check_backend=False).transform is None


@pytest.mark.skipif(not has_pyg_lib(), reason="temporal NeighborLoader needs pyg-lib")
def test_real_sampler_batches_match_an_independent_python_computation():
    """End to end: sampler -> LP transform -> DropAttributes. Checks the layout assumptions (seeds first,
    subgraph id per node, history = non-seed transaction nodes) against a pure-python reference."""
    g = tiny_graph()
    loader = build_neighbor_loader(g, [10_000, 10_000], 15, batch_transforms=[LPFeatureTransform(W, log1p_counts=False)])
    batch = next(iter(loader))
    st = batch[TXN]
    bs = int(st.batch_size)
    assert st.x.size(1) == 3 + N_LP_FEATURES and "y_true" not in st

    t, det, y = g[TXN].time, g[TXN].detection_time, g[TXN].y_true
    src, dst = g[(TXN, "uses_card", "card")].edge_index
    for r, s in enumerate(st.n_id[:bs].tolist()):
        ts = int(t[s])
        ents = {int(e) for e in dst[src == s] if int(g["card"].time[e]) <= ts - 1}
        nbrs = {int(j) for j, e in zip(src.tolist(), dst.tolist()) if e in ents and int(t[j]) <= ts - 1}
        n_lab = n_fr = 0
        for j in nbrs:
            reported = int(y[j]) == 1 and float(det[j]) <= ts
            if reported:
                n_lab += 1
                n_fr += 1
            elif ts - int(t[j]) >= W * SECONDS_PER_DAY:
                n_lab += 1
        got = st.x[r, 3:].tolist()
        want = [float(n_lab), float(n_fr), n_fr / max(n_lab, 1), float(n_fr > 0)]
        assert got == pytest.approx(want), f"seed {s}: {got} != {want}"

"""Round-6 tests: metrics with hand-computed values, CI and the Recovery Ratio gate."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pytest

from src.eval import metrics as M

Y = np.array([1, 0, 1, 0, 0])
S = np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.3, 0.1])
AMT = np.array([100.0, 5.0, 50.0, 1.0, 1.0])


def test_average_precision_and_roc_auc_by_hand():
    assert M.average_precision(Y, S) == pytest.approx(0.5 * 1.0 + 0.5 * (2.0 / 3.0))
    assert M.roc_auc(Y, S) == pytest.approx(5.0 / 6.0)


def test_ties_give_chance_level():
    y, s = np.array([1, 0]), np.array([0.5, 0.5])
    assert M.roc_auc(y, s) == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert M.average_precision(y, s) == pytest.approx(0.5)


def test_threshold_and_recall_at_fpr():
    assert M.threshold_at_fpr(Y, S, 0.01) == pytest.approx(0.9)     # budget 0 FP
    assert M.recall_at_fpr(Y, S, 0.01) == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert M.threshold_at_fpr(Y, S, 0.34) == pytest.approx(0.7)     # budget 1 FP of 3
    assert M.recall_at_fpr(Y, S, 0.34) == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert M.threshold_at_fpr(np.array([0, 1]), np.array([0.9, 0.1]), 0.01) == math.inf
    assert M.recall_at_fpr(np.array([0, 1]), np.array([0.9, 0.1]), 0.01) == 0.0


def test_operating_point_and_efl():
    op = M.operating_point(Y, S, AMT, threshold=0.9)
    assert op["missed_fraud_amount"] == 50.0 and op["efl"] == 50.0
    assert op["efl_ratio"] == pytest.approx(50.0 / 150.0)
    op2 = M.operating_point(Y, S, AMT, threshold=0.8, review_cost=2.0)   # flags rows 0 and 1 -> 1 FP
    assert op2["fp"] == 1.0 and op2["efl"] == pytest.approx(50.0 + 2.0)
    assert M.operating_point(Y, S, AMT, threshold=math.inf)["efl_ratio"] == pytest.approx(1.0)  # flag nothing


def test_summarize_scores_matches_the_single_metrics():
    m = M.summarize_scores(Y, S, fpr=0.34)
    assert m["pr_auc"] == pytest.approx(M.average_precision(Y, S))
    assert m["roc_auc"] == pytest.approx(M.roc_auc(Y, S))
    assert m["recall_at_fpr"] == pytest.approx(1.0) and m["prevalence"] == pytest.approx(0.4)


def test_invalid_inputs_raise():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        M.roc_auc(np.ones(4), np.arange(4.0))                  # one class only
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        M.roc_auc(np.array([0, 1]), np.array([np.nan, 1.0]))   # NaN score


def test_ci95_three_seeds():
    mean, lo, hi = M.ci95([0.1, 0.2, 0.3])
    assert mean == pytest.approx(0.2)
    assert hi - mean == pytest.approx(4.303 * 0.1 / math.sqrt(3))
    assert lo == pytest.approx(mean - (hi - mean))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        M.ci95([0.5])


def test_counts_as_improvement_needs_the_favourable_side():
    naive = [0.29, 0.30, 0.31]                                  # CI ~ 0.30 +- 0.0248
    assert M.counts_as_improvement([0.34, 0.35, 0.36], naive) is True
    assert M.counts_as_improvement([0.31, 0.31, 0.31], naive) is False    # inside the CI
    assert M.counts_as_improvement([0.20, 0.21, 0.22], naive) is False    # outside but WORSE
    assert M.counts_as_improvement([0.20, 0.21, 0.22], naive, higher_is_better=False) is True


def test_recovery_ratio_is_reported_only_if_the_oracle_gap_exceeds_the_naive_ci():
    naive, oracle, method = [0.29, 0.30, 0.31], [0.39, 0.40, 0.41], [0.34, 0.35, 0.36]
    r = M.recovery_ratio(method, naive, oracle)
    assert r.reported and r.value == pytest.approx(0.5)
    weak_oracle = [0.30, 0.31, 0.32]                                      # mean 0.31 is inside A0's CI
    r2 = M.recovery_ratio(method, naive, weak_oracle)
    assert (not r2.reported) and r2.value is None and "CI" in r2.reason


def test_recovery_ratio_for_a_lower_is_better_score():
    naive, oracle, method = [99.0, 100.0, 101.0], [89.0, 90.0, 91.0], [94.0, 95.0, 96.0]
    r = M.recovery_ratio(method, naive, oracle, higher_is_better=False)
    assert r.reported and r.value == pytest.approx((100 - 95) / (100 - 90))

"""Round-6 tests: schedule helpers, config parsing and a tiny end-to-end fit on toy batches (CPU, no pyg-lib)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
pytest.importorskip("torch_geometric")
from torch_geometric.data import HeteroData  # noqa: E402

from src.graph.builder import PNU_CODES, TXN  # noqa: E402
from src.losses import DelayIPWLoss, MaturityMaskLoss, NaiveBCELoss, TimeWindowNNDFLoss  # noqa: E402
from src.models.classifier import FraudClassifier  # noqa: E402
from src.models.temporal_gnn import ModularTemporalGNN  # noqa: E402
from src.train.trainer import TrainConfig, Trainer, compute_batch_loss  # noqa: E402
from src.train.utils import EarlyStopper, warmup_factor  # noqa: E402

P, N, U = PNU_CODES["P"], PNU_CODES["N"], PNU_CODES["U"]
VOCAB = {"card": 40, "device": 25}
BS, HIST, IN_DIM = 16, 3, 16


def test_warmup_factor():
    assert warmup_factor(0, 4) == pytest.approx(0.25)
    assert warmup_factor(3, 4) == 1.0 and warmup_factor(99, 4) == 1.0
    assert warmup_factor(0, 0) == 1.0


def test_early_stopper_counts_patience_only_after_warmup():
    s = EarlyStopper(patience=2, start_epoch=1)
    assert [s.update(v, e) for e, v in enumerate([0.5, 0.4, 0.4], 1)] == [True, False, False]
    assert s.should_stop and s.best_epoch == 1

    w = EarlyStopper(patience=2, start_epoch=3)            # epochs 1-2 are warm-up
    for e, v in enumerate([0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2], 1):
        w.update(v, e)
        if e < 4:
            assert not w.should_stop
    assert w.should_stop and w.bad_epochs == 2

    nan = EarlyStopper(patience=1)
    assert nan.update(float("nan"), 1) is False and nan.best is None


def test_train_config_from_cfg():
    cfg = {"seed": 7, "train": {"epochs": 10, "warmup_epochs": 2, "patience": 3},
           "eval": {"fpr": 0.02}, "loss": {"name": "naive_bce", "delay_ipw": {"lam": 0.3}}}
    t = TrainConfig.from_cfg(cfg, loss="delay_ipw")
    assert (t.loss, t.seed, t.epochs, t.warmup_epochs, t.fpr) == ("delay_ipw", 7, 10, 2, 0.02)
    assert t.loss_params == {"lam": 0.3}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        TrainConfig(epochs=2, warmup_epochs=2)               # warm-up must leave at least one epoch


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Toy batches with the NeighborLoader layout
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def toy_batch(seed: int, with_y_true: bool = True) -> HeteroData:
    g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    n = BS * (1 + HIST)
    owner = torch.cat([torch.arange(BS), torch.arange(BS).repeat_interleave(HIST)])
    seed_time = 10_000_000 + torch.arange(BS, dtype=torch.long) * 7_200
    j = torch.cat([torch.zeros(BS, dtype=torch.long), torch.arange(HIST).repeat(BS)])
    time = torch.where(torch.arange(n) < BS, seed_time[owner], seed_time[owner] - (HIST - j + 1) * 86_400)

    pnu = torch.full((n,), N).long()
    pnu[:2] = P
    pnu[9:BS] = U
    batch = HeteroData()
    t = batch[TXN]
    t.x = torch.randn(n, IN_DIM, generator=g)
    t.time, t.batch, t.batch_size = time, owner, BS
    t.pnu = pnu
    t.y_obs = (pnu == P).long()
    t.in_train_view = torch.ones(n, dtype=torch.bool)
    t.amount = torch.rand(n, generator=g) * 100 + 1
    if with_y_true:
        t.y_true = t.y_obs.clone()
        t.y_true[[14, 15]] = 1                               # two frauds hidden in U
    hist_idx = torch.arange(BS, n)
    for e, size in VOCAB.items():
        ent = batch[e]
        ent.node_id = torch.randint(0, size, (BS,), generator=g)
        ent.batch = torch.arange(BS)
        ent.time = seed_time - 5 * 86_400
        rev, fwd = (e, f"rev_uses_{e}", TXN), (TXN, f"uses_{e}", e)
        batch[rev].edge_index = torch.stack([torch.arange(BS), torch.arange(BS)])
        batch[rev].timestamp = seed_time.clone()
        batch[fwd].edge_index = torch.stack([hist_idx, owner[hist_idx]])
        batch[fwd].timestamp = time[hist_idx]
    return batch


class FakeDS:
    """Minimal stand-in for TemporalGraphDataset: iterable of batches + ``data.label_cut``."""

    def __init__(self, batches: list[HeteroData]) -> None:
        self.batches = batches
        self.data = SimpleNamespace(label_cut=10_000_000.0 + 40 * 86_400.0)

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter(self.batches)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.batches)


def toy_model(use_temporal: bool = True) -> FraudClassifier:
    bb = ModularTemporalGNN(in_dim=IN_DIM, entity_vocab_sizes=VOCAB, hidden_dim=16, num_layers=2, dropout=0.0,
                            entity_embedding_dim=8, max_history=2, use_temporal=use_temporal)
    return FraudClassifier(bb, dropout=0.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("use_temporal", [True, False])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["naive_bce", "maturity_mask", "delay_ipw", "tw_nndf", "oracle"])
def test_fit_runs_for_every_loss_and_the_oracle_switch(name, use_temporal):
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    losses = {"naive_bce": NaiveBCELoss(), "maturity_mask": MaturityMaskLoss(), "delay_ipw": DelayIPWLoss(lam=0.2),
              "tw_nndf": TimeWindowNNDFLoss(prior=0.1), "oracle": NaiveBCELoss()}
    train, val = FakeDS([toy_batch(s) for s in range(3)]), FakeDS([toy_batch(10)])
    cfg = TrainConfig(loss="naive_bce" if name == "oracle" else name, oracle=(name == "oracle"), epochs=2,
                      warmup_epochs=1, patience=2, device="cpu")
    res = Trainer(toy_model(use_temporal), losses[name], train, val, cfg, log=lambda s: None).fit()
    assert res["epochs_run"] == len(res["history"]) == 2
    assert math.isfinite(res["val"]["pr_auc"]) and math.isfinite(res["train_seconds"])
    assert 1 <= res["best_epoch"] <= 2
    if name == "tw_nndf":
        assert "nndf_clamped_rate" in res["history"][0] and "nndf_neg_risk_raw" in res["history"][0]


def test_oracle_needs_y_true_in_the_train_batches():
    batch = toy_batch(0, with_y_true=False)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        compute_batch_loss(toy_model(), NaiveBCELoss(), batch, 1e7, oracle=True)
    loss, _ = compute_batch_loss(toy_model(), NaiveBCELoss(), batch, 1e7, oracle=False)   # y_obs path still works
    assert torch.isfinite(loss)

FILE: tests\test_tw_nndf_ascent.py
